# SPEC-serum2 — Serum 2 additions (Phase 2): implementation contract (delta on SPEC-serum v2)

Binding contract for the Phase-2 engineers (roles in §0.2) and the integrator. It is a **delta**: everything in `SPEC-serum.md` (v2), as amended by `RULINGS.md` and by the Phase-1 code that now exists in the repository, stays in force unless a section here replaces it. Read §0 completely first.

- Review round 2 (critics A: coverage, B: contract/back-compat/performance) is folded in; Appendix C logs every point and its resolution, §0.8 lists the decisions awaiting the lead's sign-off.
- Precedence: **this spec** > `RULINGS.md` > `SPEC-serum.md` > the Phase-1 code (where it deviates in documented ways, the code is the baseline) > `ARCH2.md` / `ARCH.md` > `serum-inventory.md` (feature authority: Part B = this phase; cited "inv B7", "inv C12").
- Where Serum 2's maths is unpublished, the formulas here are **normative for this project** (IMPL). Do not "improve" them silently: tests on both sides of every interface depend on them.
- MUST / SHOULD / MAY carry their usual meaning. "n" is always a normalised destination value ∈ [0,1] (Phase-1 §0.4). Indices and string ids defined here never change once M1a lands; additions append (review round 2 appended idx 524–528, §3.11.2).
- Notation from Phase-1 §0.4 applies. New: **vi** = voice-vector index of a synth destination (§3.11.1); **FX-ctx index** = index into the processor-gain + FX-parameter vector (§4.2); **S1 mode** = `s.global.s1 = 1` (§2.6); **asset** = any sample-like data object (sample, multisample, spectral analysis, impulse response; §9).

---

## 0. Ground rules

### 0.1 Non-negotiables (ARCH2) and where this spec satisfies them

| ARCH2 rule | Normative mechanism |
|---|---|
| 1 Backward compatibility | Versioned state (`s.v = 2`, `fx.v = 2`) with an exact v1 → v2 migration (§2.6); **S1 mode** keeps every behaviour that differs between Serum 1 and Serum 2 on the Phase-1 code path (§6.21); every v1 destination keeps its index, id, law and default (§3.11); Phase-1 FX modules survive as **S1 variants** with their v1 parameter tables and DSP (§3.16); Phase-1 randomness keeps its draw order (second PRNG streams for everything new, §6.2); every new term at its neutral value is skipped, never computed (§0.7); golden tests render through a frozen copy of the Phase-1 code and compare (§15.1, merge gate from M1a). Classic models and non-Serum FX racks: **bit-identical**; v1 Serum states: ≤ 1e-6 absolute per sample (−120 dBFS) |
| 2 Original content only | All factory content is generated by code in this repository (§10): procedural generators and offline renders of this project's own engines (`engine.js` models and the Phase-1 Serum DSP). No Xfer audio, tables, IRs, presets, text or artwork |
| 3 Browser constraints | No deps, no build; all new worklet code is classic IIFE scripts (§0.4) loaded in a fixed order (§0.3); decoding/analysis/STFT/IR preparation in module workers or on the main thread (§9); large buffers transferred, cached per AudioContext, included in `processorOptions` for bounces (§9.6) |
| 4 Performance | Budgets per feature (§14.1, gated in node and measured on iPad with `tests/bench.html`), cheap bypass when features are unused (`CP.use` bits, §4.1), memory caps incl. touch-device budgets (§14.2), quality-reduction hooks driven by the existing CPU safeguard (§14.3), part-keyed table delivery (§4.1, §4.4) |
| 5 File ownership | New classic scripts per DSP area (§0.3); one owner per file during the build (§0.2) |
| 6 Site style, node-testable | Phase-1 §0.2 code rules unchanged; every new module has a node test file (§15) |

Browser modules keep the Phase-1 rule: no DOM/`window`/`navigator`/storage access at module top level.

### 0.3 Classic DSP scripts: globals and load order

Every AudioContext (live and every `OfflineAudioContext`: bounce, resample, preview, render farm) loads exactly this list: scripts 1–11 are requested **concurrently** (`Promise.allSettled` over the `addModule` calls; a rejected one is logged and its feature degrades per the last column), then `engine.js` is loaded last and awaited. The table order is the **injection order** for the main-thread `wk.loadDSP` and the `importScripts` order of the render farm; nothing may depend on evaluation order (lazy references, below).

| # | File | Global | Required for | Failure ⇒ |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `sx-base.js` | `SxBase` | Serum v2 (all), S2-variant FX instances | Serum tracks silent; S2-variant FX instances bypassed (S1-variant instances and every classic-model rack keep working: fx-dsp's S1 code does not use `SxBase`) |
| 2 | `sx-filt2.js` | `SxFilt2` | filter types 96–107 | those types pass audio unfiltered (gain 1) |
| 3 | `sx-warp.js` | `SxWarp` | S2 warp modes (WARPS2 ≥ 1) | S2 warp slots act as Off; S1 legacy warps keep working |
| 4 | `sx-mod.js` | `SxMod` | envelopes, LFOs, matrix (all Serum) | Serum tracks silent |
| 5 | `sx-sample.js` | `SxSample` | Sample + Multisample oscillators | those oscillators silent |
| 6 | `sx-gran.js` | `SxGran` | Granular oscillators | silent |
| 7 | `sx-spec.js` | `SxSpec` | Spectral oscillators | silent |
| 8 | `sx-seq.js` | `SxSeq` | arp, clip player, keyboard processing | notes bypass the sequencer (played directly, keyboard transpose still applied by the engine) |
| 9 | `serum-dsp.js` | `SerumDSP` | Serum | Serum tracks silent |
| 10 | `fx-mods2.js` | `FXMods2` | Bode, Convolve, Utility, S2 reverbs, Overdrive/Soft Sat, splitters | those instances pass audio (bypassed) |
| 11 | `fx-dsp.js` | `FXDSP` | every FX rack | racks bypassed |
| 12 | `engine.js` | (registers `synth-engine`) | everything | — |

- Every script uses the Phase-1 IIFE shape (`const prev = globalThis.X; try { … globalThis.X = Object.freeze({…}) } catch { globalThis.X = null; console.error }`). Stores that must survive re-evaluation (`SxBase.assets`, `SerumDSP.tables`) are taken from `prev`.
- **Lazy cross-script references only.** No script reads another script's global at load time. References are resolved in constructors, `attach`, `apply`, `set`, `setType`, `prealloc` and cached in fields (never looked up per sample). A missing global yields the "Failure ⇒" column, plus one `console.warn` per script per scope.
- Shared math lives in exactly one script: the distortion shapers (FX modes 0–17, used by FX Distortion S2 and the distortion warps) are `SxBase.shape(mode, x, d)` (owner D5, ported verbatim from fx-dsp's Phase-1 shapers + Overdrive/Soft Sat., §8.2); fx-dsp's **S1 variants keep their own Phase-1 copy** (bit-exactness and independence from `SxBase`), and an integration test asserts `SxBase.shape` ≡ the Phase-1 shapers for modes 0–15 bit for bit.
- `wk.loadDSP()` default URL list = files 1–11 (in table order) with `DSP_GLOBALS` extended accordingly; `main.js DSP_URLS` = the same list. No DSP script runs on the main thread for audio purposes (MIDI OUT stays inside the worklet scope, §7.9).
- Environment neutrality, allocation-free hot paths, determinism and denormal rules of Phase-1 §0.2 apply to every new script. New: **no script may hold more than one reference to another script's object in a hot loop**; per-voice objects created by another script (e.g. `SxGran.GranOsc`) are stored in fields of the voice.

### 0.4 Code rules (delta)
- Site style and Phase-1 §0.2 rules unchanged. ES2020 except: `Float32Array`/`Float64Array` methods, `Math.fround`, `DecompressionStream` (feature-detected, main thread only).
- Every pure ES module exports its constants deep-frozen; DSP scripts embed identical copies, generated from the same normative builders (§3.11.2, §3.16), compared by integration tests.
- Determinism: DSP randomness from xorshift32 seeded per processor/voice (Phase-1 rule) — Phase-1 streams keep their draw order and count; every Phase-2 draw uses its own stream (§6.2). Main-thread generators use `mulberry32(fnv1a32(<id>))` from `wt.js`. The classic models' one use of `Math.random` (engine.js Solina `Divide` phases) stays for live playback; every **test harness and the render farm** replace `Math.random` in their vm/worker scope by `mulberry32(fnv1a32(<render id>))`, reseeded before each render, before `engine.js` is evaluated (§10.1, §15.1).
- No `SharedArrayBuffer`, no `Atomics` (the site is not cross-origin isolated). Processors of one AudioContext share one `AudioWorkletGlobalScope`; the only cross-processor channel is the engine's `PROCS` registry (§7.9).
- **Import suffixes**: every relative import of a site module uses the shared `?v=N` suffix (Phase 1: `?v=5`), including the new `laws`, `fx-core`, `seq-core`, `tuning`, `smp`, `smpgen`, `smpstore`, `assets`, `midi`, `appstore` and `sx-ui-*` modules; I bumps N for the release. A module imported with two different specifiers would be instantiated twice (separate caches). `tests/lint.test.mjs` fails on any relative `import`/`import()` without the current suffix (test files map the suffix through their loader).

### 0.6 Decided deviations from Serum 2 (binding)
1. **MTS-ESP** cannot exist in a browser (no shared host memory, no IPC). The GLOBAL page shows the MTS-ESP group disabled with "Not available in the browser". The **Step** pitch mode works with a loaded `.scl` scale instead (§6.3.3).
2. **MIDI OUT** cannot reach a DAW. Off / Clip Player / On select which generated notes are sent to *another track of this project* (§7.9), sample-accurately inside the audio thread with a fixed latency of one render quantum (128 frames), identically live and in bounces.
3. No VST3 Note Expression. Expression comes from MPE over Web MIDI (§6.17).
4. *(Withdrawn in review round 2, A5: every S2 warp is available on every oscillator type, §3.4, §6.6.10. Spectral oscillators realise the phase- and frequency-domain warps on their resynthesised output through a per-stream delay line with a note-constant base delay ≤ 25 ms — IMPL, §6.10.7.)*
5. Audio-rate modulation sources (oscillators, sub, noise, filters as matrix sources) are sampled once per chunk (control rate), as Phase-1's Noise source.
6. FM/PD/AM/RM **from Filter 1/2** read the filter output delayed by one chunk (§6.6.7).
7. Per-route matrix delay (S2S keys DelayOffset/DelayBeatSync, inv B18 [med], no documented UI) is not implemented.
8. Main volume keeps the Phase-1 law (`g.master` pow3, default −9.3 dB) instead of S2's −9.0 dB at 50 %.
9. *(Withdrawn in review round 2, A23: S2 bend ranges up 0…48 / down −48…0 are the new destinations `g.bu2`/`g.bd2`, used outside S1 mode, §3.11.2, §6.3.2.)*
10. Splitters cannot contain splitters.
11. "Limiter Latency Comp" has no host to report to; the menu item is shown disabled with an explanation.
12. The S2 distortion "DC bias" control (inv C17, UI not located) is not implemented.
13. K35 (inv B12, unverified 2.1.x) is implemented as an IMPL type 107.
14. User assets are always embedded in exported preset/project files (no file-system paths in a browser); the S2 "Embed" button is shown as a status badge only.
15. `.fxp` / `.SerumPreset` import and `.SerumFX*` files are not supported; this app's JSON formats are used (Phase-1 §0.3-12 extended in §9.8).
16. The instrument keeps its own undo/redo stack (S2 global undo), separate from the app's project history (§13.2.4).
17. Skins, UI resize, MIDI CC map files and CC rockers remain excluded (ARCH). (The header drag-export and clip export are **implemented**, adapted per deviation 21.)
18. **Velocity Legato** (inv B19, reported for 2.1.0, [low, unverified]) is not implemented: no changelog or manual text defines it, and any guessed behaviour would have to be changed later; the menu does not show it. Revisit when a 2.1.x build can be measured (inv C18).
19. The arp menu's **Poly Count / Voice Steal Priority** items (inv B22, 2.1.0 [low]) are not implemented, for the same reason; the arpeggiator plays through the instrument's POLY and Voice Steal Priority (§12.3).
20. **Convolve SIZE, TONE, ϕ MIN, ATTACK, DECAY and DAMP are not modulation destinations** (`nomod`, §3.16.2): they reshape the impulse response, which is rebuilt off the audio thread (ARCH2 rule 3) and swapped with a 20 ms crossfade; a modulated reshaping would never settle. They stay automatable as base values (knobs, MIDI learn, presets).
21. **Drag-export** (header wave icon, clip slots): a browser cannot write into a DAW's track. A click (or tap) downloads the render/the SMF file; dragging uses the `DownloadURL` drag type where the browser supports it (Chromium), else behaves as a click. "Renders" is a user folder of the sample store, not a disk folder (§13.2.5).
22. **S1 Chaos sources** keep working in both S1 and S2 mode (exactly as in Phase 1) instead of being re-mapped automatically to Chaos LFOs; GLOBAL > CHAOS offers the approximate mapping as an explicit action (`chaosToLfo`, §11.1). An automatic re-map would make the S1 checkbox irreversible (§2.6.4).

### 0.7 Neutral-term rule (normative; ARCH2 rule 1)
Every factor, offset or extra stage that Phase 2 adds to a Phase-1 computation is **skipped — never computed — at its neutral value**, so that v1 states take the Phase-1 instruction sequence (fast `exp2`/`pow` approximations at a neutral argument may return 1 ± 1e-7, which shifts envelope stage boundaries by a sample, ≈ 1e-3 error). Gates are `CP.use` bits (constant per patch) or exact equality tests on the eff/base value (`=== 0`, `=== 0.5`); approximate tests (`< eps`) are not allowed. The complete list (each owner adds a unit test per row: a patch with the term at neutral renders **bit-identically** to the same patch with the term's code removed — `tests/ref-p1` or a test hook):

| Term | Neutral value | Gate | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|
| VC per-note offsets `panO`, `detO`, `cutO`, `envS` | VC inactive | `use & 1024` = 0 | D2/D1 |
| `vc.escale`, `vc.lscale` | eff n = 0.5 exactly (× 1) | equality per chunk | D2 |
| `lN.phase` (start phase) | eff value 0 | equality: start = `pts[start].x` without `frac` (§6.15.2) | D2 |
| `s.crs`, `k.hz`, `k.ratio` (ratio mode only), harmonics/step pitch modes | eff n 0.5 / mode `semi` | equality / `pMode` | D1 |
| `f.level`, `f2.level`, `g.direct`, `g.bus1v`, `g.bus2v`, bus sends | eff n 0.5 (× 1) / n 0 | equality; buses `use & 64` | D1, F1 |
| filter balance `k.fbal` / `s.fbal` / `n.fbal` at n 0 | F1 share exactly 1, F2 share 0 | equality (F2 sum skipped) | D1 |
| tuning table | identity | `use & 512` = 0 | D1 |
| MPE terms (`mpeSt`, expression sources) | MPE off | `use & 256` = 0 | D1, D2 |
| ENV 2–4 Start/End | 0 | equality (Phase-1 attack/release formula) | D2 |
| matrix `crv`, `acurve`, `rise`, `fall`, `randd` | none | `use & 4096` per row flags; no `randd` draws without `mRd` (§6.2) | D2 |
| unison START/SPAN, phase-memory menu | 0 / S1 mode | equality / `s1` | D1, D4 |
| OSC C, Filter 2, warp slot 2, sequencer, keyboard transpose/scale/swing | off / 0 | `use` bits 1, 2, 4, 8192 | all |
| LFO direction, swing, ×10, types | Forward / 0 / Normal | equality | D2 |
| FX: S2 rows of an S1-variant instance | — (not present) | variant | F1 |

### 0.8 Sign-off (decisions taken in review round 2 that need the lead's approval)
Each item names the decision (and, where there was one, the rejected alternative). Until signed off, engineers implement the decision as written.

1. **All warps on all oscillator types** (A5; deviation 4 withdrawn): sample-type phase-domain warps through the intra-period phase of the root period (§6.6.10); Spectral phase/frequency-domain warps through a per-stream delay line with a note-constant base delay ≤ 25 ms (§6.10.7), which is an **exception to the neutral-term rule** (a selected F/P-domain slot on a Spectral oscillator delays its output even at amount 0). Alternative: keep deviation 4 as an approved ARCH2 exception.
2. **S1 Compatibility Mode becomes a reversible checkbox** (A19): two field sets (v1 fields read in S1 mode, S2 fields otherwise), pre-filled by migration and kept in sync by edits where an exact mapping exists, matrix rows aliased at compile time, no "Convert to Serum 2" button (§2.6.4). Alternative: the one-way conversion as a numbered deviation.
3. **S1 Chaos sources stay** in both modes; conversion to Chaos LFOs is an explicit action (deviation 22).
4. **MIDI OUT inside the audio thread** through the engine's `PROCS` registry with one quantum of latency; the main-thread forwarding, `seqout` and `SxSeq.simulate` are dropped (B14; deviation 2 rewritten, §7.9).
5. **Milestone M1 split** into M1a (serial, no audible change, golden suite as merge gate) and M1b (skeletons) (B6, §0.5), with one joint D1+D2 edit.
6. **New modules and roles**: `laws.js` (C1), `assets.js` (S1), `appstore.js` (I), `midi.js` with the new role **I2** (B7, B19, B21, §0.2); the FX constants move from serum-core to fx-core once, by C1 in M1a.
7. **App storage moves to IndexedDB** (`synth-app`) for project autosave, user presets and banks, with a localStorage fallback and `navigator.storage.persist()` (B21, §12.6). This changes where every model's data lives, not only Serum's.
8. **Convolve shaping parameters are not modulatable** (B8; deviation 20) and IR shaping runs in `smpwork`.
9. **Factory multisample table reduced** to fit 16 MB per instrument / 150 MB total (fewer velocity layers and shorter sustain gates, §10.1) (B11).
10. **CPU gates tightened**: node fails at 1.5× (machine-calibrated), iPad loads measured with `tests/bench.html` at M2 end with a hard ceiling of 0.6 per scenario (B17, §14.1).
11. **Poly legato stays voice-side** (Phase-1 mechanism extended; the allocator does not pass `legato = 1`) (A20 partially rejected, §6.2, §12.3).
12. **Touch-device memory budgets** (worklet 128 MB, main LRU 128 MB, `KEEP_BYTES` 32 MB, IndexedDB 256 MB) and the project asset meter that refuses imports over budget (B11, §14.2).
13. **New deviations 18–22** (Velocity Legato, arp Poly Count menu, Convolve `nomod`, drag-export adaptation, chaos sources).
14. **S1-mode list extended** (§6.21 items 5–7: Hz-LFO HOST, knob-end phase memory, v1 bend rows), i.e. these Phase-1 behaviours change when the checkbox is switched off.
15. **Spectral onset and control timing** (B9): the first hop of a Spectral note is faded in and each frame's control values are taken one hop early (staggered synthesis), §6.10.1.
16. **Render-last captures the input** (A6): the header render replays the last *input* note group through the full patch incl. the sequencer instead of capturing post-sequencer notes in the worklet (§12.6, §13.2.5).
17. **Delay/filter memory caps** (B12): S2-variant Delay lines in Float32 (S1 variants unchanged), 32 MB per-track FX cap with bypass + notice, 20 Hz delay floor for Diffusor/Comb 2 (their tuning below 20 Hz is clamped).

---

**Lead sign-off on §0.8 (binding):** items 1–6, 8 and 10–17 APPROVED as written.
- Item 7 APPROVED WITH CONDITIONS: on first run `appstore.js` migrates every existing localStorage key (`synth.project`, `synth.settings`, `synth.v.*`, `synth.user.*`, `synth.seq303`, `synth.sx.*`, FX chain libraries, preferences) into IndexedDB; the localStorage copies are kept (read-only fallback) until the migrated data has been read back and verified equal, and are never deleted in the same session; project/preset file export/import is unchanged; if IndexedDB is unavailable or fails, the app keeps working on localStorage exactly as in Phase 1. A test covers migration of a realistic Phase-1 localStorage snapshot.
- Item 9 APPROVED WITH CONDITIONS: factory multisamples/samples/IRs are generated ON DEMAND on the user's device (render-farm worker / smpgen), lazily per instrument when first selected, with visible progress and cancel, cached in IndexedDB (evictable under the §14.2 budgets) — they are NEVER committed to the repository or shipped as binary assets. Bounces wait for (or trigger) generation of the assets they need.

## 1. Signal flow v2 (normative)

Per **voice** (stereo, OS rate; chunk = `proc.CH` samples):

```
           ┌──────── per source X ∈ {A, B, C, SUB, NOISE}: level/pan/unison applied inside the source ────────┐
OSC A ──┐  │ route(X) = Filter → share s1=(1−b)/2, s2=(1+b)/2 (b = eff X.fbal)                               │
OSC B ──┤  │      to filter k: s_k·G_k(X)·x  (G_k = on_k ∧ switch_k(X), ramped)   → u_k (filter input)        │
OSC C ──┼──┤      remainder s_k·(1−G_k(X))·x                                      → v_k (filter-k bypass)     │
SUB ────┤  │ route(X) = Main   → mainPre(envOn(X))     route(X) = Direct → directPre(envOn(X))                │
NOISE ──┘  │ route(X) = None   → nothing               sends: x·sendB1(X), x·sendB2(X) → bus pre-sums      │
           └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
FILTER k channel:  y_k = level_k · ( (1 − mix_k)·u_k + mix_k·F_k(u_k) + v_k )     (off: F_k not run, u_k ≡ 0)
                   route(F_k) = other filter → added to u_other (no gating);  Main / Direct / None as sources; sends
Channel gain:      g = AMP · (envOn ? ENV1 : 1)  applied per (destination, envOn) group, ramped per sample
Voice outputs:     main (L,R), direct (L,R), bus1 (L,R), bus2 (L,R)  ── added into the processor's OS-rate buffers
```

- ENV 1 (×AMP, `g.amp`) is applied **after** the filters, as in Phase 1. A source routed to a filter has no ENV toggle of its own; the filter channel's toggle applies. A source's bus sends are taken post its level/pan and get its channel gain (ENV toggle; Filter-routed sources: ENV on).
- Filter order per chunk: if `route(F2) = Filter (→F1)` and `route(F1) ≠ Filter` then F2 first, else F1 first. Both routed to each other is impossible after normalisation (§2.2.5) and treated at runtime as F2 → Main.
- Per **processor** (base rate after the engine's decimation of each used pair):

```
bus1_in = Σvoices bus1 (+ BUS 2 output when route(BUS2) = Bus 1)      bus2_in likewise
for each bus k in dependency order:  y_bk = rack_k(bus_k_in) (bypassed: identity) × g.busKv
    route(bus k): Main → added to main_in   Direct → added to direct   other Bus → added to that bus's input
main_out = rack_0(main_in)  (rack-0 bypass: identity)
out      = (main_out + direct·g.direct) × g.master × bypass-gate × soft-reset fade → engine DC block / limiter
```

v1 equivalence: no buses, `g.direct` = 1, every Phase-1 term in its Phase-1 order (§6.21, §8.1).

---

## 2. State schema v2

### 2.1 Serum state skeleton (`defaultSerum()` v2; new states, Init Preset)

```js
{
  v: 2,
  author: '', notes: '', tags: [],                 // tags: ≤ 16 strings of ≤ 24 chars (inv B25 browser tags)
  p: { /* every STORED destination id (§3.11): v1 synth ids 0–147, 'g.master', 'g.bypass', every non-virtual SYN2 id → n */ },
  osc: [ /* ×3: [0] A, [1] B, [2] C — same shape */ {
    type: 'wt',                                     // 'wt' | 'multi' | 'sample' | 'gran' | 'spec'            (inv B1)
    wt: 'factory:Default Shapes', remap: [[0, 0, 0], [1, 1, 0]], memSet: [16 numbers ∈ [0,1)],
    smp: '',                                        // sample asset id (or a wavetable id, §2.2.1), shared by sample / gran / spec (inv B1, B8)
    multi: '',                                      // multisample asset id                               (inv B5)
    phMem: 'all', phLegato: 0, smoothWT: 0,         // inv B3 phase memory / Phase Legato / Smooth Interpolation
    pitch: { mode: 'semi', src: 'note' },           // 'semi'|'harm'|'ratio'|'step'; src 'note'|'A'|'B'|'C'  (inv B2)
    bendTrack: 1,                                   // Pitch Bend Tracking (only shown when tracking is off)  (inv B2)
    fm: [0, 0],                                     // FM type per warp slot: 0 Thru-Zero, 1 Exp, 2 Linear      (inv B3.1)
    uni: { range: 2, mode: 0, spawn: 0 },           // per-osc unison RANGE (st), MODE (§3.7), SPAWN PATTERN     (inv B4)
    route: 0, env: 1,                               // 0 Filter 1 Main 2 Direct 3 None; ENV toggle (Main/Direct) (inv B1, B13)
    map: { k: [0, 127], v: [1, 127], fold: 0, warp: 0 },   // keyboard OSC MAPPING                             (inv B21)
    smpo: { /* §2.2.3 */ }, msmo: { override: 0, dlyBpm: 0 }, spec: { /* §2.2.4 */ }, xy: { on: 0, y: 0, yMode: 0 },
  } ],
  sub:    { direct: 0, route: 1, env: 1, contig: 0, map: {…} },          // direct: v1 legacy flag (always 0 after migration)
  noise:  { src: 'noise:White', oneshot: 0, keytrack: 0, direct: 0, route: 1, env: 1, map: {…} },
  filter: [ { keytrack: 0, clean: 0, route: 1, env: 1, pz: PZ_DEFAULT },     // Filter 1
            { keytrack: 0, clean: 0, route: 1, env: 1, pz: PZ_DEFAULT } ],   // Filter 2
  env:    [ /* ×4 */ { bpm: 0, legInv: 0, stealRe: 0 } ],                // inv B15
  lfo:    [ /* ×10 */ { mode: 'retrig', mono: 0, type: 'normal', dir: 'fwd', bpm: 1, dot: 0, trip: 0, anchor: 1,
                        x10: 0, swing: 0, pts: TRIANGLE, path: PATH_DEFAULT, loop: -1, start: 0 } ],
  lfoGrid: { x: 8, y: 8 },
  velo:   { pts: DIAG, porta: 0 }, note: { pts: DIAG, porta: 0 },
  chaos:  [ { mono: 0, sh: 0 }, { mono: 0, sh: 0 } ],                    // kept for S1 sounds (sources 17/18)
  macros: [ /* ×8 */ { name: 'Macro m', v: 0 } ],
  matrix: [ /* ×64 */ { src: '', amt: 0, dst: '', bi: 0, curve: 0, aux: '', inv: 0, byp: 0, out: 1,
                        acurve: 0, crv: null, rise: 0, fall: 0, slink: 0 } ],
  voicing: { mono: 0, legato: 0, poly: 8, always: 0, scaled: 0, oneNote: 1, steal: 0 },
  vc:     { name: 'Init', len: 1, steps: [/* ×8 */ [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]], sel: [1, 1, 1, 1, 1], lmode: 0 },   // inv B20
  kbd:    { trans: 0, key: -1, scale: 0, swing: 0, div: 1, arpMap: { k: [0, 127], v: [1, 127], fold: 0 } },  // inv B21
  arp:    { /* §2.4.1 */ }, clip: { /* §2.4.2 */ },
  global: { os: 2, a4: 440, s1: 0, nosmooth: 0,
            mpe: { on: 0, bend: 48, xyz: 0 },               // the two MPE preferences are app-wide (§13.10), not state
            tun: { name: '', kind: 'none', scl: '', kbm: '', tab: null } },
  midi: {},
  locks: { p: [], mod: [], unp: [], misc: { poly: 0, os: 0, tuning: 0 } },   // unp: controls unlocked inside a locked module
  ui: { page: 'osc', env: 0, mod: 'lfo1', envLock: 1, envZoom: 1, view3d: [0, 0, 0], wtSel: [0, 0, 0], filtView: [0, 0],
        osc: 0, oscTab: 0, filtTab: 0, mixFold: 0, lfoEd: 0, mtxSort: 0, kbdOpen: 1, arpEd: 0, clipZoom: [1, 1], envGrid: 0 },
}
```
`PZ_DEFAULT = [[1, 0, 0], [0, 1, 0], [0, 0, 1], [1, 0, 1]]` (corners TL LP, TR BP, BL HP, BR notch, §6.13.4). `TRIANGLE`, `DIAG` as Phase 1. `PATH_DEFAULT = [[0.5, 1, 0], [1, 0.5, 0], [0.5, 0, 0], [0, 0.5, 0], [0.5, 1, 0]]` (a diamond, closed). `DEF2` (§3.11.4) lists the base values of new states that differ from the destination defaults (v1 destinations keep their v1 defaults in the DEST table).

### 2.2 Field reference (new and changed fields only)

#### 2.2.1 Top level, oscillators

| Path | Type / range | Default | Meaning (inventory) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `v` | 2 | 2 | schema version (§2.6) |
| `tags` | ≤ 16 strings ≤ 24 chars | `[]` | preset tags (B25) |
| `osc[i].type` | `wt` `multi` `sample` `gran` `spec` | `wt` | oscillator type (B1). C is `wt` and off by default |
| `osc[i].wt` | table id | A/B/C `factory:Default Shapes` (v1 states keep theirs) | B3 (9-frame default table, §10.5) |
| `osc[i].smp` | asset id (`fsmp:` / `smp:`), a wavetable id (`factory:` / `user:`, "wavetables as samples", B8 / manual pp.70, 86, 105) or `''` | `''` | B6/B7/B8 sample, kept across Sample/Granular/Spectral switches. A wavetable id plays the table's frames concatenated as one looping sample (§9.4.8) |
| `osc[i].multi` | asset id (`fmsmp:` / `msmp:`) or `''` | `''` | B5 |
| `osc[i].phMem` | `all` `contig` `voice` | `all` | B3 phase memory menu (§6.4) |
| `osc[i].phLegato` | 0/1 | 0 | B3 Phase Legato (§6.4) |
| `osc[i].smoothWT` | 0/1 | 0 | B3 Smooth Interpolation (§6.5) |
| `osc[i].pitch.mode` | `semi` `harm` `ratio` `step` | `semi` | B2 OCT/SEM mode (§6.3) |
| `osc[i].pitch.src` | `note` `A` `B` `C` (≠ self) | `note` | Ratio source (SRC) |
| `osc[i].bendTrack` | 0/1 | 1 | B2 Pitch Bend Tracking (Sample/Granular/Spectral with tracking off) |
| `osc[i].fm[j]` | 0/1/2 | 0 | FM type of warp slot j: Thru-Zero / Exp / Linear (B3.1) |
| `osc[i].uni.range` | 0–48 st (0.01) | 2 | B4 RANGE (v1 `global.uniRange[i]` moves here) |
| `osc[i].uni.mode` | 0–4 (§3.7) | 0 | B4 MODE (v1 `global.uniMode[i]`) |

While on: the SCAN knob is relabelled **X** and bound to `k.pos`, and slicing acts as Off at runtime (the slicing state is kept, its menu items are disabled) — inv B7 "SCAN = X, slicing off" |

"MPE Pitch Bend (also) maps to Expr X" and "MPE Expr Y acts bi-directional" are **app preferences** (`mpeBendX`, `mpeYBi`, §13.10), not state; normalisation deletes the Round-1 keys `ybi`/`bendX` |

#### 2.2.3 Sample options `osc[i].smpo` (Sample, Granular, Spectral share them; inv B6–B8)

| Field | Range | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `loop` | 0 One-shot · 1 Fwd · 2 Rev · 3 Fwd/Rwd · 4 Tailed | 0 | B6 loop menu |
| `rel`, `link`, `exitRel` | 0/1 | 0 | Relative Loop, Link Loop Length, Exit Loop on Release |
| `loopGrains` | 0/1 | 0 | B7 Loop Grains |
| `scanRange` | 0 ±200 % · 1 ±400 % · 2 ±800 % | 0 | SCAN range |
| `scanKey`, `scanLock` | 0/1 | 0 | Key Track, Lock Scan Rate (to tempo, §6.7.2) |
| `lenBpm` | 0/1 | 0 | Sample Length to BPM (the SCAN knob becomes RATE and binds to the destination `k.lenrate`, §3.11.2; the Round-1 field `lenIdx` is gone — normalisation moves it to `p['k.lenrate'] = lenIdx/14`) |
| `snap` | 0 Off · 1 Zero · 2 Beats · 3 Loop | 0 | marker snapping (UI, §13.3.2) |
| `ops` | `{fade: 0–8, norm: 0/1, rev: 0/1, trim: null | [a, b]}` | none | non-destructive operations (§9.4.2); `fade` index into `[0,1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128]` ms. `trim`/`fade` change the playback entry; `norm`/`rev` are applied by the DSP (CP flags, §4.1) |
| `slice` | `{mode: 0 off·1 auto·2 manual, thr: 0–1, marks: ≤ 256 sorted fractions, toEnd, single, root: 0–127, sync: 0/1}` | `{0, 0.5, [], 0, 0, 36, 0}` | B6 slicing (`root` = MIDI note of the first slice; menu offers C−1…C8). `marks` always holds the **effective** marks: in Auto mode the panel rewrites them from the onset function whenever `thr` or the sample changes (so compiling stays pure); switching Auto → Manual keeps them as the starting manual marks (manual p.75); Manual → Auto recomputes. `sync` = Auto-Sync to Clip (§7.5.6) |
| `win` | 0–9 (§3.8) | 0 Hann | B7 window SHAPE |
| `dens` | `{mode: 0 Free·1 BPM·2 Grains, trip, dot, jump: 1, max: 1–256}` | `{0,0,0,1,256}` | B7 DENS |
| `glen` | `{mode: 0 Free·1 BPM·2 Percent, trip, dot}` | `{0,0,0}` | B7 LENGTH |
| `phaseLock`, `transients` | 0/1 | 0 | B8 SCAN menu |
| `anim`, `zoomSE`, `wave` | 0/1 | 0 | UI: Show Marker Animation, Zoom to Start and End, spectral Show Waveform |

#### 2.2.4 Spectral filter `osc[i].spec`

| Field | Range | Default | Meaning (inv B8) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `filt.kind` | `curve` · `wt` | `curve` | custom curve or "wavetable used as the filter" |
| `filt.pts` | points (§2.3 of Phase 1; x = log-frequency 20 Hz…20 kHz, y = gain) | flat `[[0,1,0],[1,1,0]]` | FILTER curve |
| `filt.wt` | table id | `''` | table whose frame 0 is the filter (§6.10.6) |
| `filt.grid` | 0–64 | 8 | GRID field |
| `smooth`, `postWarp` | 0/1 | 0 | Hi/Lo pane options |

#### 2.2.5 Normalisation constraints (in addition to Phase-1 §8.1)
- `filter[1].route = 0` (F2 → F1) and `filter[0].route = 0` (F1 → F2) at once ⇒ F2 becomes 1 (Main).
- Warp-source cycles (§6.6.6) are allowed in state; they are broken at runtime. `warpChoice2` (§11.1) resolves them interactively (most recent choice wins).
- `osc[i].pitch.src` = own oscillator ⇒ `note`. Ratio-source chains A→B→A ⇒ the later oscillator in A, B, C order falls back to `note` (normalised).
- `map.k[0] ≤ map.k[1]`, `map.v[0] ≤ map.v[1]` (swapped if reversed).
- `slice.marks` deduplicated, sorted, within (0, 1), ≤ 256.
- Ids by kind: `osc[i].wt` ∈ `factory:`/`user:`; `osc[i].smp` ∈ `fsmp:`/`smp:`/`factory:`/`user:` or `''`; `osc[i].multi` ∈ `fmsmp:`/`msmp:` or `''`; `noise.src` ∈ `noise:`/`nzuser:`/`color:`; `spec.filt.wt` ∈ `factory:`/`user:`/`''`.
- Matrix rows: `slink = 1` ⇒ `fall = rise`.
- `locks.unp` ⊆ ids that belong to a module in `locks.mod`; ids also in `locks.p` are removed from `unp` (an explicit parameter lock wins).
- Round-1 keys are migrated, never kept: `global.mpe.ybi`/`bendX` deleted; `smpo.lenIdx` → `p['k.lenrate']`; `kbd.swing` clamped to [−1, 1].

#### 2.2.6 Voice Control `vc` (inv B20)

| Field | Range | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | ≤ 24 | `Init` | preset name shown in the VC preset menu |
| `len` | 1–8 | 1 | active steps |
| `steps[j]` | `[pan, det, cut, env, m1, m2]` each ∈ [−1, 1] | zeros | SEQ values per step (§6.16) |
| `sel` | 5 × 0/1 for A, B, C, N, S | all 1 | oscillator selection toggles |
| `lmode` | 0 % · 1 Rate | 0 | LFO SCALING "by percentage or rate" (manual p.318): Rate snaps the effective LFO scale to the nearest (in log) of `VC_RATES = [1/4, 1/3, 1/2, 2/3, 3/4, 1, 4/3, 3/2, 2, 3, 4]` and shows it as a ratio (`3/2`) |

The RANDOM amounts and SCALING are destinations (`vc.*`, §3.11). Both SCALING fields display as 25–400 % (100 % = n 0.5 = neutral).

### 2.3 Matrix row v2 (inv B18)

| Field | Type | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `src`, `amt`, `dst`, `bi`, `curve`, `aux`, `inv`, `byp`, `out` | as Phase 1 | | `dst` may be a dynamic FX id `fx.<inst>.<pid>` (§3.16.4) |
| `acurve` | [−1, 1] | 0 | aux CRV (single bend on the aux source) |
| `crv` | `null` or points (2–64, Phase-1 §2.3 graph, x/y ∈ [0,1]) | `null` | Editable Curve; replaces `curve` when present |
| `rise`, `fall` | [0, 1] | 0 | RISE / FALL slew of the curved source; time = `4·n³` s (0 = none) |
| `slink` | 0/1 | 0 | SmoothLink (inv B18 slot key): RISE and FALL edit together (`fall = rise`, kept by normalisation); link toggle in the `mcurve` editor |

Active iff Phase-1 rule (`src && dst && !byp && amt ≠ 0 && out ≠ 0`) and the destination resolves (a dynamic FX id whose instance no longer exists makes the row inactive but keeps it; the matrix shows it red).

### 2.4 Sequencer and keyboard state (inv B21–B23; owner D6, schema in `seq-core.js`)

#### 2.4.1 `s.arp`

```js
arp: {
  bank: 'Init', dirty: 0, sel: 0, quant: 4, editAll: 0, midiOut: 0, outTrack: '', trigOct: 1,
  slots: [ /* ×12 */ {
    name: '',                    // '' → auto name "<Shape> (<rate>)"
    shape: 7,                    // ARP_SHAPES index (§3.15.1), 7 = Up
    bpm: 1, trip: 0, dot: 0,
    n: { rate: 0.75, hz: 0.5, shift: 0.75, range: 0, offset: 0, repeats: 0, gate: 0.375, chance: 1,
         rrate: 0.4, vdecay: 0.5, vtarget: 0.5, trans: 0.5, wtrans: 0.5, wrange: 0 },   // virtual-dest bases (n)
    rshape: 0,                   // transpose range shape (§3.15.2)
    latch: 0, thru: 0, chancePre: 0,
    rLaunch: 1, rRate: 0, rNote: 0, rFirst: 0,
    vel: 0, vRetrig: 1,
    pat: { name: 'Init', len: 16, mode: 0, time: 4, stepMode: 0, wrap: 0, grid: 2,
           ev: [ /* [t16, len16, row, vel 1–127, accent 0/1] */ ],
           strum: [ /* [t16, amt 0–1] */ ], lanes: { vel: null, gate: null, chance: null, x: null } },
  } ],
}
```
| Field | Range | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `quant` | `LAUNCH_QUANT` index 0–8 (§3.15.5) | LAUNCH QUANT |
| `midiOut` | 0 Off · 1 Clip Player · 2 On | MIDI OUT (§7.9) |
| `outTrack` | track id or `''` | MIDI OUT target track of this project (§7.9; same field in `s.clip`) |
| `trigOct` | 0 Off · 1–11 = octave index (notes `12·(k−1)` … `12·(k−1)+11`) | MIDI Input Trigger Octave (default 1 = notes 0–11, "lowest octave") |
| `slots[j].n.*` | [0,1] | base n of the virtual destinations `arp.*` (§3.11, §5.6) for this slot |
| `pat.len` | 1–256 sixteenths | pattern LENGTH |
| `pat.mode` | `ARP_PMODES` 0–7 | MODE |
| `pat.time` | `TIME_LADDER` 0–6 | TIME |
| `pat.stepMode` | 0–3 | STEP MODE |
| `pat.wrap` | 0 Wrap · 1 Phantom · 2 Clamp | WRAP (§7.4.3) |
| `pat.grid` | `GRID_LADDER` 0–6 | editor grid (UI) |
| `pat.ev` | ≤ 512 events; `t16` ≥ 0 < len, `len16` > 0, `row` ∈ ±1…±32 (±: from bottom / top of the held notes) | graph events |
| `pat.lanes.*` | `null` or points over x = t/len | automation lanes |

#### 2.4.2 `s.clip`

```js
clip: {
  bank: 'Init', dirty: 0, sel: 0, trig: 0, editAll: 0, midiOut: 0, outTrack: '', trigOct: 1, preview: -1,
  rec: { mode: 0, metro: 1 },
  clips: [ /* ×12 */ {
    name: '', len: 64, start: 0, off: 0, offQ: 2,
    kb: 1, mode: 0, time: 4, quant: 6, retrig: 1, veloTrig: 0, noteGate: 0,
    n: { trans: 0.5, rate: 0.5, offset: 0 },          // virtual-dest bases of clip.trans / clip.rate / clip.offset
    notes: [ /* [t16, len16, note, vel 1–127, mute 0/1, x 0–1, y 0–1, z 0–1] */ ],
    lanes: { m1: null, …, m8: null },                 // macro automation over x = t/len
  } ],
}
```
| Field | Range | Meaning (inv B23) |
|---|---|---|
| `trig` | 0 MONO · 1 POLY | TRIGGER MODE |
| `preview` | −1 or 0–11 | Set as Preview Clip |
| `rec.mode` | 0 OVERDUB · 1 EXTEND | record mode; `metro` metronome on |
| `len` | 1–1024 sixteenths | clip length (right flag) |
| `start` | 0–len−1 | left flag |
| `off` | start ≤ off < len | start offset marker |
| `offQ` | `GRID_LADDER` | offset quantise (marker menu Quantize; KB SPAN Offset step) |
| `kb` | 0 Off · 1 Mono · 2 Poly · 3 Offset | KB SPAN |
| `mode` | `CLIP_MODES` 0–8 | MODE |
| `time` | `TIME_LADDER` | slice TIME for random modes |
| `quant` | `LAUNCH_QUANT` | LAUNCH QUANT |
| `retrig`, `veloTrig`, `noteGate` | 0/1 | RETRIG, VELO TRIG, NOTE GATE |
| `notes` | ≤ 1024 per clip, ≤ 4096 per bank | piano-roll notes (16ths, float) |

#### 2.4.3 `s.kbd`

| Field | Range | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `trans` | −24…+24 | 0 | TRANSPOSE |
| `key` | −1 (off, "--") or 0–11 (C…B) | −1 | KEY |
| `scale` | `SCALES` index | 0 Major | SCALE |
| `swing` | −1…1 (0 = OFF) | 0 | SWING amount (§7.2); displayed in Live's convention `50 + 37.5·s` % (12.5–87.5 %, inv B21), "OFF" only at 0 |
| `div` | 0 1/8 · 1 1/16 · 2 1/32 | 1 | swing division |
| `arpMap` | `{k, v, fold}` | full | ARP row of OSC MAPPING |

### 2.5 FX state v2 (`track.fx`, every track, any model; owner F1, schema in `fx-core.js`)

```js
{
  v: 2,
  racks: [
    { mods: [ /* instances */ ], byp: 0, lock: 0, route: 0 },   // [0] MAIN   (route unused)
    { mods: [], byp: 0, lock: 0, route: 0 },                    // [1] BUS 1: route 0 Main · 1 Direct · 2 Bus 2
    { mods: [], byp: 0, lock: 0, route: 0 },                    // [2] BUS 2: route 0 Main · 1 Direct · 2 Bus 1
  ],
  ui: { rack: 0, sel: '', expand: 0, br: {} },
}
// instance
{ id: 'dly', type: 'dly', v: 1, byp: 0, p: { /* pid → n, every parameter of the type/variant table (§3.16) */ },
  sub: { /* type-specific structured data, §3.16.3 */ }, ph: 0,
  br: [[/* inst */], [/* inst */]], bb: [0, 0],           // splitters only: branch module lists, branch bypasses
  ui: { lvl: 0, lvl2: 0 } }
```

| Field | Range | Meaning (inv B14) |
|---|---|---|
| `racks[k].mods` | ≤ 16 top-level instances per rack | processing order = list order |
| `racks[k].byp` | 0/1 | rack FX bypass (mixer red buttons, B13) |
| `racks[k].lock` | 0/1 | Lock FX Bus (B14.1): `applyPresetFx` keeps that rack; modulation to its instances is not kept (the incoming matrix replaces the old one and incoming rows addressing ids of the locked rack are dropped), as S2 clears modulations to a locked bus. Init Preset clears every rack lock (B2) |
| `racks[k].route` | 0/1/2 | bus routing (B13); rack 0 ignores it. Both buses routed to each other ⇒ normalisation sets BUS 2 → Main |
| `inst.id` | `[a-z][a-z0-9]{0,11}` unique per track | stable instance id; dynamic destination ids are `fx.<id>.<pid>` |
| `inst.type` | `FXTYPES` id (§3.16.1) | |
| `inst.v` | 1 (S1 variant) · 2 (S2 variant) | §3.16.2; types without an S1 variant are always 2; Hyper/Flanger are always 1 |
| `inst.byp` | 0/1 | v1 temporary bypass (power button in Phase-1 headers). S2 UI shows only the `on` parameter; `byp = 1` (possible in migrated states) is shown as a "bypassed" chip that clears it |
| `inst.ph` | [0,1) | saved LFO phase (Hyper, Flanger L, Phaser L, Chorus, Bode retrig phase) |
| `inst.br`, `inst.bb` | splitters: 2 or 3 branches, ≤ 8 instances each, **no splitters inside** | B14.3 |
| total instances | ≤ 48 per track (top level + branches) | |

New instance ids: the type id if unused in the track, else `<type><k>` with the lowest k ≥ 2. Duplicate ("Duplicate FX Module") and paste assign new ids; matrix rows copied "with mods" are rewritten to the new id.

Other track kinds: non-Serum tracks show and process only `racks[0]` (their buses have no input). Effect chains (`synth.fxchains`) v2: `{type: 'synth.fxchains', v: 2, chains: [{name, fx}]}` (v1 chains migrate on read, §2.6.3). Rack presets: `{type: 'synth.fxrack', v: 1, name, rack}`. Module presets: `{type: 'synth.fxmod', v: 2, name, ftype, fv, p, sub}` stored per type in `synth.fxmod.<type>` (v1 lists `synth.fxmod.<m>` migrate on read). **Default module preset** (inv B14.1 "Save as Default Preset"): one payload of the same format per type under `synth.fxmod.default.<type>`; `newInstance` applies it (its `p`, `sub`, variant 2) when the caller passes it (§11.2); "Clear Default Preset" deletes the key.

### 2.6 Versioning, migration v1 → v2, S1 mode (normative)

#### 2.6.1 `normalizeSerum(s)`
`s.v` absent/1 ⇒ `migrate1(normalizeV1(s))` then v2 normalisation; `s.v = 2` ⇒ v2 normalisation; `s.v > 2` ⇒ best effort (unknown keys dropped). `normalizeV1` = the Phase-1 `normalizeSerum` (kept verbatim in `serum-core.js` as an internal function). Idempotent; returns a new object.

#### 2.6.2 `migrate1(v1)` — every step is exact (no audible change)

| Step | Rule |
|---|---|
| p | copy every v1 key (synth 0–147, `g.master`, `g.bypass`) unchanged. Every stored SYN2 id ← its **destination default** (§3.11) — the defaults are chosen so that the new switches (`f.routeC`, `f2.route*`, `s.track`, `c.track`) and levels (`f.level`, bus levels) reproduce Phase-1 behaviour — except the **S2 counterparts** below |
| S2 counterparts (`S1_PAIRS`, §2.6.4; unused in S1 mode, read when the checkbox is switched off) | `a.w1`/`a.wvar1` and `b.w1`/`b.wvar1` ← `S1_TO_S2_WARP[round(22·n_k.warpMode)]` (mode index / 81, var); `s.shp` ← `S1_TO_S2_SUB[round(4·n_s.shape)]/5`; `f.type2` ← `round(95·n_f.type)/107`; `g.bu2` ← `clamp(v_bendup, 0, 48)/48`; `g.bd2` ← `(clamp(v_benddn, −48, 0) + 48)/48` |
| oscillators | `osc[0..1]` = v1 `{wt, remap, memSet}` + `type 'wt'`, `uni {range: global.uniRange[i], mode: global.uniMode[i], spawn 0}`, `route 0` (Filter), `env 1`, `phMem` = `'voice'` if base `k.phase` n ≥ 0.9995 and `k.rand` n ≥ 0.9995, `'contig'` if only `k.phase` n ≥ 0.9995, else `'all'` (the S2 rendition of Phase-1 Mem/MemTrig; S1 mode keeps reading the knob ends, §6.4), every other field default; `p['a.fbal'] = p['b.fbal'] = 0` (n 0 ⇒ balance −1 = Filter 1). `osc[2]` = default C (`c.on` 0) with `route 0`, `wt 'factory:Default'` |
| sub / noise | `route = direct ? 2 : 0`, `env 1`, `direct 0`; `s.fbal`/`n.fbal` n 0 |
| filters | `filter[0] = {keytrack: v1 filter.keytrack, clean 0, route 1, env 1}`, `filter[1]` default (F2 off) |
| envelopes | `env` = 4 × default; ENV 4 and Start/End destinations at defaults (Start/End n 0) |
| LFOs | 8 → 10. `mode`: `trig → retrig`, `env → env`, `off → free` with `mono = 1`; others `mono 0`; `type normal`, `dir fwd`, `x10 0`, `swing 0`, `path PATH_DEFAULT`; LFO 9/10 default with `mode 'free', mono 1` (inert, like v1's unused LFOs) |
| macros | 4 → 8 (`Macro 5`…`Macro 8`, v 0) |
| matrix | 32 rows → slots 0–31 unchanged + `acurve 0, crv null, rise 0, fall 0, slink 0`; slots 32–63 empty; FX ids renamed by **RENAME1** |
| voicing | + `steal 0` (Newest = v1 allocation) |
| global | `s1: 1`, `os`, `a4`, `nosmooth 0`, `mpe {on 0, bend 48, xyz 0}`, `tun` none; `uniRange`/`uniMode` removed (moved to osc) |
| midi, locks | ids renamed by RENAME1; `locks.mod` names unchanged (`oscA`, `oscB`, `noise`, `sub`, `filter`); `locks.unp = []` |
| ui | v1 fields kept; new fields default; `page` unchanged |
| arp, clip, kbd, vc, tags | defaults (`arp.on`, `clip.on` n 0) |

**RENAME1** (destination ids): `fx.dim.size → fx.hyper.dsize`, `fx.dim.mix → fx.hyper.dmix`, `fx.dim.level → fx.hyper.dlevel`. Every other v1 FX id `fx.<m>.<pid>` already equals the v2 id of the migrated instance `m` (§2.6.3).

#### 2.6.3 `normalizeFx(fx)` and FX migration
`fx.v` absent/1 ⇒ `migrateFx1(normalizeFxV1(fx), refs)` (refs = optional Serum state, to keep instances its rows/locks/MIDI map reference). For each module m in v1 `order`: instance `{id: m, type: m, v: 1, byp: byp[m], p: v1 values of every v1 FX dest of m (+ RENAME1 for Hyper's Dimension params), sub, ph, ui: {lvl: ui.lvl[m], lvl2: ui.lvl.dim (hyper only)}}` with `sub`: dist `{xa, xb}`, cho `{mono}`, rev `{type}`; `ph`: hyper `phase[0]`, flg `[1]`, phs `[2]`, cho `[3]`. The instance is **kept** if its enable base ≥ 0.5, or any parameter or `sub` differs from the v1 default, or `byp = 1`, or `refs` references one of its ids; otherwise it is dropped (lossless: a pristine disabled module produces no output and is re-creatable). `racks[0].lock = fx.lock`; buses empty. Order of kept instances = v1 order.

#### 2.6.4 S1 mode (`global.s1`) — a reversible checkbox (manual p.319 "Disable this option…")
- Migrated v1 states have `s1 = 1`; `defaultSerum()` has `s1 = 0`; factory presets carry their own flag.
- The behaviours listed in §6.21 depend on it. Every other v2 feature (OSC C, Filter 2, warp slot 2, new osc types, buses, arp, clip, MPE, …) works in both modes.
- GLOBAL > QUALITY shows the checkbox **S1 Compatibility Mode**. Toggling it changes **only** `global.s1` (`setS1(s, on)`, §11.1, undoable): no other field is written, so switching it back on restores the S1 rendition exactly. There is no "Convert to Serum 2" button.
- **Paired fields** `S1_PAIRS` (v1 field → S2 counterpart): `a.warpMode` → `a.w1` + `a.wvar1`; `b.warpMode` → `b.w1` + `b.wvar1`; `s.shape` → `s.shp`; `f.type` → `f.type2`; `g.bendup` → `g.bu2`; `g.benddn` → `g.bd2`; the knob ends of `k.phase`/`k.rand` → `osc[k].phMem` (k = A, B). S1 mode reads the v1 field, S2 mode the counterpart. Migration fills the counterparts (§2.6.2). **Edits keep the pairs consistent where an exact mapping exists** (`syncS1(s, id)`, called by the panel after every edit of a paired field): an edit of a v1 field writes its counterpart through the forward map (`S1_TO_S2_WARP`, `S1_TO_S2_SUB`, identity for filter types, clamps for bends, knob-end rule for phase memory); an edit of a counterpart writes the v1 field through the inverse map when the new value is in the image of the forward map (e.g. Sync with Var 0, ½ or 1; any filter type ≤ 95; a sub shape other than Triangle; bends within ±24), and leaves it unchanged otherwise (the GLOBAL page then shows "S1 rendition differs: <controls>").
- **Matrix rows** are never rewritten by the checkbox. A row whose destination is the other mode's field of a pair compiles against the active field with `amt × hiFrom/hiTo` (`a.warpMode` 22 ↔ `a.w1` 81; `s.shape` 4 ↔ `s.shp` 5; `f.type` 95 ↔ `f.type2` 107; bends by their ranges) — approximate under modulation, shown in the matrix with an "≈" badge.
- The sub level law, the filter key-track reference, Hz-LFO HOST and knob-end phase memory change with the flag (§6.21); the checkbox tooltip says so. S1 chaos sources keep working in both modes (deviation 22).
- `S1_TO_S2_SUB = [0, 1, 3, 4, 5]` (Sine, RoundRect, Saw, Square, Pulse → S2 indices; S2 inserts Triangle at 2).
- `S1_TO_S2_WARP` (v1 index → `[WARPS2 index, var]`): 0→[0,0], 1→[1,0], 2→[1,0.5], 3→[1,1], 4…10→[2…8,0] (same order), 11→[9,0] Flip, 12→[10,0] Mirror, 13…16→[11…14,0], 17→[15,0] Quantize, 18→[39,0] PD (o1) (for B: PD (A) is o1 too), 19→[46,0] AM (o1), 20→[52,0] RM (o1), 21→[41,0] PD (Noise), 22→[42,0] PD (Sub). Exact for every entry except 2 (Sync 1/2 Win.) — the S2 PD index law equals the v1 FM law (§6.6.4), and S2 Sync with Var 1 equals v1 Sync Window (§6.6.2).

#### 2.6.5 Compiled versions
`CP_VER = 2`, `FXP_VER = 2`, `SQP_VER = 1`. A DSP script receiving another major renders silence (voices inactive / rack bypassed / sequencer inert) and warns once (Phase-1 rule). Minor bumps (fractional, RULINGS) only append.

### 2.7 Limits

| Item | Limit |
|---|---|
| Serum state (JSON) | typical < 48 KB; worst case (all graphs at 480 points, 64 rows with 64-point curves, full clip banks) < 512 KB |
| FX state | < 64 KB |
| Clip notes | 1024 per clip, 4096 per bank; arp events 512 per pattern |
| Points | LFO/remap/velo/note/path graphs ≤ `MAX_PTS` 480; matrix curves ≤ 64; spectral filter ≤ 128 |
| Instances | 16 top-level per rack, 8 per splitter branch, 48 per track |

---

## 3. Canonical constants (delta; exported by the named pure module, deep-frozen, embedded identically in the DSP scripts)

Every list keeps its Phase-1 entries at their Phase-1 indices; new entries append. Every new list has a stable index and string id.

### 3.1 Laws
No new law. `LAWS` stays the 12 laws of Phase-1 §3.1. Destinations whose engine value is a dB figure use `lin` with `dbv`/`dbtrim` formats and convert in the DSP (`g = 10^(v/20)`).

### 3.2 Display formats (new `FMT` codes; `fmtParam(id, n, ctx)` with `ctx = {s, fx, bpm, frames: [Fa, Fb, Fc], interp: [ia, ib, ic], osc: 0–2}`)

| code | output |
|---|---|
| `ratio2` | `'×' + v.toFixed(3)` (`×1.000`) |
| `hzoff` | `(v>0?'+':'') + v.toFixed(2) + ' Hz'` |
| `st1` | `(v>0?'+':'') + v.toFixed(1) + ' st'` |
| `xmul` | `'×' + v.toFixed(2)` |
| `smpPos` | `(100·v).toFixed(1) + '%'`; with `ctx.s` and the osc's sample length L known to the UI: time of `v·L` (`time` format) |
| `scan` | `Math.round(v·R·100) + '%'`, R = 2/4/8 from `smpo.scanRange` (no ctx: 2). (With Length to BPM the knob shows `k.lenrate`, format `enum:LEN_LADDER`) |
| `vcScale` | `vc.escale`, and `vc.lscale` with `vc.lmode = 0`: `Math.round(100·v) + '%'` (25–400 %); `vc.lscale` with `lmode = 1`: the snapped `VC_RATES` entry as a ratio (`'3/2'`, `'1'`) |
| `semiH` | SEM in Harmonics mode: semi ≥ 0 → `'×' + (semi + 1)`; semi < 0 → `'÷' + (1 − semi)` (§6.3.3); in Step mode `semi + ' steps'` |
| `slice` | `'Slice ' + (v + 1)` |
| `dens` | `smpo.dens.mode`: 0 → `hz` of `0.1·8000^n`; 1 → label of `gridLadder(dot, trip)[round(n·(L−1))]`; 2 → `Math.round(1 + 63n) + ' grains'` |
| `glen` | `smpo.glen.mode`: 0 → `time` of `0.001·10^(4n)` s; 1 → ladder label as `dens`; 2 → `Math.round(800^n)` + `%` (1–800 %) |
| `fbal` | v = 2n−1: v ≤ −0.995 → `F1`; v ≥ 0.995 → `F2`; else `'F1 ' + round(50(1−v)) + ' / F2 ' + round(50(1+v))` |
| `arpRate` | the playing/selected slot's `bpm`: ladder label (`arpLadder(dot, trip)`, §3.15.4); else `hz` of `0.5·100^n` |
| `inf0` | v = 0 → `∞`, else `String(v)` (arp REPEATS) |
| `enum:WARPS2` | label with o1/o2 resolved for the owning oscillator (§3.4.2) |
| `enum:<LIST>` | for every new list of §3.15–§3.16 |
| `fx:<pid>` | FX parameter formats (§3.16): delegated to the type table's `fmt` |

`parseParam` accepts every new format (ratios `x1.5`, `3:2`; Hz offsets; ladder labels for `arpRate`/`dens`/`glen` in BPM mode; `F1 30 / F2 70`, `F1`, `F2`; slice numbers). `fmtParam`/`parseParam` MUST NOT throw for any id, including dynamic FX ids.

### 3.3 Oscillator types, pitch modes, phase memory (`OSC_TYPES`, `PITCH_MODES`, `PHASE_MEM`)

| Constant | index: id (label) |
|---|---|
| `OSC_TYPES` | 0 `wt` (Wavetable) · 1 `multi` (Multisample) · 2 `sample` (Sample) · 3 `gran` (Granular) · 4 `spec` (Spectral) |
| `PITCH_MODES` | 0 `semi` (Semitones) · 1 `harm` (Harmonics) · 2 `ratio` (Ratio) · 3 `step` (Step — enabled only when `global.tun.kind = 'scl'`) |
| `PHASE_MEM` | 0 `all` (All Voices) · 1 `contig` (Contiguous) · 2 `voice` (Per Voice) |
| `ROUTES_SRC` | 0 Filter · 1 Main · 2 Direct · 3 None |
| `ROUTES_FILT` | 0 Filter (the other one) · 1 Main · 2 Direct · 3 None |
| `ROUTES_BUS` | 0 Main · 1 Direct · 2 Bus (the other one) |
| `STEAL` | 0 Newest · 1 Oldest · 2 Highest · 3 Lowest · 4 Velocity (labels = S2's: Newest keeps newest notes, steals the earliest) |
| `MPE_XYZ` | 0 Off · 1 XYZ → Macro 1,2,3 · 2 YZ → Macro 1,2 · 3 Y → Mod Wheel |
| `FM_TYPES` | 0 Thru-Zero · 1 Exp · 2 Linear |

### 3.4 Serum 2 warp list (`WARPS2`; destinations `k.w1`, `k.w2`; inv B3.1)

#### 3.4.1 Table
Domains: **F** frequency (modifies the phase increment), **P** phase (maps the read phase), **O** output (acts on the read sample), **S** spectral (acts on STFT frames, Spectral oscillators only). Availability: **W** Wavetable, **X** Multisample / Sample / Granular, **Sp** Spectral. Every mode except the S-domain list (58–81) is available on **every** type (ARCH2 "every S2 warp"; inv B3.1 "all osc types unless noted"): on X types the P/F/O domains act on the intra-period phase of the root period (§6.6.10); on Spectral oscillators O-domain modes act per stream after resynthesis and P/F-domain modes through the per-stream warp delay line (§6.10.7). The avail column below therefore reads **all** for 0–57 and **Sp** for 58–81 (`WARP_AVAIL`). `w` = effective slot amount per unison voice (Phase-1 §6.7 rule with `k.warp`/`k.uniwarp` for slot 1, `k.warp2`/`k.uniwarp2` for slot 2), `v` = slot Var (`k.wvar1`/`k.wvar2`), `x(φ)` = table read, `m` = modulator sample (§6.6.6). Formulas are normative; §6.6 gives the evaluation order.

| idx | id | label (OSC A) | dom | avail | transform | speed | neutral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `off` | Off | — | all | — | 1 | always |
| 1 | `sync` | Sync | P | all | `r = 2^(4.4w)`; `φ' = frac(φ·r)`; output × `(1 − v + v·sin²(πφ))` | r | w = 0 and v = 0 |
| 2 | `bend_p` | Bend + | P | all | Phase-1 Bend + | Phase 1 | w = 0 |
| 3 | `bend_n` | Bend - | P | all | Phase-1 Bend − | | w = 0 |
| 4 | `bend_pn` | Bend +/- | P | all | Phase-1 Bend +/- | | w = 0.5 |
| 5 | `pwm` | PWM | P | all | Phase-1 PWM | | w = 0 |
| 6 | `asym_p` | Asym + | P | all | Phase-1 | | w = 0 |
| 7 | `asym_n` | Asym - | P | all | Phase-1 | | w = 0 |
| 8 | `asym_pn` | Asym +/- | P | all | Phase-1 | | w = 0.5 |
| 9 | `flip` | Flip | O | all | Phase-1 Flip | 1 | never |
| 10 | `mirror` | Mirror | P | all | Phase-1 Mirror | | never |
| 11–14 | `remap1`…`remap4` | Remap 1–4 | P | all | Phase-1 (G = the osc's remap graph) | | w = 0 |
| 15 | `quantize` | Quantize | P | all | Phase-1 | 1 | w = 0 |
| 16 | `oddeven` | Odd/Even | O | all | `xo = (x(φ) − x(φ+½))/2`, `xe = (x(φ) + x(φ+½))/2`; `y = w < ½ ? xo + (x − xo)·2w : x + (xe − x)·(2w − 1)` | 1 | w = 0.5 |
| 17 | `lpf` | LPF | O | all | per unison voice TPT SVF LP, Q = 0.7071, `fc = clamp(f_i·2^(10(1−w)), 8, 0.45·fs)` | 1 | w = 0 (skipped) |
| 18 | `hpf` | HPF | O | all | per unison voice TPT SVF HP, `fc = clamp(f_i·2^(10w − 3), 8, 0.45·fs)` | 1 | w = 0 (skipped) |
| 19–32 | `d_tube`, `d_soft`, `d_hard`, `d_diode1`, `d_diode2`, `d_linfold`, `d_sinfold`, `d_zerosq`, `d_asym`, `d_rectify`, `d_sinesh`, `d_stomp`, `d_tape`, `d_softsat` | Tube, Soft Clip, Hard Clip, Diode 1, Diode 2, Linear Fold, Sine Fold, Zero-Square, Asym, Rectify, Sine Shaper, Stomp Box, Tape Sat., Soft Sat. | O | all | `y = x + (D_m(x, d=w) − x)·min(1, 10w)`, D_m = the FX distortion shaper of the same name (Phase-1 §8.5.4 modes 0–7, 9, 10, 13, 14, 15; Soft Sat. as FX mode 17, §8.2), at the oscillator rate, per unison voice (Stomp Box keeps its filter state per unison voice) | 1 | w = 0 |
| 33 | `fm_o1` | FM (B) | F | all | `inc' = inc·g(m)`: Thru-Zero `g = 1 + 8w²·m`; Linear `g = max(0, 1 + 8w²·m)`; Exp `g = 2^(4w·m)` (type = `osc.fm[slot]`) | TZ/Lin `1 + 8w²`, Exp `2^(4w)` | w = 0 |
| 34 | `fm_o2` | FM (C) | F | all | as 33 | | |
| 35 | `fm_noise` | FM (Noise) | F | all | as 33, m = noise | | |
| 36 | `fm_sub` | FM (Sub) | F | all | as 33, m = sub | | |

| 38 | `fm_f2` | FM (Filter 2) | F | all | as 33 | | |
| 39–44 | `pd_o1`, `pd_o2`, `pd_noise`, `pd_sub`, `pd_f1`, `pd_f2` | PD (B), PD (C), PD (Noise), PD (Sub), PD (Filter 1), PD (Filter 2) | P | all | `φ' = frac(φ + I·m)`, `I = 3·w^1.5` (sub: `4·w^1.5`) — the Phase-1 FM law | `1 + 6.3·I` | w = 0 |
| 45 | `pd_self` | PD (Self) | P | all | as 39 with m = this unison voice's previous output sample, `I = 1.5·w^1.5` | | w = 0 |

#### 3.4.2 Naming and availability rules
- `o1`/`o2` = the first / second **other** oscillator in A, B, C order: A → (B, C), B → (A, C), C → (A, B). `WARPS2[i] = {id, name, nameO: [nameForA, nameForB, nameForC], dom, avail}`.
- A mode unavailable for the oscillator's current type (column avail: only the spectral modes 58–81 on non-Spectral types) acts as **Off** (no state change; the menu shows it greyed with the reason). The "Swap Warps" menu item is an action (§13.3, `swapWarps` §11.1), not a list entry.
- The Phase-1 list `WARPS` (23 entries) stays and is used **only** by `a.warpMode`/`b.warpMode` in S1 mode (§6.21).

Destinations with the 0–107 list: `f.type2`, `f2.type`, S2-variant FX Filter `type`; `f.type` (v1, 0–95) stays for S1 mode and S1-variant FX filters.

### 3.7 Unison (delta)
`UNI_STACKS`, `UNI_MODES` and every Phase-1 unison formula unchanged; they apply per oscillator with `osc[i].uni.range`/`.mode`. `UNI_SPAWN = ['Together', 'Even', 'Exp', 'Random']` (granular only, §6.9.2). New per-voice offsets: START (`k.ustart`) and SPAN (`k.uspan`) for Sample/Granular/Spectral (§6.7.6).

### 3.8 Granular windows (`GRAIN_WINDOWS`; u ∈ [0,1] = position in the grain)

| idx | id | label | `W(u)` |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `hann` | Hann | `0.5 − 0.5·cos(2πu)` |
| 1 | `welch` | Welch | `1 − (2u − 1)²` |
| 2 | `gauss` | Gaussian | `exp(−0.5·((u − 0.5)/0.15)²)` |
| 3 | `bh` | Blackman-Harris | `0.35875 − 0.48829 cos 2πu + 0.14128 cos 4πu − 0.01168 cos 6πu` |
| 4 | `sinc` | Sinc | `sinc(4·(2u − 1))·(0.5 − 0.5 cos 2πu)`, `sinc(t) = sin(πt)/(πt)` |
| 5 | `tukey` | Tukey | α = 0.5: `u < α/2 ? 0.5 − 0.5 cos(2πu/α) : u > 1 − α/2 ? 0.5 − 0.5 cos(2π(1 − u)/α) : 1` |
| 6 | `tri` | Triangle | `1 − |2u − 1|` |
| 7 | `trap` | Trapezoid | `min(1, 4u, 4(1 − u))` |
| 8 | `expdec` | ExpDec | `exp(−6u)·min(1, 200u)` (2.5 % fade-in, click guard) |
| 9 | `expdecrev` | Exp Dec Rev | `exp(−6(1 − u))·min(1, 200(1 − u))` |

Precomputed tables of 1025 points per shape (SxBase, linear interpolation). AMOUNT a and SKEW s (§6.9.3) reshape them.

Changed semantics of existing sources: `mpex`/`mpey`/`mpez` (29–31) now carry MPE values (§6.17): X from CC10 (or from the per-note bend with the preference "MPE Pitch Bend (also) maps to Expr X"), Y from CC74, Z from channel pressure; `mpey` is reported bipolar when the app preference `mpeYBi` is on (`ctx.mpeYBi` → CP `srcBi` override, §4.1). `chaos1`/`chaos2` (17/18) stay; the S2 source menu lists them under "S1" only when `s1 = 1` or a row uses them. `noise` (19) keeps its Phase-1 meaning (pre-level raw noise, chunk start).

`NSRC = 62`. `SRC_MENU2` order: `env1–4, lfo1–10, lfo1y–lfo10y (only for Path-type LFOs), velo, note, wheel, at, pat, bend, mpex, mpey, mpez, relvel, rand1, rand2, randd, alt1, alt2, macro1–8, osca, oscb, oscc, sub, noise, filt1, filt2, actv, vidx, vmod1, vmod2, fixed, (S1: chaos1, chaos2)`. Drag tiles: Env 1–4, LFO 1–10, VELO, NOTE, WHEEL, Macro 1–8 (macro labels), and the module labels of OSC A/B/C, SUB, NOISE, FILTER 1/2 (dragging a module label onto a control = that audio source, inv B18).

### 3.11 Destinations v2 (`DESTS` 248 → 529)

#### 3.11.1 Index spaces
- **Static index** `idx`: 0–147 synth v1, 148–247 FX v1 static block (unchanged rows, fixture `dests.json` stays valid as the prefix; generated from fx-core's `FX_ROWS_V1`, §11.2), **248–528 SYN2** (new synth-side destinations, this section; 524–528 appended in review round 2). `ND = 529`, `NS = NS1 = 148`, `NV1 = 248` (end of the v1 table), `NF1 = 100` (v1 FX block length), `NS2 = 281`.
- **Voice index** `vi` (the per-voice vector, `CP.base`, `eff`): `vi(idx) = idx` for idx < 148; −1 for 148–247; −1 for scope-`M` rows (`g.direct`, `g.bus1v`, `g.bus2v`, idx 503–505 — they live only in the FX context, their `CP.base` slots 403–405 stay 0 and no row ever writes them); `idx − 100` otherwise. `NSV = 429`.
- **FX-ctx index** (processor-level vector, §4.2): 0 `g.master`, 1 `g.bypass`, 2 `g.direct`, 3 `g.bus1v`, 4 `g.bus2v`, 5–7 reserved, 8 + k = flat FX parameter k of the current FX layout.
- **Dynamic FX destinations** `fx.<inst>.<pid>`: not in `DESTS`; resolved by `fxDest(id, fx)` (§3.16.4). The v1 static rows 149–247 describe the S1-variant parameters (§3.16.2). **With an FX state supplied, every `fx.*` id resolves only through `fxDest`**; the static rows are used only by `normalizeV1`, `migrate1`/`migrateFx1` and tests (e.g. `fx.dist.mode` is the static v1 row with 16 modes, and `fx.dist.mode` of an S2 instance resolves dynamically to 18 modes). `compileSerum` never uses `DEST_INDEX` for FX rows.
- **The Phase-1 test "idx ≥ NS ⇒ FX destination" is forbidden** (it is false for idx ≥ 248). Use `isFxId(id)` (`/^fx\./.test(id) || id === 'g.master' || id === 'g.bypass'`) and `vi(idx)`; `CP.base`/`eff` are indexed only through `vi()`. Mandatory migrations in M1a (owner in brackets): serum-core `FX_P_IDX`, `fxModded`/`compileFxCore` `NF = ND − NS` → `NF1` (C1, moved to fx-core); main.js locked-FX filter (`sxLoad`), `midiMapCC` FX branch, the `sxUni` safeguard (I; the safeguard is retired in favour of `qr.uniMax`, §14.3, which the DSP applies to every oscillator incl. `c.uni`); fx-ui `lockedIds` (U3); serum-ui `isFxIdx` (U1).
- `DIDX` (v2) maps constant names to **vi** (Phase-1 naming rule): e.g. `DIDX.C_LEVEL = 148`, `DIDX.A_W1 = 167`, `DIDX.F2_ON = 339`. `GIDX = {MASTER: 0, BYPASS: 1, DIRECT: 2, BUS1V: 3, BUS2V: 4}`.

#### 3.11.2 Normative builder (append after the Phase-1 builder; `add(id, label, law, lo, hi, def, fmt, scope, ctr, group)`)

```js
// Block C — OSC C base (idx 248–266): the 17 Phase-1 per-oscillator rows with k='c', K='C', then:
for (const r of OSC_ROWS_V1) add(r.id('c'), r.label('C'), r.law, r.lo, r.hi, r.def, r.fmt, r.scope, r.ctr, 'Osc C');
add('c.on', 'Osc C On', 'bool', 0, 1, 0, 'bool', 'V', 0, 'Osc C');
add('c.track', 'Osc C Pitch Track', 'bool', 0, 1, 1, 'bool', 'V', 0, 'Osc C');
// Block O2 — per oscillator a, b, c (51 rows each; idx 267–317 a, 318–368 b, 369–419 c)
for (const [k, K] of [['a','A'], ['b','B'], ['c','C']]) {
  const g = `Osc ${K}`, gs = `Osc ${K} Sample`, gg = `Osc ${K} Granular`, gp = `Osc ${K} Spectral`, gm = `Osc ${K} Multisample`;
  add(`${k}.w1`, `Osc ${K} Warp 1 Mode`, 'enum', 0, 81, 0, 'enum:WARPS2', 'V', 0, g);
  add(`${k}.w2`, `Osc ${K} Warp 2 Mode`, 'enum', 0, 81, 0, 'enum:WARPS2', 'V', 0, g);
  add(`${k}.warp2`, `Osc ${K} Warp 2`, 'lin', 0, 1, 0, 'pct', 'V', 0, g);
  add(`${k}.wvar1`, `Osc ${K} Warp 1 Var`, 'lin', 0, 1, 0, 'pct', 'V', 0, g);
  add(`${k}.wvar2`, `Osc ${K} Warp 2 Var`, 'lin', 0, 1, 0, 'pct', 'V', 0, g);
  add(`${k}.uniwarp2`, `Osc ${K} Uni Warp 2`, 'lin', -1, 1, 0.5, 'bip100', 'V', 1, g);
  add(`${k}.ratio`, `Osc ${K} Ratio`, 'exp', 0.0625, 16, 0.5, 'ratio2', 'V', 0, g);
  add(`${k}.hz`, `Osc ${K} Hz Offset`, 'lin', -50, 50, 0.5, 'hzoff', 'V', 1, g);
  add(`${k}.sstart`, `Osc ${K} Sample Start`, 'lin', 0, 1, 0, 'smpPos', 'V*', 0, gs);
  add(`${k}.send`, `Osc ${K} Sample End`, 'lin', 0, 1, 1, 'smpPos', 'V', 0, gs);
  add(`${k}.lstart`, `Osc ${K} Loop Start`, 'lin', 0, 1, 0, 'smpPos', 'V', 0, gs);
  add(`${k}.lend`, `Osc ${K} Loop End`, 'lin', 0, 1, 1, 'smpPos', 'V', 0, gs);
  add(`${k}.xfade`, `Osc ${K} Loop X-Fade`, 'lin', 0, 1, 0, 'pct', 'V', 0, gs);
  add(`${k}.scan`, `Osc ${K} Scan`, 'lin', -1, 1, 0.75, 'scan', 'V', 1, gs);
  add(`${k}.scanrev`, `Osc ${K} Scan Reverse`, 'bool', 0, 1, 0, 'bool', 'V', 0, gs);
  add(`${k}.pos`, `Osc ${K} Position`, 'lin', 0, 1, 0, 'smpPos', 'V', 0, gs);
  add(`${k}.slice`, `Osc ${K} Single Slice`, 'int', 0, 255, 0, 'slice', 'V*', 0, gs);
  add(`${k}.gy`, `Osc ${K} X|Y Y`, 'lin', 0, 1, 0.5, 'pct', 'V', 0, gg);
  add(`${k}.dens`, `Osc ${K} Density`, 'lin', 0, 1, 0.6, 'dens', 'V', 0, gg);
  add(`${k}.glen`, `Osc ${K} Grain Length`, 'lin', 0, 1, 0.5, 'glen', 'V', 0, gg);
  add(`${k}.wamt`, `Osc ${K} Window Amount`, 'lin', 0, 1, 1, 'pct', 'V', 0, gg);
  add(`${k}.wskew`, `Osc ${K} Window Skew`, 'lin', -1, 1, 0.5, 'bip100', 'V', 1, gg);
  for (const [id, lb, hi, fmt] of [['roff','Rand Offset',1,'pct'], ['rdir','Rand Dir',1,'pct'], ['rpitch','Rand Pitch',12,'st2'],
    ['rlen','Rand Length',1,'pct'], ['rpan','Rand Pan',1,'pct'], ['rlvl','Rand Level',1,'pct'], ['rwamt','Rand Window',1,'pct'],
    ['rwskew','Rand Skew',1,'pct'], ['rwarp','Rand Warp',1,'pct'], ['rwarp2','Rand Warp 2',1,'pct']])
    add(`${k}.${id}`, `Osc ${K} ${lb}`, 'lin', 0, hi, 0, fmt, 'V', 0, gg);
  add(`${k}.grev`, `Osc ${K} Reverse Grains`, 'bool', 0, 1, 0, 'bool', 'V', 0, gg);
  add(`${k}.flo`, `Osc ${K} Freq Lo`, 'exp', MIDI0, 22050, 0, 'hz', 'V', 0, gp);
  add(`${k}.fhi`, `Osc ${K} Freq Hi`, 'exp', MIDI0, 22050, 1, 'hz', 'V', 0, gp);
  add(`${k}.cut`, `Osc ${K} Spectral Cut`, 'lin', -1, 1, 0.5, 'bip100', 'V', 1, gp);
  add(`${k}.smix`, `Osc ${K} Spectral Mix`, 'lin', 0, 1, 1, 'pct', 'V', 0, gp);
  for (const [id, lb, law, hi, def, fmt] of [['mdly','MS Delay','pow5',32,0,'time'], ['matt','MS Attack','pow5',32,0.11,'time'],
    ['mhold','MS Hold','pow5',32,0,'time'], ['mdec','MS Decay','pow5',32,0.5,'time'], ['msus','MS Sustain','lin',1,1,'pct'],
    ['mrel','MS Release','pow5',32,0.215,'time']])
    add(`${k}.${id}`, `Osc ${K} ${lb}`, law, 0, hi, def, fmt, 'V*', 0, gm);
  add(`${k}.mvel`, `Osc ${K} Vel Track`, 'lin', 0, 1, 1, 'pct', 'V*', 0, gm);

  add(`${k}.bus2`, `Osc ${K} Bus 2`, 'pow2', 0, 1, 0, 'pct', 'V', 0, g);
}

  add(`${k}.lenrate`, `Osc ${K} Scan BPM Rate`, 'enum', 0, 14, 5 / 14, 'enum:LEN_LADDER', 'V', 0, `Osc ${K} Sample`);

```
(`arp.rrate` default 0.4 = index 2 of 5 → "1 bar". `k.lenrate` default 5/14 = "1 bar"; scope `V` like `k.scan`, whose role it takes while Length to BPM is on.) `tests/fixtures/dests2.json` is generated from this builder by `tests/fixtures/gen2.mjs` (same format as `dests.json`) and copied verbatim; a test checks `DESTS` against it.

#### 3.11.3 Scopes, contexts, note-on destinations, virtual destinations
- Scope column semantics are Phase-1's (`V`, `V*`, `V*M`, `G`, `M`). Context bits: `V`,`V*` → 1; `V*M` → 3; `G` → 2; `M` → 4 (FX context).
- **Note-on sampled** (`mOn`, Phase-1 §6.1): Phase-1's eight + `c.uni`, `c.phase`, `c.rand`, `s.phase`, and for k ∈ a, b, c: `k.sstart`, `k.slice`, `k.mvel`, `k.mrand`, `k.timbre`, `k.ustart`, `k.uspan`.
- **Virtual** destinations (never stored in `s.p`; `normalizeSerum` drops them; `compileSerum` writes the base): `m1…m8` (base `macros[m].v`), `arp.rate … arp.wrange` (base 0 in CP; the sequencer adds the playing slot's `n.*`, §7.3), `clip.trans`, `clip.rate`, `clip.offset` (likewise). Locks, MIDI learn and drag-and-drop work on them as on any destination.
- `MATRIX_ONLY` unchanged (Phase-1 list). `m1…m8` are reachable by dragging a macro tile onto another macro knob.
- `modded` and the FX/mono contexts follow Phase-1 §4.1/§5.5 with the vi mapping.

#### 3.11.4 Defaults of new states (`DEF2`, applied by `defaultSerum()` on top of the destination defaults)
`f.routeB = f.routeN = f.routeS = 1` (v1 destination defaults are 0; Phase-1 rows stay unchanged). Nothing else differs.

### 3.12 Envelopes and LFOs (delta)

| Constant | Values |
|---|---|
| `N_ENV` | 4 (`ENV_K`, curve law Phase 1) |
| `ENV_LADDER` (BPM units, §6.14) | index 0 = 0 s, 1–15 = the 15 straight `LFO_LADDER` entries **reversed** (`fast` … `32 bar`), i.e. beats `[0, 1/128, 1/64, 1/32, 1/16, 1/8, 1/4, 1/2, 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128]` |
| `N_LFO` | 10 |
| `LFO_MODES2` | 0 `retrig` (RETRIG) · 1 `env` (ENVELOPE) · 2 `free` (FREE) — same numeric codes as Phase-1 `lfoMode` 0 trig / 1 env / 2 off |
| `LFO_TYPES` | 0 `normal` · 1 `path` · 2 `lorenz` (Chaos: Lorenz) · 3 `rossler` (Chaos: Rossler) · 4 `sh` (S&H) |
| `LFO_DIRS` | 0 `fwd` Forward · 1 `rev` Reverse · 2 `pp` Ping Pong |
| `LFO_TOOLS` | `point`, `flat`, `rampUp`, `rampDown` (graph drawing tools, inv B16) |

### 3.13 Matrix (delta)
`N_MATRIX = 64`. `MC_K = 6` (curve and aux curve). `CRV_N = 128` (editable-curve table resolution). `SLEW_T(n) = 4·n³` s.

### 3.14 Tuning and MPE constants
`TUN_KINDS = ['none', 'scl', 'tun']`. `MPE_BEND_DEF = 48`, range 1–96. `A4_RANGE = [400, 480]`.

#### 3.15.1 `ARP_SHAPES` (inv B22 internal list)
0 Played · 1 Pattern · 2 Chord · 3 Converge · 4 Diverge · 5 Conv&Div · 6 Down · 7 Up · 8 UpDown · 9 DownUp · 10 Up&Down · 11 Down&Up · 12 Thumb Up · 13 Thumb UD · 14 Rand · 15 Rand Once · 16 Rand Drift · 17 Rand NoDup. Sequences in §7.4.1.

#### 3.15.2 `RANGE_SHAPES` (transpose range shape)
0 Up · 1 Down · 2 Up/Down · 3 Down/Up · 4 Up+Down · 5 Down+Up · 6 Thumb Up · 7 Thumb UD · 8 Pinky Up · 9 Pinky UD · 10 Converge · 11 Diverge · 12 Con+Diverge · 13 Chord · 14 Random · 15 Rnd.NoDup · 16 Rnd.Drift · 17 Rnd.Once. Sequences in §7.4.2.

#### 3.15.3 Pattern / clip modes
`ARP_PMODES` 0 Normal · 1 Reverse · 2 Pendulum · 3 Random · 4 Rand Start · 5 Rand End · 6 One Shot · 7 Static. `STEP_MODES` 0 Normal · 1 New Only · 2 Chord · 3 Chord (new). `WRAP_MODES` 0 Wrap · 1 Phantom · 2 Clamp. `CLIP_MODES` 0 Normal · 1 Reverse · 2 Pendulum · 3 One Shot · 4 Random · 5 Rand.No Dup · 6 Rand.Start · 7 Rand.End · 8 Static. `KB_SPAN` 0 Off · 1 Mono · 2 Poly · 3 Offset. `CLIP_TRIG` 0 MONO · 1 POLY. `MIDI_OUT` 0 Off · 1 Clip Player · 2 On.

#### 3.15.4 Ladders (beats; 1 bar = 4 beats)
| Constant | Entries |
|---|---|
| `ARP_LADDER` (straight) | `4 bar` 16 · `2 bar` 8 · `1 bar` 4 · `1/2` 2 · `1/4` 1 · `1/8` 0.5 · `1/16` 0.25 · `1/32` 0.125 · `1/64` 0.0625; `arpLadder(dot, trip)` built like Phase-1 `lfoLadder` (dot: entries 0–7 × 1.5 `.`; trip: × 2/3 `T`), sorted by beats descending; lengths 9/17/17/25 |
| `GRID_LADDER` | `1/4` · `1/8` · `1/16` · `1/32` · `1/64` · `1/8T` · `1/16T` (editor grids, clip offset quantise) |
| `TIME_LADDER` | `1/16` 0.25 · `1/8` 0.5 · `1/4` 1 · `1/2` 2 · `1 bar` 4 · `2 bar` 8 · `4 bar` 16 |
| `RRATE_LADDER` | `1/4` · `1/2` · `1 bar` · `2 bar` · `4 bar` · `8 bar` |
| `CLIP_RATES` | ×1/4 · ×1/3 · ×1/2 · ×2/3 · ×3/4 · ×1 · ×4/3 · ×3/2 · ×2 · ×3 · ×4 |
| `gridLadder(dot, trip)` (granular DENS/LENGTH BPM) | `ARP_LADDER` extended to `1/128` and `1/256`, dot/trip as above |

#### 3.15.5 `LAUNCH_QUANT`
0 Off · 1 1/32 · 2 1/16 · 3 1/8 · 4 1/4 · 5 1/2 · 6 1 bar · 7 2 bar · 8 4 bar.

#### 3.15.6 `LEN_LADDER` (Sample Length to BPM)
0 `1/16` 0.25 · 1 `1/8` 0.5 · 2 `1/4` 1 · 3 `1/2` 2 · 4 `3/4` 3 · 5 `1 bar` 4 · 6 `1.5 bar` 6 · 7 `2 bar` 8 · 8 `3 bar` 12 · 9 `4 bar` 16 · 10 `6 bar` 24 · 11 `8 bar` 32 · 12 `12 bar` 48 · 13 `16 bar` 64 · 14 `32 bar` 128.

#### 3.15.7 `SCALES` (`{id, name, steps}`; steps = semitone set from the key)
| # | name | steps | # | name | steps |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | Major | 0 2 4 5 7 9 11 | 21 | Neapolitan Minor | 0 1 3 5 7 8 11 |
| 1 | Natural Minor | 0 2 3 5 7 8 10 | 22 | Persian | 0 1 4 5 6 8 11 |
| 2 | Harmonic Minor | 0 2 3 5 7 8 11 | 23 | Phrygian Dominant | 0 1 4 5 7 8 10 |
| 3 | Melodic Minor | 0 2 3 5 7 9 11 | 24 | Lydian Dominant | 0 2 4 6 7 9 10 |
| 4 | Dorian | 0 2 3 5 7 9 10 | 25 | Altered | 0 1 3 4 6 8 10 |
| 5 | Phrygian | 0 1 3 5 7 8 10 | 26 | Bebop Major | 0 2 4 5 7 8 9 11 |
| 6 | Lydian | 0 2 4 6 7 9 11 | 27 | Bebop Dominant | 0 2 4 5 7 9 10 11 |
| 7 | Mixolydian | 0 2 4 5 7 9 10 | 28 | In | 0 1 5 7 8 |
| 8 | Locrian | 0 1 3 5 6 8 10 | 29 | Hirajoshi | 0 2 3 7 8 |
| 9 | Major Pentatonic | 0 2 4 7 9 | 30 | Iwato | 0 1 5 6 10 |
| 10 | Minor Pentatonic | 0 3 5 7 10 | 31 | Kumoi | 0 2 3 7 9 |
| 11 | Blues | 0 3 5 6 7 10 | 32 | Pelog | 0 1 3 7 8 |
| 12 | Major Blues | 0 2 3 4 7 9 | 33 | Egyptian | 0 2 5 7 10 |
| 13 | Whole Tone | 0 2 4 6 8 10 | 34 | Prometheus | 0 2 4 6 9 10 |
| 14 | Diminished (W-H) | 0 2 3 5 6 8 9 11 | 35 | Enigmatic | 0 1 4 6 8 10 11 |
| 15 | Diminished (H-W) | 0 1 3 4 6 7 9 10 | 36 | Arabian | 0 2 4 5 6 8 10 |
| 16 | Augmented | 0 3 4 7 8 11 | 37 | Spanish 8-Tone | 0 1 3 4 5 6 8 10 |
| 17 | Chromatic | 0–11 | 38 | Hungarian Major | 0 3 4 6 7 9 10 |
| 18 | Hungarian Minor | 0 2 3 6 7 8 11 | 39 | Yo | 0 2 5 7 9 |
| 19 | Double Harmonic | 0 1 4 5 7 8 11 | 40 | Major Hexatonic | 0 2 4 5 7 9 |
| 20 | Neapolitan Major | 0 1 3 5 7 9 11 | 41 | Minor Hexatonic | 0 2 3 5 7 10 |

`quantize(note, key, scale)`: key = −1 → unchanged. Else pitch class `pc = (note − key) mod 12`; the nearest member of `steps` by circular distance; ties → the lower one; return `note + (member − pc)` with the wrap correction (±12) that gives the smallest |Δ|.

### 3.16 FX v2 catalogue (`fx-core.js`; embedded identically in `fx-dsp.js`/`fx-mods2.js`)

#### 3.16.1 `FXTYPES` (index = S2 type id, inv B14.1)
| idx | id | label | variants | mix kind | DSP owner |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `dist` | Distortion | 1, 2 | sin² | fx-dsp (+ new shapers in fx-mods2) |
| 1 | `flg` | Flanger | 1 | sin² | fx-dsp |
| 2 | `phs` | Phaser | 1, 2 | sin² | fx-dsp |
| 3 | `cho` | Chorus | 1, 2 | sin² | fx-dsp |
| 4 | `dly` | Delay | 1, 2 | sin² | fx-dsp |
| 5 | `cmp` | Compressor | 1, 2 | sin² | fx-dsp |
| 6 | `rev` | Reverb | 1, 2 | sin² | fx-dsp (Plate, Hall) + fx-mods2 (Vintage, Nitrous, Basin) |
| 7 | `eq` | Equalizer | 1, 2 | none | fx-dsp |
| 8 | `fil` | Filter | 1, 2 | linear | fx-dsp (SFilter) |
| 9 | `hyper` | Hyper/Dimension | 1 | additive (Phase 1) | fx-dsp |
| 10 | `bode` | Bode | 2 | sin² | fx-mods2 |
| 11 | `conv` | Convolve | 2 | sin² | fx-mods2 |
| 12 | `util` | Utility | 2 | linear | fx-mods2 |
| 13 | `split2` | Splitter L/H | 2 | — (branches) | fx-dsp (+ fx-mods2 crossovers) |
| 14 | `split3` | Splitter L/M/H | 2 | — | |
| 15 | `splitms` | Splitter M/S | 2 | — | |

New instances get the highest variant. `addable(type)` lists every type for MAIN/BUS racks, and the 13 effects (no splitters) inside a branch.

#### 3.16.2 Parameter tables
Each `(type, variant)` has an ordered parameter table `FXP_TAB[type][v] = [{pid, label, law, lo, hi, def, fmt, ctr}]`; the flat-layout order (§4.2) is the table order.

**S1 variants** (`v = 1`) are exactly the Phase-1 FX destination rows of that module, in fx-idx order, with `pid` = the id suffix after `fx.<m>.` (Hyper's Dimension rows `fx.dim.size/mix/level` become pids `dsize`, `dmix`, `dlevel`):

| type | S1 pids in order |
|---|---|
| `hyper` | on, rate, detune, unison, mix, level, dsize, dmix, dlevel, retrig |
| `dist` | on, drive, lbh, freq, q, mix, level, mode (0–15), filt |
| `flg` | on, rate, depth, feed, phase, mix, level, bpm |
| `phs` | on, rate, depth, freq, feed, phase, mix, level, bpm |
| `cho` | on, rate, dly1, dly2, depth, feed, lpf, mix, level, bpm |
| `dly` | on, feed, timeL, timeR, offL, offR, freq, q, mix, level, bpm, link, mode |
| `cmp` | on, thr, ratio, att, rel, gain, mbL, mbM, mbH, mix, level, mb |
| `rev` | on, size, t, locut, c, hicut, w, mix, level (structured `sub.type` 0 Plate / 1 Hall) |
| `eq` | on, freqL, qL, gainL, freqH, qH, gainH, typeL, typeH |
| `fil` | on, cutoff, res, drive, var, pan, mix, level, type (0–95) |

**S2 variants** (`v = 2`): the S1 table (where one exists) followed by the new rows below; `on` default **1** for instances created in v2 (the S1 row default 0 is kept for migrated instances). New types list all rows.

| type | additional / complete rows (pid, label, law, lo, hi, def n, fmt) |
|---|---|
| `dist` 2 | **mode** replaced by `mode` enum 0–17 def 0 (`enum:DIST_MODES2`) at the same position; + `kt` Key Track bool 0; + `stages` Stages int 1–16 def 3/15 (= 4) `int` |
| `phs` 2 | + `poles` Poles int 1–18 def 3/17 (= 4) `int`; + `depth2` Depth 2 lin 0–1 def 0 `pct` |
| `cho` 2 | + `hp` HPF bool 0 (label of the LPF knob becomes HPF) |
| `dly` 2 | + `hq` High Quality bool def 1 |
| `cmp` 2 | + `xlo` X-Low exp 20–1000 def 0.4751 (128.3 Hz) `hz`; `xhi` X-High exp 500–16000 def 0.4644 (2.5 kHz) `hz`; `below` Below ratio 1–32 def 0 (1:1) `ratio`; `gL`, `gM`, `gH` Low/Mid/High Gain lin −24…24 def 0.5 `dbv` |
| `rev` 2 | complete table (the S1 rows are **not** used): `on` bool 1 · `type` enum 0–4 def 0 (`REV_TYPES2`) · `size` lin 0–1 0.5 `pct` · `pre` Pre-Delay pow3 0–2500 ms 0 `ms` · `prebpm` bool 0 · `prelad` enum 0–11 def 7/11 (`DLY_LADDER`, 1/4) · `locut` lin 0–1 0 `pct` · `hicut` lin 0–1 0.3 `pct` · `damp` lin 0–1 0.5 `pct` · `width` lin 0–1 1 `pct` · `decay` lin 0–1 0.3 `pct` · `spinr` lin 0–1 0.25 `pct` · `spind` lin 0–1 0.2 `pct` · `ersize` lin 0–1 0.5 `pct` · `diffa` lin 0–1 0.7 `pct` · `diffb` lin 0–1 0.5 `pct` · `chr` Chorus Rate lin 0–1 0.2 `pct` · `chd` Chorus Depth lin 0–1 0.2 `pct` · `feed` lin 0–1 0.6 `pct` · `diff` lin 0–1 0.7 `pct` · `nmode` enum 0–4 0 (`NITROUS_MODES`) · `mix` lin 0–1 0.3 `pct` · `level` pow2 0–4 0.5 `dbtrim` (23 rows) |
| `eq` 2 | + `level` pow2 0–4 0.5 `dbtrim` |
| `fil` 2 | `type` enum 0–107 def 0 at the same position; + `kt` Key Track bool 0; + `clean` Clean Mode bool 0 |
| `bode` 2 | `on` bool 1 · `mono` Mono Input bool 0 · `shift` lin −1…1 0.5 `bip100` (C) · `range` exp 0.1–5000 Hz 0.6384 (100 Hz) `hz` · `dir` lin −1…1 1 `bip100` (C) · `width` lin 0–1 1 `pct` · `delay` pow2 0–2000 ms 0 `ms` · `bpm` bool 0 · `dlad` enum 0–11 7/11 · `feed` lin 0–1 0 `pct` · `bal` lin −1…1 0.5 `bip100` (C) · `blur` lin 0–1 0 `pct` · `retrig` bool 0 · `mix` lin 0–1 1 `pct` · `level` pow2 0–4 0.5 `dbtrim` (15 rows) |
| `conv` 2 | `on` bool 1 · `size`ⁿ exp 0.1–10 0.5 (100 %) `pct1` · `tone`ⁿ lin −1…1 0.5 `bip100` (C) · `minph`ⁿ bool 0 · `pre` pow2 0–1000 ms 0 `ms` · `prebpm` bool 0 · `prelad` enum 0–11 7/11 · `att`ⁿ lin 0–1 0 `pct` · `dec`ⁿ lin 0–1 1 `pct` · `damp`ⁿ lin 0–1 0 `pct` · `irgain` lin −48…12 dB 0.5 (−18 dB) `dbv` · `mix` lin 0–1 0.5 `pct` · `level` pow2 0–4 0.5 `dbtrim` (13 rows). ⁿ = `nomod: true` (deviation 20): not offered as matrix/drag destinations, `fxDest` reports `nomod`, `compileSerum` treats rows targeting them as inactive (red in the matrix); their base values select the shaped IR asset (§8.4) |
| `util` 2 | `on` bool 1 · `invL` bool 0 · `invR` bool 0 · `lpf` exp 50–20000 1 `hz` (20 kHz = off) · `hpf` exp 1–400 0 `hz` (1 Hz = off) · `mbass` bool 0 · `mfreq` exp 20–400 0.5021 (90 Hz) `hz` · `width` lin 0–8 0.125 (100 %) `pct1` · `pan` lin −1…1 0.5 `pan` (C) · `mix` lin 0–1 1 `pct` · `level` pow2 0–4 0.5 `dbtrim` (11 rows) |
| `split2` 2 | `on` bool 1 · `freq` exp 30–5800 0.4374 (300 Hz) `hz` · `level` pow2 0–4 0.5 `dbtrim` |
| `split3` 2 | `on` bool 1 · `freq1` exp 30–5800 0.3604 (200 Hz) · `freq2` exp 30–5800 0.7978 (2 kHz) · `level` |
| `splitms` 2 | `on` bool 1 · `level` |

Enumerations: `DIST_MODES2` = Phase-1 16 + 16 Overdrive + 17 Soft Sat. (S2 menu order, inv B14.2). `REV_TYPES2` 0 Plate · 1 Hall · 2 Vintage · 3 Nitrous · 4 Basin. `NITROUS_MODES` 0 Space · 1 Marble · 2 Rectangle · 3 Hexagon · 4 Box. `REV_LABELS2[type]` = the controls shown per type (inv B14.4): Plate {locut, hicut, size, pre, damp, width}; Hall {locut, hicut, size, pre, decay, spinr, spind}; Vintage {locut, hicut, size, pre, ersize, decay, damp, diffa, diffb, chr, chd}; Nitrous {locut, hicut, size, pre, feed, diff, nmode, chr, chd}; Basin {locut, hicut, size, pre, feed, chr, chd}.

#### 3.16.3 Structured instance data (`inst.sub`)
| type | `sub` |
|---|---|
| `dist` | `{xa, xb}` X-Shaper curves (Phase 1) |
| `cho` | `{mono}` |
| `rev` v1 | `{type: 0 Plate | 1 Hall}` |
| `conv` | `{ir: asset id ('fir:…' / 'ir:…') or ''}` (default `fir:Medium/Chamber`) |
| others | `{}` |

#### 3.16.4 Dynamic destinations
`fx.<inst>.<pid>` for every parameter of every instance (all racks and branches). `fxDest(id, fx) → {inst, type, v, pid, row, flat}` or null; `destLabel` = `<instance label> <param label>` where instance label = type label + (` 2`, ` 3` … for later instances in list order) + (` (Bus 1)` / ` (Bus 2)` outside MAIN). The destination menu groups FX parameters by rack and instance. `fmtParam`, `parseParam`, `lawValue`, `lawNorm` accept dynamic ids when given `ctx.fx`.

---

## 4. Compiled formats v2

### 4.1 `compileSerum(s, ctx) → patch` (owner C1)
