`new SxSeq.Seq(proc)` (allocation in the constructor; `apply(sqp, seqT)` copies data into preallocated arrays: ≤ 12 slots × 512 events, ≤ 4096 clip notes, 32 held notes, 128 pending note-offs); `next(F) → frames to the next event (≥ 1, 1e9 when none)`; `fire(F)` emits every event due at F (note-offs before note-ons) through `proc.sxNote(on, n, vel, ch, x, y, z, gen)`; `input(on, n, vel, ch, F)`; `cmd(name, arg)` (`arpLaunch` j, `arpStop` j, `clipLaunch` j, `clipStop` j, `clipStopAll`, `latch` 0/1 (§7.4.5)); `merge(part)` (part-keyed table delivery, §4.4); `transport(playing)`; `panic()`; `snapshot(out Float64Array(16))`. Allocation-free after `apply`. The processor field holding it is **`sxSeq`** (the Phase-1 field `sq` and message `'seq'` belong to the TB-303 sequencer and keep their meaning).

### 7.8 Transport, panic, determinism
`transport(true)` (play with seek): sequencer PRNG ← seed, latch kept, running arps restart at the next quant boundary of the song grid, clips with RETRIG off resync by §7.5.2, others restart at the next quant. `transport(false)`: generated notes are released. Panic: everything stops, held/latched notes cleared. A song rendered from tick T is identical live and offline (Phase-1 §10.7f extended to the sequencer).

### 7.9 MIDI OUT (deviation 2)
With `midiOut ≠ 0` (ARP or CLIP pane) the generated notes of the chosen tap (Clip Player = clip output before the arp; On = final output) are delivered **inside the audio thread**: all processors of one AudioContext share one `AudioWorkletGlobalScope`, and engine.js keeps a module-level registry `PROCS = new Map()` (trackId → processor). Every `SynthProcessor` registers itself under `processorOptions.trackId` (main.js passes the track id; preview/render-farm nodes pass none and are not registered) and unregisters on `{t: 'bye'}` (sent by main.js before disconnecting a node) or when `process` returns false. The panel's MIDI OUT field selects a **target track** (`s.arp.outTrack` / `s.clip.outTrack` = track id or ''; compiled into `seq.outIds`, §4.4). For each tapped event the source sequencer calls `PROCS.get(outTrack)?.extNote(F + 128, on, n, vel)` with F = the event's frame: the target's `extNote` writes into a preallocated ring of 256 events (overflow drops the oldest note-on, never a note-off), and the target's `process` treats ring entries like song events (span splitting, `noteOn`/`noteOff` through its own sequencer chain, §7.1). The constant one-quantum delay (128 frames) makes delivery independent of the order in which the context processes the nodes. The same code runs in an `OfflineAudioContext`, so a bounce of the whole project reproduces the live result sample-accurately; a single-track render (resample, preview, render farm) has no target and drops the events. Events are not recorded into the target's song data. A target that is itself a MIDI OUT source does not forward `extNote` events again (no chains, no loops).

### 7.10 Snapshot `seq` (Float64Array(16))
`[arp playing slot (−1), arp step, arp pass, held count, clip playing mask (bits 0–11), selected clip playhead (sixteenths, −1), most recent clip index, its playhead, timebase beat, transport playing, latch, 0…]`.

---

## 8. FX v2 (fx-dsp + fx-mods2; owner F1; inv B14)

### 8.1 Rack engine
- `FXRack` keeps one module object per **instance id** (with its type and variant). `set(fxp, fxt)` (allocation allowed) builds the processing graph from FXP: existing objects whose id/type/variant match are kept with their state (no glitch for untouched instances); new instances are allocated when `iAlloc`; removed instances are dropped after a 5 ms fade of their output (their tail is cut).
- Per instance, the Phase-1 machinery applies unchanged: base smoothing per parameter (τ = 10 ms × smooth per process call), modulation offsets linear across the call, eff re-evaluated every 16-sample block, enable state machine with 5 ms crossfades, MIX/LEVEL laws by mix kind, phase memory and re-seeding (`iPh`, seed, `transport()`), ducked re-seeds, `idle()`, lazy allocation, denormal flushing.
- **Bit-exactness**: a FXP whose instances are S1 variants in Phase-1 module order (the result of migrating any v1 FX state) MUST produce output bit-identical to the Phase-1 `FXRack` fed the v1 FXP of the same state, for every input (§15.1).
- `process(L, R, n, dL?, dR?, b1L?, b1R?, b2L?, b2R?)`: bus racks, routing and the output stage per §1. With no instance enabled, no bus/direct input, gains at unity and bypass off, `process` returns after O(1) work and leaves L/R bit-identical (Phase-1 bypass-cheap rule).
- Rack bypass (`rByp`): the rack's input passes unchanged (5 ms crossfade); while bypassed its instances are not processed (state frozen) and resume from that state when the bypass ends.
- `setNote(pitch)` (engine, once per span): MIDI-float pitch used by instances with Key Track (Distortion FREQ, Filter CUTOFF): factor `2^((pitch − 60)/12)`. Serum: the recent voice's gliding pitch; other models: the last note-on; none: 60.
- `trigger()` (every note-on): Hyper RETRIG (Phase 1) and Bode RETRIG (shifter oscillators to `iPh`) for every instance with the flag on.
- `gr() → Float32Array(4·48)` (per instance, Phase-1 layout per compressor), `phases() → Float64Array(48)` (`[i]` = the LFO phase of FXP instance i in `fxLayout` order — Hyper, Flanger L, Phaser L, Chorus, Bode — and `NaN` for instances without an LFO; main.js copies non-NaN entries into `inst.ph` by instance id), `latency` (max look-ahead of Limit-mode compressors on the main path), `bytes()`.

### 8.2 Changed modules (S2 variants; S1 variants are Phase 1 exactly)
| Module | S2 behaviour |
|---|---|
| Distortion | modes 0–15 Phase 1; **16 Overdrive**: `u₀ = G·x`, `G = 0.5·10^d`; for j = 1…stages: `u_j = LP₉ₖ(tanh(1.8·u_{j−1})/tanh(1.8))` (one-pole LP at 9 kHz per stage, at the 2× shaper rate), `y = 0.9·u_stages`; **17 Soft Sat.**: `G = 0.5·10^(0.6d)`, `y = |u| < 1 ? 1.5u − 0.5u³ : sign(u)`; `kt`: filter F × key factor; X-Shaper curves per instance from FXT |
| Phaser | `poles` P first-order allpasses per channel, corners `f_j = f_sweep·2^(depth2·(2j/(P−1) − 1))` (P = 1: f_sweep); P = 6, depth2 = 0 ⇒ Phase 1 |
| Chorus | `hp`: the wet 2-pole filter is a high-pass at the LPF frequency |
| Delay | `hq` on: lines always at full rate (no half-rate above 96 kHz) and 4-point Hermite reads; off: Phase 1 |
| Compressor | crossovers at `xlo`/`xhi` (LR4 + low-band allpass compensation, coefficients per block); below-threshold ratio `below` (Phase 1 used RATIO); band output gains `gL/gM/gH` (dB) after each band's gain computer; Limit mode, look-ahead, makeup laws Phase 1 |
| Equalizer | `level` gain `(2n)²` after the bands |
| Filter | types 0–107, `kt` cutoff key tracking, `clean` Clean Mode (§6.13.3) |
| Reverb | §8.3 |

### 8.3 Reverb S2 (`type`; inv B14.4)
Common: pre-delay `pre` ms (BPM: `DLY_LADDER[prelad]` beats at bpm, ≤ 2.5 s); output HP `20·50^locut` Hz and LP `20000·0.05^hicut` Hz (100 % = all removed ⇒ the filters reach their extremes); `width` scales the wet side (Plate); MIX/LEVEL Phase 1.
| Type | Recipe (IMPL; delay lengths in samples at 48 kHz, scaled to the base rate) |
|---|---|
| Plate | Phase-1 Plate topology; tank decay set so that `RT60(size) = size < 0.1 ? 0.2 + 2·size : 0.4·30^min(1, (size − 0.1)/0.55)` s (inv B14.4 measurement); `damp` = Phase-1 damping law; wet × 2 (+6 dB, measured) |
| Hall | Phase-1 Hall with `T60 = 0.8 + 11.2·decay` (≥ 0.999 ⇒ infinite), SPIN rate `0.05 + 3·spinr²` Hz, depth `12·spind` samples, lengths × `(0.4 + 1.6·size)`, + pre-delay |
| Vintage | ER: 12 taps at `ersize·(5 + 6.25j)·(1 + 0.07·sin 2.3j)` ms, gains `0.8·0.88^j`, alternating L/R; input diffusion 4 allpasses `[142, 107, 379, 277]·(0.5 + size)`, g = `0.9·diffa`; tank: 4-line Hadamard FDN `[1447, 1979, 2311, 2741]·(0.5 + size)`, `T60 = 0.3 + 19.7·decay²` s, in-loop one-pole LP `20000·0.05^damp` Hz, in-loop allpass per line `[47, 59, 71, 83]·(0.5 + size)` g = `0.7·diffb`; CHORUS: line reads modulated at `0.05·100^chr` Hz, depth `12·chd` samples; L = lines 0+2 + ER(L), R = lines 1+3 + ER(R) |
| Nitrous | 8-line Householder FDN; MODE sets (× `0.4 + 1.6·size`): Space `[1123, 1439, 1777, 2063, 2399, 2803, 3121, 3517]`, Marble `[389, 433, 487, 523, 571, 619, 661, 709]`, Rectangle `[601, 613, 977, 991, 1607, 1621, 2593, 2609]`, Hexagon `[1009, 1013, 1019, 1021, 1031, 1033, 1039, 1049]`, Box `[211, 263, 307, 353, 401, 449, 503, 557]`; loop gain `0.99·feed`; 2 input allpasses and one in-loop allpass per line, g = `0.7·diff`; in-loop LP `20000·0.05^hicut`; CHORUS as Vintage |
| Basin | two parallel chains of 8 series allpasses (delays the primes 113, 163, 229, 317, 421, 557, 733, 967 and 131, 179, 251, 337, 449, 593, 787, 1031, × `(0.5 + size)`, g = 0.75) into a 4-line FDN `[2203, 2689, 3253, 3851]·(0.5 + size)` with loop gain `feed` (≥ 0.999 ⇒ 1 with `1.5·tanh(x/1.5)` in the loop), in-loop LP `6000·(1 − 0.8·hicut)` Hz, CHORUS depth `40·chd` samples |

### 8.4 New modules (fx-mods2)
| Module | Recipe (IMPL) |
|---|---|
| **Bode** | Hilbert pair: two cascades of four first-order allpasses with coefficients `a² ∈ {0.6923878, 0.9360654322959, 0.9882295226860, 0.9987488452737}` and `{0.4021921162426, 0.8561710882420, 0.9722909545651, 0.9952884791278}` (second path one sample later) → I, Q. Shift `f = v_shift·range` Hz (v_shift ∈ [−1,1]); quadrature oscillator θ += f/fs; `up = I cos θ − Q sin θ`, `down = I cos θ + Q sin θ`. DIR d: `L = d ≥ 0 ? up : (1 + d)·up − d·down`, `R = d ≤ 0 ? down : (1 − d)·down + d·up`. WIDTH w: `L' = L·(1+w)/2 + R·(1−w)/2`, `R'` mirrored. MONO INPUT: both shifters get `(L+R)/2`. Delay: stereo line (`delay` ms or BPM ladder), input `((1 − b)/2)·down + ((1 + b)/2)·up` per channel (b = BALANCE), output added to the wet; FEED: delay output × feed into the shifter input (spiralling shifts); BLUR: 4 allpasses (g 0.5) in the feedback path with LFO-modulated delays (0.3–1.7 Hz, depth `blur·4` ms). RETRIG: θ ← `2π·ph` at note-on |
| **Convolve** | **Shaping (off the audio thread)**: the chain SIZE time-stretch by `size` value (linear-interpolated resampling of the IR; length ≤ `convMax` s) → TONE tilt (tone < 0: one-pole LP at `20000·2^(8·tone)`, > 0: one-pole HP at `20·2^(8·tone)`) → ATTACK raised-cosine fade-in over `0.5·att·len` → DECAY keeps `dec·len` with a half-cosine fade over its last 30 % → DAMP: LR2 split at 2 kHz, high band × `exp(−6.9·damp·t/len)` → MIN PHASE: real-cepstrum minimum-phase conversion (FFT `nextPow2(2·len)`, in the worker) → IR GAIN is **not** baked (applied live) — runs in `smpwork` (op `irshape`, pure `smp.irShape`, §9.4.1), which also cuts the shaped IR into its partitions and computes their spectra (Float32), delivering the entry `<ir id>@<rate>~<shapeKey>` (§4.5). The six shaping parameters are `nomod` (deviation 20); a change of their base values (debounced 50 ms while dragging) makes the host build the new entry; when it arrives the instance crossfades old → new over 20 ms (one rebuild in flight per instance, newer targets replace the pending one; until then the old IR keeps playing). The worklet only adopts buffers (no FFT of the IR, no allocation beyond `set`). **Engine (zero latency for spans of 4–128 frames)**: an input FIFO of 128 frames; **head** = IR [0, 128) as a direct-form FIR (128 taps per sample and channel), so output is available for any span length; **middle** = IR [128, 8192) in 63 uniform partitions of 128 (FFT 256), the frequency-domain delay line advanced and multiplied each time 128 input frames complete; **tail** = IR [8192, len) in partitions of 4096 (FFT 8192) — a partition of size B starts at an offset ≥ 2B, so the FFT/IFFT work of each completed 4096-frame input block is spread evenly over the next 4096 frames (in 128-frame slices) and its result is due exactly when needed. Output = head + middle + tail, sample-aligned with the direct convolution (test ≤ −100 dB). Pre-delay line before the convolver; IR GAIN, MIX, LEVEL live. A missing IR entry ⇒ the instance passes its input (bypassed) |
| **Utility** | polarity per channel; LPF 2-pole TPT (bypassed at ≥ 19.9 kHz), HPF 2-pole TPT (bypassed at ≤ 1.01 Hz); MONO BASS: LR4 split at `mfreq`, low band summed to mono; WIDTH: side × width (0–8); PAN: Phase-1 equal-power balance; MIX linear; LEVEL |
| **Splitter L/H** | LR4 crossover at `freq`; band 0 → branch 0 instances, band 1 → branch 1 (each instance with its own enable/mix/level); a bypassed branch passes its band; sum × level |
| **Splitter L/M/H** | LR4 at `freq1` and `freq2` (freq2 ≥ 1.2·freq1 at runtime), low band through the freq2 allpass (phase compensation); three branches |
| **Splitter M/S** | branch 0 gets `(m, m)`, m = (L+R)/2; branch 1 gets `(s, −s)`, s = (L−R)/2; output `L = m₀ + s₀`, `R = m₁ + s₁` where (m₀, m₁) and (s₀, s₁) are the branch outputs' L/R (identity when the branches are empty) |

Splitter `on` off ⇒ the whole splitter (incl. branches) passes its input (5 ms crossfade).

### 8.5 FX rack budgets and memory
§14. Convolve and the S2 reverbs allocate on `iAlloc`; Plate/Hall S2 reuse the Phase-1 allocations. Per-instance budgets at base 192 kHz (normative, B12): S1 variants as Phase 1 (Delay: half-rate lines above 96 kHz, RULINGS); **S2 Delay** ≤ 4 MB using **Float32** lines (2 s × 192 kHz × 2 ch × 4 B = 3.1 MB with `hq` on); S2 reverbs ≤ 2 MB; Bode ≤ 1 MB; Convolve per §14.2. **Per track** the rack's `bytes()` is capped at 32 MB (Convolve partition spectra excluded, they count as assets): `set()` that would exceed the cap leaves the new instance unallocated and bypassed, and the host shows "FX memory limit reached — <instance> bypassed".

---

## 9. Sample and IR data pipeline (owner S1; main-thread integration I)

### 9.1 Asset ids
| Prefix | Kind | Origin |
|---|---|---|
| `fsmp:<Name>` | sample (factory) | §10.2 generators |
| `smp:<uuid>` | sample (user) | import, "Switch to Single Sample", resample |
| `fmsmp:<Name>` | multisample (factory) | §10.1 render farm |
| `msmp:<uuid>` | multisample (user) | SFZ import |
| `fir:<Folder>/<Name>` | impulse response (factory) | §10.3 |
| `ir:<uuid>` | impulse response (user) | Convolve import |

Worklet entry ids derived from them: playback `<sample id>` or `<sample id>~<opsKey>` (8-hex FNV-1a of the canonical JSON of the non-default **entry-changing** ops `trim` and `fade`; `norm`/`rev` are CP flags and never change the entry); a wavetable played as a sample `wtsmp:<table id>` (§9.4.8); spectral analysis `spec:<playback id>`; shaped IR `<ir id>@<rate>~<shapeKey>` (§4.3); multisample `<multi id>` (zones reference playback ids). Ids are opaque to the DSP.

### 9.2 Storage (`smpstore.js`; IndexedDB `synth-smp` v1)
| Store | Key | Records |
|---|---|---|
| `assets` (index `kind`) | `id` | sample `{id, kind: 'smp', name, folder ('' or `'Renders'`, §13.2.5), rate, ch, len, L: ArrayBuffer (Float32 LE), R: ArrayBuffer | null, root, loop: {start, end} | null, odf: ArrayBuffer (Float32, §9.4.5), peak, src: {file, type, bytes}, hash, created, modified}` · multisample `{id, kind: 'multi', name, zones: [{lo, hi, vlo, vhi, root, tune, gain, pan, smp: sample id, mode, ls, le, off, env, rr, vt}], src: {sfz ≤ 64 KB}, created, modified}` · IR `{id, kind: 'ir', name, rate, ch, len, L, R, hash, created, modified}` |
| `cache` | `key` | `gen:<asset id>@<GEN_VER>` → generated factory record (same shape); `spec:<playback id>@<SPEC_VER>` → `{N, hop, frames, mag, ph, trans}`; each with `bytes`, `lastUse` |

API mirrors `wtstore.js` (`openStore`, `list({kind})`, `get`, `put(obj, {id?, name})`, `rename`, `del`, `gc(keepIds)`, `memoryStore()`), plus `cacheGet(key)`, `cachePut(key, rec)`, `cacheTrim(maxBytes = 512 MB)` (least recently used first). Quota errors: notice "Storage full — remove unused samples", the operation fails cleanly.

### 9.3 Import (main thread; `smp.js` pure helpers + `host.smp` in `assets.js`)
1. **Sniff** by magic bytes: `RIFF…WAVE`, `FORM…AIFF|AIFC`, `fLaC`, `OggS`, `ID3`/MPEG sync, `ftyp` (M4A/AAC), `PK\3\4` (ZIP), PNG (wavetable paths only), else text containing `<region>`/`<group>`/`<control>`/`<global>` (SFZ).
2. **Decode**: WAV with `wt.readWav`; AIFF/AIFC (`NONE`, `sowt`, `fl32`, `fl64`, 8–32-bit) with `smp.readAiff`; any other audio with `new OfflineAudioContext(2, 1, 48000).decodeAudioData(copy)` (canonical 48 kHz). Failure ⇒ notice "Unsupported audio file", nothing stored.
3. **Canonicalise**: > 2 channels → first two; rate > 96 kHz → 96 kHz (`smp.resample`, windowed sinc, 32 taps per side, Kaiser β 8); length caps: sample 60 s, multisample zone 30 s, IR 10 s (longer: truncated + notice).
4. **Root**: filename suffix `[ _]<Note><Octave>` before the extension (C, C#, Db … B; octave −2…8; **C3 = MIDI 60**, Serum convention for file names only — the UI keeps the app's naming C4 = MIDI 60) → WAV `smpl` (MIDIUnityNote + MIDIPitchFraction/2³² semitones) → `inst` (UnshiftedNote + FineTune cents) → `acid` (root note when its flag is set) → AIFF `INST` base note → 60.
5. **Loop**: WAV `smpl` first loop (`start`, `end + 1`), AIFF `INST` sustain loop with `MARK`s; else null.
6. **ZIP**: central directory parsed; stored and deflate entries (deflate via `DecompressionStream('deflate-raw')`; unavailable ⇒ deflated entries skipped with a notice). A ZIP holding an `.sfz` imports it with its samples; otherwise every audio file imports as a sample.
7. **SFZ** (`smp.parseSfz(text) → {regions, warnings}`): headers `<control>` (`default_path`, `note_offset`, `octave_offset`), `<global>`, `<master>`, `<group>`, `<region>` with inheritance; opcodes `sample, lokey, hikey, key, pitch_keycenter, lovel, hivel, tune, transpose, volume, pan, amp_veltrack (% → `vt` = clamp(v/100, 0, 1); absent ⇒ `vt = 1`, the SFZ default of 100 %; negative values clamp to 0 with a warning), loop_mode (no_loop, one_shot, loop_continuous, loop_sustain), loop_start|loopstart, loop_end|loopend, offset, end, ampeg_delay, ampeg_attack, ampeg_hold, ampeg_decay, ampeg_sustain, ampeg_release, seq_length, seq_position, trigger`; note names with **c4 = 60** (SFZ convention); `trigger=release` regions, `#define`, `#include` and other opcodes are ignored and listed once in a notice. Sample paths resolve against the selected files/ZIP by case-insensitive path, then basename. Each referenced sample imports as a `smp:` record (root = the region's `pitch_keycenter`), the multisample record references them.
8. **Deduplicate**: `hash` = two FNV-1a-32 (seeds 0x811c9dc5 and 0x01000193) over the canonical PCM; an identical hash reuses the existing id.
9. Store the record (`smpstore.put`), run the onset function (§9.4.5) in the worker, refresh menus. The first user-asset import of a session calls `navigator.storage.persist()` (Safari evicts unpersisted IndexedDB data after 7 days without a visit); a refusal shows a one-time notice recommending project export. The project asset meter (§14.2) refuses an import that would exceed the device budget.

### 9.4 Analysis and entry building (`smpwork.js` module worker importing `smp.js` and `smpgen.js`; main-thread fallback = the same functions)

#### 9.4.1 Protocol
Request `{q, op, …}` → reply `{q, ok: true, …}` or `{q, ok: false, err}`; ArrayBuffers transferred both ways.
| op | Request | Reply |
|---|---|---|
| `resample` | `{L, R, rate, to}` | `{L, R, rate}` |
| `odf` | `{L, R, rate}` | `{odf}` |
| `entry` | `{id, rec, ops}` | playback entry (§4.5) |
| `spec` | `{id, rec, ops}` | spectral analysis entry |
| `ir` | `{id, rec, rate}` | prepared IR record at `rate` (§9.4.7; cached) |
| `irshape` | `{id, rec, rate, shape: {size, tone, minph, att, dec, damp}}` | shaped-IR entry (§4.5, §8.4): `smp.irShape` + partition spectra |
| `wtsmp` | `{id, frames: Float32Array(R·2048)}` | playback + `spec:` entries of a wavetable played as a sample (§9.4.8) |
| `multi` | `{id, rec}` | multisample entry (zones; zone samples are separate `entry` requests) |
| `gen` | `{id}` (factory procedural sample/IR id) | generated record |
| `loop` | `{L, R, rate, from, to, f0}` | `{ls, le}` (loop search, §10.1) |

#### 9.4.2 Non-destructive operations (`ops`, in this order)
trim `[a, b]` (fractions) → reverse → normalize (peak to 0 dBFS) → fade edges (raised cosine of `FADE_MS[fade]` at both ends). Record loop points are mapped through trim and reverse. The playback entry bakes **trim and fade** only; reverse and normalize are applied by the DSP (`sRev`, `sNorm`, §6.7.7) — the order is preserved because fade is symmetric and normalize is a gain. The spectral analysis (§9.4.6) must see the playback direction, so it is built from the entry data reversed when `rev` is set and named `spec:<playback id>~r` (`norm` stays a gain, applied by SxSpec). **Reload Sample** clears `smpo.ops` and re-reads the stored record (`smpstore.get`), rebuilding the entries.

#### 9.4.3 Playback levels
Level 0 = the processed data **resampled to ≤ 48 kHz** (records above 48 kHz are kept at their rate in the store; the entry is band-limited and resampled with `smp.resample`); level ℓ+1 = level ℓ filtered by the Phase-1 63-tap half-band (Kaiser β 9, linear convolution) and decimated by 2, loop points halved; up to 8 levels or until `len < 64` (`wt.noiseLevels` gains a `maxLevels` option; owner W). Multisample zone entries get levels only up to `ceil(log2(2^(maxUp/12)·rate/fsMin))` where `maxUp` = the zone's largest upward transposition (`hi − root` semitones, + 12 for TIMBRE headroom) and fsMin = 44.1 kHz (typically level 0 and 1 only).

#### 9.4.4 Peak
`peak` = max |x| of level 0.

#### 9.4.5 Onset detection function
Mono sum; STFT N 1024, hop 256, Hann; `odf[t] = Σ_k max(0, ln(1 + 100|X_t,k|) − ln(1 + 100|X_t−1,k|))`, normalised to max 1. Auto slicing at threshold thr: local maxima of odf above thr, at least 50 ms apart, ≤ 256 → `marks` (fractions of len).

#### 9.4.6 Spectral analysis
Mono sum of the processed data, **resampled to ≤ 48 kHz** first (so `N_a` = 2048 always and the analysis stays ≤ 16 MB at the 20 s cap: 20 s·48000/512 frames × 1025 bins × (4 + 4) bytes ≈ 15.4 MB); `N_a` = 2048; `hop_a = N_a/4`; Hann; frames centred at `t·hop_a` with zero padding; `mag = |X|·2/Σw`; `ph = arg X`; transient flag when the frame's flux (as §9.4.5) > max(0.05, 1.5 × median of the 15 surrounding frames). Cap: the first 20 s (notice). Cached in `cache` (`spec:…`).

#### 9.4.7 IR preparation
Resample to the requested rate (windowed sinc); trim leading samples below −60 dB of the peak (keep 1 ms before the first sample above), trailing below −90 dB; normalise so that the larger channel energy Σx² = 1; cap 10 s. The shaped entry (`irshape`) applies §8.4's chain to this record and partitions it.

#### 9.4.8 Wavetables as samples (inv B8, manual pp.70, 86, 105)
A wavetable id in `osc[i].smp` plays as one sample: the table's R frames of 2048 concatenated (frame 0 first) at **48 kHz**, mono, `root = 69 + 12·log2(48000/(2048·440))` ≈ 18.232 (one frame = one cycle of the root), loop = the whole table (`{start: 0, end: R·2048}`), no `odf` (slicing marks at the frame boundaries are offered as Auto marks). The asset manager reads the frames (factory: `wt.factoryTable`; user: `wtstore`) and requests op `wtsmp`, which returns the playback entry `wtsmp:<table id>` and its spectral analysis `spec:wtsmp:<table id>`. The Sample, Granular and Spectral source menus list the tables in a **Wavetables ▸** folder (factory categories, User).

### 9.5 Main ↔ worklet protocol (additions to Phase-1 §11.6)
| Message | Handling |
|---|---|
| `{t: 'smp', …entry}` (buffers transferred) | `SxBase.storeAsset(entry.id, entry)` |
| `{t: 'smpdel', id}` | `SxBase.dropAsset(id)` |
| `processorOptions.smps` | entries stored by the first node of an offline context (Phase-1 `wts` rule) |

The main-thread asset manager (`assets.js`, owner S1; generalises Phase-1 `tables`) keeps per AudioContext the `sent` set, builds entries in `smpwork`, reference-counts ids over every track's `patch.needs` (Serum `needs` + FX `needs`), posts `smpdel` for ids unreferenced 10 s, and frees unreferenced entries above `KEEP_BYTES` = 64 MB (32 MB on touch devices, §14.2; oldest first). Entries are always freshly built (transfer detaches buffers). Live edits that change an entry (`trim`, `fade`, re-import, Convolve shaping) post a new id, never mutate a sent entry; the **superseded** id (`~opsKey`, `~shapeKey`) is dropped (`smpdel`) as soon as the patch that no longer needs it has been posted to every node of the context (no 10 s grace for superseded variants). Slicing never changes an entry (marks travel in `CP.sSlPos`).

### 9.6 Bounces, resample, preview
`OfflineAudioContext` at rate R: the asset manager builds entries for every id any rendered track needs, **IR entries for R**; they travel as `processorOptions.smps` on the first node with `wts` (structured-cloned — acceptable for an offline render, which owns its copies). Live, IR entries are built for the live context rate. The **preset-preview node** (§12.6) lives in the live context: it receives **no** assets in `processorOptions`; its `needs` go through the asset manager like a track's (ids already sent are shared, nothing is copied).

### 9.7 Caching
Factory assets are deterministic: generated once per `GEN_VER` and stored in `cache` (`gen:<id>@<GEN_VER>`); the first use of factory content shows a progress notice ("Generating factory sounds… n %"). Main-thread record LRU ≤ 256 MB (unreferenced first). Spectral analyses cached per playback id.

### 9.8 Files
Exported projects/presets/packs add `smps: { [id]: encoded }` for every **user** asset they reference (oscillator samples and multisamples with their zone samples; Convolve IRs of any track). Encoding: `{kind, name, rate, ch, len, root, loop, enc: 'pcm24', L: base64, R: base64 | null}` (24-bit LE PCM; float data quantised to 24 bit), multisamples `{kind: 'multi', name, zones}` (zone samples listed separately). Never in the autosave, the history or localStorage presets. Import: decode → `smpstore.put` (skip identical ids) → drop `smps`. Factory ids are never embedded. Preset packs: `{type: 'synth.serum.pack', v: 1, name, presets: [preset…], wts, smps}` (also accepted inside a ZIP as `pack.json`).

### 9.9 Deletion and missing assets
As Phase-1 `wtHost.del`: deleting a user asset switches every sound of the project using it to none (silent oscillator / bypassed Convolve) with a "Missing sample" badge; dropping a file on the badge relinks.

---

## 10. Factory content generators (owner S1; original, deterministic, documented here)

All generators are pure functions in `smpgen.js` except the render farm (§10.1), seeded with `mulberry32(fnv1a32(<id>))`, at 48 kHz, and covered by determinism tests (§15).

### 10.1 Multisamples rendered from this project's models (`FACTORY_MULTI`)
**Render farm** `smprender.js` (classic Worker): defines the worklet globals as stubs (`sampleRate` = 48000, `currentFrame`, `AudioWorkletProcessor` with a fake port, `registerProcessor` capturing the class), replaces `Math.random` by `mulberry32(fnv1a32(<render id>))` (reseeded per request; engine.js's Solina `Divide` is the one consumer, §0.4), and `importScripts` the 11 DSP scripts and `engine.js` in §0.3 order. Request `{q, id, patch, wts, smps, events: [[frame, on, n, vel127]], frames, os: 2}` → **one** fresh `SynthProcessor({processorOptions: {os, offline: true, patch, wts, smps}})` renders the whole event list (so loops, legato and arp/clip sequences render like a bounce) in 128-frame quanta for `frames` frames, or until the output stays below −80 dBFS for 2048 frames after the last note-off → reply `{q, L, R}`. A multisample plan issues one request per (root, velocity) zone render; a loop sample one request for its whole phrase. Patches come from the pure **`patchFor(model, vals, fx, {sr, bpm, drift: 0, hiss: 0})`** exported by `models.js` (owner I) — the same function main.js's `buildPatch` uses for a track (main.js adds only live-session fields: node routing, safeguard caps), and that `assets.js`, `smpgen` plans and the tests use; FX `defaultFx()`. Serum sources (Drum Loop, Break, Arp Loop) are the **migrated** Phase-1 presets (`s1 = 1`) with their wavetables in `wts`. Deterministic (fixed engine seeds, seeded `Math.random`).

**Post-processing** (`smpgen.multiFromRenders`): one gain per instrument `g = 10^(−1/20)/max peak`; leading silence below −70 dBFS trimmed (keep 1 ms); `sustain` instruments get a loop found in `[0.45·gate, 0.95·gate]`: candidate lengths = integer multiples ≥ 0.25 s of the YIN period of that region, both ends at upward zero crossings, minimising the RMS seam error over 32 samples (op `loop`), zone mode `sustain`; `oneshot` instruments no loop. Zone key ranges: root j covers `[r_j − ⌊(r_j − r_{j−1})/2⌋, r_j + ⌈(r_{j+1} − r_j)/2⌉ − 1]`, first from 0, last to 127; velocity layers split at the midpoints of their velocities.

**Memory budget (normative, B11)**: each instrument ≤ **16 MB** of raw Float32 PCM (all zones, level 0) and `FACTORY_MULTI` ≤ **150 MB** in total; a render whose channels are identical (L ≡ R bit for bit) is stored **mono**. Roots: step 6 inside MIDI 36–84 and step 12 outside it (‡: 24–96 → 24, 36, 42, …, 84, 96 = 11 roots; 24–60 → 24, 36, 42, 48, 54, 60 = 6; 36–96 → 10; 48–96 → 8). Sustain instruments render a **1.5 s** gate at most (their loop carries the sustain). The MB column is the worst case for the channel count expected from the model (st = chorus/ensemble/stereo spread; mo = mono models); if an instrument expected mono renders in stereo, S1 halves its velocity layers (keeping the extremes) — `tests/smpgen.test.mjs` asserts both budgets on the actual renders.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| Juno Sub Bass | juno60 | Sub Bass | 24–60 ‡ | 100 | 1.2 | 0.5 | sustain | st | 3.9 |

### 10.3 Factory impulse responses (`FACTORY_IRS`; stereo 48 kHz; Convolve folders)
Generators: **room** = image-source early reflections (shoebox L×W×H m, source (0.3L, 0.4W, 1.5), listener (0.6L, 0.55W, 1.6) and a second listener +0.17 m in x for R, order ≤ 3, reflection gain `√(1 − α)`, 1/distance, air LP 20 kHz at 0 m to 8 kHz at 100 m) cross-faded at the mixing time `√V` ms into a late tail of velvet noise (2000 impulses/s, independent L/R) decaying with three-band RT60 (low < 250 Hz ×1.2, mid ×1, high > 4 kHz ×0.6); **cab** = magnitude design (2nd-order HP at f_hp, 4th-order LP at f_lp, peak (f_r, Q, +dB), presence peak, notch) → minimum phase by real cepstrum (FFT 4096) → 2048 samples with a half-Hann tail; **spring** = 3 dispersive chirp trains (cascades of 120 first-order allpasses, a = 0.6) at 25/31/37 ms spacing decaying with RT60 2.5 s.

| Folder | IRs (parameters) |
|---|---|
| Short | Small Room (4×3×2.5, α 0.3, RT60 0.3 s) · Studio Booth (2.5×2×2.2, α 0.5, 0.15 s) · Bathroom (3×2×2.4, α 0.05, 0.8 s, bright: high ×1) |
| Medium | Chamber (8×6×4, α 0.15, 1.2 s) · Club (12×10×4, α 0.25, 0.9 s, high ×0.4) · Wood Room (6×5×3, α 0.2, 0.7 s) |
| Long | Concert Hall (40×25×18, α 0.12, 2.4 s) · Church (30×15×20, α 0.06, 3.8 s) · Parking Garage (50×30×3, α 0.04, 2.8 s, high ×0.9) |
| Massive | Cathedral (60×30×35, α 0.05, 6 s) · Canyon (8 discrete echoes 120–900 ms seeded + 8 s tail) · Endless Plate (velvet tail 9 s, density 6000/s, no early reflections) |
| Cab | 1x12 Open (f_hp 80, f_lp 5200, peak 110 Hz Q 1.2 +4 dB, presence 2.6 kHz +3 dB) · 2x12 Closed (90, 4800, 120 Hz +6 dB, 2.2 kHz +4 dB, notch 4.5 kHz −8 dB) · 4x12 Vintage (70, 4200, 100 Hz +5 dB, 1.8 kHz +5 dB) · Bass 1x15 (40, 3000, 60 Hz +3 dB) · Tiny Speaker (300, 6000, 900 Hz +6 dB) · Radio (450, 3200, 1.2 kHz +4 dB, mono) |
| Coloration | Vintage EQ (+3 dB 100 Hz shelf, −2 dB 3 kHz, +2 dB 12 kHz, min phase) · Tape Head (head bump 60 Hz +3 dB, HF loss −6 dB at 15 kHz) · Telephone (BP 300–3400 Hz) · Formant A (Phase-1 table I vowel a, 3 resonators) · Comb Shimmer (comb 7 ms g 0.7, 400 ms) |
| Weird | Reverse Swell (Concert Hall reversed, 2.4 s) · Gated Room (Chamber truncated at 250 ms with a 20 ms fade) · Pitched Comb C (comb at 261.63 Hz, g 0.97, 1.5 s) · Shepard Tail (velvet tail with a rising Shepard band-pass sweep) · Spring (spring generator) · Granular Smear (40 Hann grains of the Church tail at random positions/pitches ±7 st) |

Init (len 1, zeros) · Analog Drift (randoms pan 0.15, det 0.08, cut 0.1, env 0.1) · Stereo Spread (len 4, pan −0.6, 0.6, −0.3, 0.3) · Round Robin Pan (len 3, pan −0.8, 0, 0.8) · Cutoff Walk (len 8, cut −0.4…+0.4 ascending) · Mod Sequence (len 4, m1 = −1, −0.33, 0.33, 1).

Per S2 type, ≥ 3 factory module presets (e.g. Bode: Barber Pole, Spiral Delay, Stereo Detune; Convolve: Small Room Wet, Cab Crunch, Endless Plate; Utility: Mono Bass, Wide Stereo, Phase Flip; Reverb types: one per type), plus factory rack presets (Main: Shimmer Hall, Parallel Crush (splitter L/H with distortion on highs); Bus: Send Plate, Send Delay). Factory effect chains of Phase 1 stay (migrated).

### 10.11 Factory presets v2 (C1 + D6, M3)
≥ 30 original presets with `s1 = 0`, authored through the integration harness, covering every major feature at least once: 3-osc stacks, Multisample (each factory instrument at least once), Sample (slices), Granular (each window shape across the set), Spectral (each spectral warp across the set), true FM (each FM type), dual warps, Filter 2 series/parallel, buses with Bus FX, every new FX module and reverb type, arp patterns, clip banks with macro lanes, MPE-ready (expression rows), Voice Control, a tuning (`.scl` embedded: 7-limit just major, original text). Categories as Phase 1 plus `Texture`, `Sequence`. The Phase-1 factory presets remain listed (migrated, `s1 = 1`).
