## 11. Module APIs and host contracts (delta)

### 11.1 `serum-core.js` (C1)
Re-exports everything of `laws.js`, `fx-core.js`, `seq-core.js` (constants and pure helpers) and `tuning.js`, so Phase-1 importers keep working (including the FX constants and v1 FX functions that moved to fx-core, §11.2).

| Export | Semantics |
|---|---|
| constants | `VERSION` 2, `CP_VER` 2, `N_OSC` 3, `N_MATRIX` 64, `N_LFO` 10, `N_ENV` 4, `N_MACRO` 8, `NSV` 429, `ND` 529, `NS` 148 (v1 synth count, kept), `NV1` 248, `NF1` 100, `NS2` 281, every list of §3, `VC_RATES`, `S1_PAIRS` (§2.6.4), `DESTS` (529; rows 148–247 generated from fx-core's `FX_ROWS_V1`), `DIDX` (vi), `GIDX`, `VIRTUAL` (frozen array of ids), `NOTE_ON_DESTS`, `DEF2`, `RENAME1`, `S1_TO_S2_WARP`, `S1_TO_S2_SUB`, `SUB_IDS2`, `MODULE_FIELDS` v2 (below), `FACTORY_PRESETS` (v2 list: new presets then the migrated Phase-1 presets), `FACTORY_PRESETS_V1` (the Phase-1 objects, frozen, for tests) |
| `vi(idx)`, `idxOfVi(vi)`, `isFxId(id)` | index mapping (§3.11.1); −1 when none (FX rows and scope-M rows); `isFxId` = `/^fx\./` or `g.master`/`g.bypass` — the only allowed FX test |
| `defaultSerum()`, `normalizeSerum(s)`, `migrate1(v1)` | §2 |
| `compileSerum(s, ctx)` | §4.1 |
| `lawValue`, `lawNorm`, `fmtParam`, `parseParam`, `destLabel`, `destBase`, `isCenter` | accept static ids and dynamic FX ids (with `ctx.fx` / `fx`) |
| `destMenu(s, fx, {matrix})` → `[{group, items: [{id, label}]}]` | destination menu (S1 legacy destinations listed under "S1" only when `s1` or in use) |
| `srcMenu(s)` → `[{group, items}]` | §3.10 ordering rules |
| `warpChoice2(s, o, slot, mode)` | in place: sets `k.w1`/`k.w2` (S1 mode slot 1 of A/B: Phase-1 `warpChoice`); if the choice creates an oscillator cycle (§6.6.6) the conflicting slots of the other oscillators are set Off (most recent choice wins) |
| `swapWarps(s, o)` | in place: swaps slot modes, amounts, vars, uni spreads, `fm[]`, and re-targets rows between the slot destinations |
| `setS1(s, on)` | §2.6.4: returns a new state with only `global.s1` changed (the checkbox) |
| `syncS1(s, id)` | in place, after an edit of a paired field (§2.6.4): writes the other field of the pair through the forward map, or through the inverse map when the value is in its image; returns the list of pairs whose renditions now differ |
| `chaosToLfo(s)` | deviation 22: returns a new state where each matrix row reading `chaos1`/`chaos2` reads instead the first LFO that no row uses and whose mode/type are default, set to type `rossler` (chaos1) / `lorenz` (chaos2; the Phase-1 attractor assignment k 0 Rössler, k 1 Lorenz), copying the chaos rate (Hz) or BPM ladder entry and MONO; a chaos S&H setting has no Chaos-LFO equivalent and is dropped; chaos rows stay where no LFO is free. Approximate (output scaling differs), announced in a confirmation |
| `addMod`, `modsFor`, `removeMods`, `setModAmount`, `toggleModBypass`, `toggleModPolarity`, `reassignSource`, `veloToAmp`, `vibratoAssign`, `initLfos`, `copyLfo`, `linkedBuses`, `newBus`, `pruneBuses`, `clearPointMods` | Phase-1 semantics with 64 rows / 10 LFOs |
| `applyMacros(s, fx)`, `sortMatrix(s, 'src' | 'dst')`, `swapMacros(s, a, b)`, `initMods(s)` (Init Modulations: clears every row and LFO point link) | §5.6, §5.8 |
| `copyModule(target, source, m, from, {mods})`, `swapModules(s, a, b)` | module copy/paste/swap incl. "with mods" (rows whose destination belongs to the module are copied with re-targeted destinations; swap exchanges rows too; inv B2). **Allowed pairs (m, from)**: (a) any pair of `oscA`/`oscB`/`oscC` — destinations `a.*`/`b.*`/`c.*` by suffix and the whole `osc[i]` object (type, `wt`, `smp`, `multi`, `smpo`, `msmo`, `spec`, `xy`, `pitch` — a ratio source equal to the target itself becomes `note` —, `uni`, `route`, `env`, `map`, `fm`, `phMem`, …); the warp-cycle rule (§6.6.6) is re-applied with the target as the most recent choice; (b) `filter` ↔ `filter2` — `f.cutoff/res/drive/var/mix/pan/on/level/x/y/bus1/bus2` ↔ `f2.*`, `f.routeA/B/N/S/C` ↔ `f2.routeA/B/N/S/C`, `f.type2` ↔ `f2.type` (in S1 mode a copy into Filter 1 also sets `f.type` when the type is ≤ 95), structured `keytrack`, `clean`, `route`, `env`, `pz`; a route that would make both filters feed each other is set to Main on the target; (c) `env1…env4` among themselves (`e<k>.*` by suffix and `env[k]`; Start/End are dropped when the target is ENV 1, and ENV 1 as a source gives Start/End 0); (d) `lfo1…lfo10` among themselves (`l<k>.*` by suffix incl. PHASE and `lfo[k]`, = Phase-1 `copyLfo` semantics); (e) every module with itself (`noise`, `sub`, `velo`, `note`, `arp`, `clip`, `vc`). Other pairs return false |
| `initModule(s, m)` | in place (inv B2 "Init Module also removes the module's modulations"): the `MODULE_FIELDS[m]` destinations and structured paths ← `defaultSerum()`'s, then every matrix row whose `dst` is one of the module's destinations is deleted (rows from the module as a *source*, e.g. an LFO's rows, are kept: Init LFOs + LFO Mods is the separate main-menu action); LFO point links of an initialised LFO are cleared; locks unchanged |
| `initPreset(cur, prefs)` → `{s, fx}` | Init Preset (manual p.33: "initializes all settings and removes any module locks"): `s` = `defaultSerum()` with the preference-driven fields (`global.os` = `prefs.defOs`, `author`, `ui.view3d`, `global.mpe.on` = `prefs.mpeDefault`) and **every lock removed** (`locks = {p: [], mod: [], unp: [], misc: {poly 0, os 0, tuning 0}}`); `fx = defaultFx()` for Serum tracks (every `racks[k].lock = 0`); `ui` keeps the current page. Does **not** go through `applyPreset` |
| `applyPreset(cur, preset, {midiFromPreset = 1})` | Phase-1 rules with v2 modules, `locks.mod` ∈ `MODULE_FIELDS` keys, Lock Matrix, and: (1) locked parameters `locks.p` keep their values; (2) locked modules are copied from `cur` (`copyFields`), **then** the preset's values are re-applied for every id in `cur.locks.unp` (controls unlocked inside a locked module, inv B2); (3) `locks.misc.tuning` keeps `global.a4` **and** `global.tun`; (4) MIDI map: `midiFromPreset = 0` keeps `cur.midi`; `1` (default, = Phase 1) takes the preset's map when it is non-empty, else keeps `cur.midi` (preference "Load MIDI Map from Presets", B24.1, manual p.315); (5) Lock Matrix as §5.8 |
| `hybridize(presets, {seed, favor})` | inv B25: genes = `MODULE_FIELDS` modules + `macros` + `voicing` + `global` (minus `os`, `a4`); each gene from one parent chosen by a `mulberry32(seed)` draw (`favor` = index of the favoured parent with probability ½, others ½·⅓ each; else uniform); matrix rows follow the parent of their destination's module (rows of FX destinations follow the parent chosen for the FX state, which is taken whole from one parent); result normalised; deterministic per seed |
| `oscTypeOf(s, o)`, `oscUses(s, o)` → `{wt, smp, multi}` | helpers for the UI and the host's `needs` |
| `assetNeeds(s)` | user/factory asset ids referenced (for embedding, §9.8) |
| `fxModded2(s, fx) → Uint8Array(NFX)` | 1 at FX-ctx index k when an active row targets it (dynamic ids through `fxDest`) — the v2 `compileFx` `modded`. The Phase-1 `fxModded(s) → Uint8Array(NF1 = 100)` stays unchanged for v1 code and tests |

`MODULE_FIELDS` v2 (module locks, copy/paste/init/swap):

| module | destinations (`group`) | structured paths |
|---|---|---|
| `oscA`/`oscB`/`oscC` | `Osc K`, `Osc K Sample`, `Osc K Granular`, `Osc K Spectral`, `Osc K Multisample` | `['osc', i]` |
| `noise` | `Noise` | `['noise']` |
| `sub` | `Sub` | `['sub']` |
| `filter` | `Filter` | `['filter', 0]` |
| `filter2` | `Filter 2` | `['filter', 1]` |
| `env1`…`env4` | `Env e` | `['env', e−1]` |
| `lfo1`…`lfo10` | `LFO l` | `['lfo', l−1]` |
| `velo`, `note` | — | `['velo']`, `['note']` |
| `arp`, `clip` | `Arp` / `Clip` | `['arp']`, `['clip']` |
| `vc` | `Voice Control` | `['vc']` |
| `matrix` | — | `['matrix']` (Lock Matrix) |

### 11.1b `laws.js` (C1; leaf module, B7)
The knob-law and display engine shared by serum-core and fx-core (moved out of serum-core so that fx-core does not import serum-core): `LAWS`, `lawValue(law, lo, hi, n)`, `lawNorm(law, lo, hi, v)`, `gridOf`, the format engine `fmtCore(fmt, v, n, ctx)` / `parseCore(fmt, text, ctx)` for every format code of Phase-1 §3.2 and §3.2 here, `fmtHz`, `fmtTime`, `parseDb`, `toF`. serum-core's `fmtParam`/`parseParam` and fx-core's parameter formatting both delegate to it. Pure, no imports.

### 11.2 `fx-core.js` (F1)
**Ownership of the FX constants (one source of truth).** In M1a C1 moves, verbatim, from serum-core into fx-core: `DIST_MODES`, `DLY_LADDER`, `MODFX_LADDER`, `REV_LABELS`, `EQ_TYPES_*`, `CMP_RATIO_DETENTS`, `FX_MODULES`, `FX_ORDER_DEFAULT`, the v1 FX destination rows as **`FX_ROWS_V1`** (the 100 rows of idx 148–247 in order) and `RENAME1`, and the v1 functions `defaultFx`, `normalizeFx`, `compileFx`, `compileFxT`, `applyPresetFx`, `FACTORY_CHAINS` (as `…V1` internals behind the v2 entry points); serum-core builds DESTS 148–247 **from** `FX_ROWS_V1` and re-exports the rest. fx-core imports only `laws.js` (no cycle). After the move F1 owns fx-core.

`FXP_VER` 2, `FXTYPES`, `FXP_TAB`, `DIST_MODES2`, `REV_TYPES2`, `NITROUS_MODES`, `REV_LABELS2`, `MAX_INST` 48, `defaultFx()` (v2: three empty racks), `normalizeFx(fx, refs?)`, `migrateFx1(v1, refs?)`, `fxLayout(fx)`, `compileFx(fx, ctx)`, `compileFxT(fx)`, `fxDest(id, fx)` (→ `{inst, type, v, pid, row, flat, nomod}`), `instLabel(fx, id)`, `newInstance(fx, type, {rack, splitter, branch, at, preset})` → id (in place; `preset` = the type's default module preset payload `synth.fxmod.default.<type>` read by the caller, applied as its `p` and `sub` on a variant-2 instance when present — fx-core stays storage-free), `removeInstance(fx, id, s?)` (also removes matrix rows/locks/MIDI of `s` targeting it), `moveInstance(fx, id, {rack, splitter, branch, at})`, `duplicateInstance(fx, id, s?, {mods})`, rack clipboard `cutRack/copyRack/pasteRack/clearRack`, `upgradeInstance(inst)` (S1 → S2 variant: same pids keep n — enums re-gridded, Phase-1-equivalent values for the new pids where the S2 behaviour can reproduce S1: Phaser poles 6, Delay hq 0, Compressor below = ratio n, band gains 0; Reverb maps Plate `t` → `pre` (n of `200·t` ms), `c` → `damp`, `w` → `width`; Hall `t` → `decay`, `c` → `spinr`, `w` → `spind`; approximate where laws differ, announced in the confirmation), `modulePresetPayload(fx, id, name)` / `applyModulePreset(fx, id, payload, lockedIds)`, `rackPresetPayload(fx, k, name)` / `applyRackPreset(fx, k, payload)`, `migrateChains1(lib)`, `FACTORY_CHAINS` (v2), `FACTORY_FX_PRESETS`, `FACTORY_RACKS`, and **`applyPresetFx(curFx, presetFx, s)`** v2 (preset load, called by main.js `sxLoad` and serum-ui): every rack with `racks[k].lock` is kept from `curFx` (its instances, ids and parameter values), the other racks come from `presetFx` (default when absent); incoming instances whose ids collide with kept ones are renamed; `s` = the new Serum state returned by `applyPreset` (current locks, the preset's rows) and is rewritten in place: its rows follow renamed instances and rows addressing instances of a locked rack are dropped (§2.5); dynamic ids in `s.locks.p` (`fx.<id>.<pid>`) keep the current value when the incoming state has an instance of the same id and type; `ui` from `curFx`. The Phase-1 signature `applyPresetFx(curFx, presetFx, lockedIds)` remains as the v1 function for v1 tests.

### 11.3 `seq-core.js` (D6)
Constants of §3.15; `defaultArp()`, `defaultClip()`, `defaultKbd()`, `normalizeArp/normalizeClip/normalizeKbd(x)`; `compileSeq(s, ctx)` → `{seq, seqT}` (§4.4; memoised by content key); `arpLadder(dot, trip)`, `gridLadder(dot, trip)`; `quantize(note, key, scale)`, `scaleMask(key, scale)`, `swingTime(beat, s, d)` (s ∈ [−1, 1], §7.2); `arpOrder(shape, notes, state)` (pure reference of §7.4.1 used by tests and the UI preview), `rangeOrder(shape, R)`; piano-roll operations on note arrays (pure, return new arrays): `add, remove, move(dt, dn), resize, chop(grid), conformToScale(key, scale), legato, mute, quantize(grid, strength), reverse, scaleTime(f), doubleClip, scaleClip(f), timeCopy(range) / timePaste(at)` (overwrite and split, inv B23), `duplicate`, `selectRange`; the same for arp pattern events; `slicesToNotes(marks, lenSec, bpm, root) → {len16, notes}` (Send to Selected Clip: note j = slice j at its original start time converted to sixteenths at the tempo, length = slice duration, pitch `root + j`, velocity 100, clip length rounded up to whole bars), `wrapAt(notes, start, off, len)` (Wrap Clip to Begin Here, §7.5.6), `clipToSmf(clip, bpm) → Uint8Array` (Export MIDI File, §7.5.6), `snapGrid(mode, {bpm, rate, S, E, lenBeats, loop})` (marker Snap to Beats/Loop positions, §13.3.2), `genBassline(seed)`, `genMelody(seed)`, `FALLBACK_CLIPS` (3 preview clips: chord, bass line, lead phrase), `FACTORY_ARP_BANKS`, `FACTORY_CLIP_BANKS`, `FACTORY_PATTERNS`; storage helpers for user banks (`synth.sx.arpbanks`, `synth.sx.clipbanks`, `synth.sx.arppats`, `synth.sx.clips`), `slotName(slot)`.

### 11.4 `tuning.js` (C1)
`parseScl(text) → {name, cents: number[] (degree 1…n, the last = period), n} | {error}`; `parseKbm(text) → {size, first, last, middle, refNote, refFreq, octDegree, map: (number | null)[]} | {error}`; `parseTun(text) → {name, hz: Float64Array(128)} | {error}` (AnaMark `[Tuning]` cents re 8.1757989 Hz and `[Exact Tuning]`; v1/v2); `tableFrom({scl, kbm} | {tun}) → Float64Array(128)` (fractional MIDI pitch relative to A4 = 440 Hz; without `.kbm`: linear mapping, middle note 60 = degree 0, reference note 69 → 440 Hz); `stepTable(scl) → {sclN, sclPer, sclSteps}`; `describe(tab)`. Limits: ≤ 1024 degrees, text ≤ 16 KB.

### 11.5 `sx-base.js` → `SxBase` (D5)
| Member | Semantics |
|---|---|
| `VER` | 1 |
| constants | `DIDX2` (vi), `SIDX2`, `GIDX`, `LAW_TAB2` `{law: Uint8Array(429), lo, hi: Float64Array(429), cont: Uint8Array(429)}`, `SRC_POL2` (62), `WARP_DOM`, `WARP_AVAIL` (Uint8Array(82)), `FILTER_FAM2` (Uint8Array(108)), `WIN_TAB` (Float64Array(10·1025)), ladders of §3.12/§3.15 — built by the same builders as serum-core (integration-tested) |
| math | `exp2`, `log2`, `tanh` (Phase-1 tolerances), `sin1`, `shape(mode, x, d)` = the FX distortion shapers 0–17 (ported verbatim from fx-dsp's Phase-1 shapers for 0–15, plus Overdrive's single-stage curve and Soft Sat.; §0.3 — the one copy used by FX Distortion S2 and the distortion warps) |
| FFT | `FFT(n)` objects (n power of two ≤ 2¹⁶) with preallocated tables: `fwd(re, im)`, `inv(re, im)` in place on Float64Array, `rfwd(x, re, im)`, `rinv(re, im, x)` |
| resamplers | `HBUp(osFactor)` / `HBDown(osFactor)` polyphase half-band chains (31 taps per stage, Kaiser β 9) with `process(in, out, n)` (HBDown also feeds the base-rate Vocode modulator rings, §6.10.5) |
| PRNG | `xorshift(s)`, `R2_SALT` = 0x85EBCA6B (§6.2) |
| warm-up | `warmTick(proc)` helper: runs at most one queued lazy warm-up job per render quantum, ≤ 1 ms (§11.10) |
| assets | `assets` (Map surviving re-evaluation), `epoch` (getter), `storeAsset(id, e) → boolean` (validates §4.5 shapes), `dropAsset(id)`, `getAsset(id)`, `assetBytes()` |

### 11.6 `sx-mod.js` → `SxMod` (D2)
Owns the Phase-1 modulation code moved out of serum-dsp (extracted **verbatim** in M1a by the D1+D2 pair, §0.5): `attach(sx, proc)` (per-processor modulation state in `sx.mod`), `begin(proc, sx, nCh)` (Phase-1 `begin` order: base smoothing over 429 vi, mono context, performance smoothers, macros incl. clip lanes and MPE mapping, Free/Mono LFOs, mono chaos, global sources), `idle(proc, sx, n)`, `transport(proc, sx, playing)`, `reset(sx)`, `fxMods(proc, sx) → Float64Array(MAXFX)`, class `ModV(voice)` with `noteOn(n, vel, legato, polyRef, ch, ex)`, `noteOff(rv)`, `kill()`, `sources(gc)`, `matrix(fr)`, `advance(gc)`, `envVal(e)`, `lfoVal(l)`, fields `eff` (Float64Array(429)), `src` (Float64Array(62)), `eV/eSt/eT` (4), `lPh/lVal/lY` (10), `vc` values, `rd` (64), `slew` (64). serum-dsp's `SerumVoice` holds `this.mod = new SxMod.ModV(this)`.

Interface details (normative, B19 — the D1/D2 boundary):
- `gc` = the index of the current chunk inside the current span (0 … `sx.nCh − 1`); `fr` = 1 on the first chunk after `noteOn` (the note-on evaluation of Phase-1 §6.1 applies), else 0.
- **Global source table** `sx.g`: Float64Array(`NSRC · MAXCH`), `MAXCH = 32` (Phase-1 constant), value of global source s at chunk c at `[s·MAXCH + c]`, written by `begin` for every chunk of the span (Phase-1 layout, extended to 62 sources; per-voice sources are not stored there).
- **Audio taps**: after step 8 of §6.1 the voice writes the last sample of each channel's mono raw signal into `this.mod.src[S_OSCA]`, `[S_OSCB]`, `[S_OSCC]`, `[S_SUB]`, `[S_FILT1]`, `[S_FILT2]` (and Phase-1's `[S_NOISE]` as before) — they are read by the next chunk's `matrix`; ModV never computes them.
- `sx.mod` fields read by serum-dsp: `monoEff` (Float64Array(429)), `monoOff`, `fxOff` (Float64Array(MAXFX)), `recent` (the most recent voice), `beatC` (Float64Array(MAXCH), beats per chunk), `macro` (Float64Array(8) smoothed macro sources), `act` (active-voices source); everything else is private to SxMod.

### 11.7 `sx-warp.js` → `SxWarp` and `sx-filt2.js` → `SxFilt2` (D3)
`SxWarp`: `META` (dom/avail/speed kind per mode), `Slots(maxUni = 16)` (per oscillator per voice: filter/stomp/PD-self/delay-line states for 2 slots), `chunk(slots, k, mode, w[16], v, fm, fi[16], fs)` (per-chunk coefficients), **block kernels** (B18; one call per unison voice per chunk, each a monomorphic loop chosen by `mode` once per call, no per-sample cross-script calls):
- `incBlock(slots, k, i, mode, fm, w, m, mStride, inc, out: Float64Array(CH), n)` — F stage: `out[s] = inc[s]·g(m[s·mStride])`;
- `phaseBlock(slots, k, i, mode, w, v, remapTab, rb, m, mStride, phIn: Float64Array(CH), phOut: Float64Array(CH), win: Float64Array(CH) | null, n)` — P stages (also used with the intra-period phase of §6.6.10 and the warp delay line of §6.10.7); `win` receives the Sync window;
- `outBlock(slots, k, i, mode, w, v, m, mStride, x: Float64Array(CH), x2: Float64Array(CH) | null, ph: Float64Array(CH), n)` — O stages in place on the read samples (`x2` = the Odd/Even second read);
- `speed(mode, w, fm)`, `renderWarp2(frame, slots, opts)` (main thread).
The callers (serum-dsp WT path, SxSample, SxGran, SxSpec) read only the precomputed `Float64Array(CH)` buffers in their own read loops.
`SxFilt2`: `make(type, fs) → {set(fcL, fcR, res, drive, vr), setXY(x, y), setPZ(c: Float64Array(12)), tick(xl, xr) (writes yl, yr), process(L, R, off, n), reset(), setRate(fs), bytes}` for types 96–107 (`setXY`/`setPZ` are called by SFilter once per chunk for PZ SVF; `c` = the 4 corners × `[lp, bp, hp]` of `CP.pz`; other families ignore them), `impulse(type, fc, res, drive, vr, fs, len, x, y, pz)`. The **FX Filter** module has no X/Y or corner data: it uses `PZ_DEFAULT` with X = Y = 0 (i.e. the TL corner, a low-pass).

### 11.8 Oscillator type scripts (D4, D5) — the common oscillator contract
Every non-wavetable oscillator class (`SxSample.SampleOsc`, `SxSample.MultiOsc`, `SxGran.GranOsc`, `SxSpec.SpecOsc`) implements:
```
new X(voice, o)                  // no audio buffers: Sample/Multi/Granular allocate per-voice state on the first apply that selects
                                 // their type (Granular: Float64Array(256·16) grain state); Spectral holds only stream indices
apply(cp, o, qr)                 // copy CP fields of oscillator o (ids, options); allocation allowed (Spectral: the processor-level
                                 // stream pool of qr.specStreams streams is created/resized here, owned by sx, §6.10.1)
noteOn(ctx, fresh, legato)       // ctx: OscCtx (below); start playheads, draws from the voice's r2 stream (voice.rnd2(), §6.2)
noteOff()
render(ctx, OL, OR, RAW)         // ADDS nothing: writes ctx.CH samples into OL/OR (Float64Array(32)); RAW (Float64Array(16·32) | null)
                                 // receives the per-unison-voice mono raw output when the oscillator is a modulator
kill()
active() → bool                  // false once a one-shot ended (the voice may skip it)
grains() → int                   // granular: active grains (others 0)
```
`OscCtx` (owned and filled per chunk by the voice): `{o, N, f: Float64Array(16) (Hz per unison voice incl. warps' F stage base), gl, gr, dgl, dgr: Float64Array(16) (unison gain ramps incl. level/pan/blend/compensation/keyboard gate), pan: Float64Array(16), t: Float64Array(16) (unison slots), w1, w2: Float64Array(16), mode1, mode2, var1, var2, fm1, fm2, mod1, mod2 (Float64Array refs), modStride1, modStride2, eff (Float64Array(429)), cp, sx, gate, fresh, CH, fs, base, bpm, keyRef (MIDI float for key tracking), rootShift (S2 C3 rule), wbuf: {inc, ph, ph2, win, x, x2: Float64Array(CH)} (scratch for the SxWarp block kernels)}`.

### 11.9 `sx-seq.js` → `SxSeq` (D6)
`Seq` per §7.7 (incl. `merge(part)` and `cmd('latch', on)`). There is no main-thread simulation (MIDI OUT runs inside the worklet scope, §7.9).

### 11.10 `serum-dsp.js` → `SerumDSP` v2 (D1)
Phase-1 exports with: `VER` 2; `tables` (wavetables, Phase 1); `attach`, `begin`, `idle`, `transport`, `reset`, `fxMods`, `snapshot` (v2), `warmUp` (constructor warm-up: the Phase-1 set plus the light new code — warp kernels, filter types 96–107, sample and multisample oscillators, LFO types; total ≤ 1.5 × the Phase-1 constructor warm-up time, measured by a test), `warmStart/warmTick/warmEnd` (Phase-1 first-use warm-up) extended with **lazy** warm-ups of the heavy engines — Granular, Spectral (stream pool, FFT), Convolve, S2 reverbs — queued the first time an `apply`/`set` in that processor selects them and run through `SxBase.warmTick` at ≤ 1 ms per render quantum (live only; offline renders skip warm-ups, Phase 1), `mergeCT(part)` (CT parts, §4.1), `recentPitch(proc) → MIDI float`, `SFilter` (0–107), `filterImpulse` (0–107), `renderWarp` (Phase 1), `SerumVoice`:
```
new SerumVoice(proc, i)
apply(p)
noteOn(n, vel01, legato, glc, ch = 0, ex = null)     // ex: Float64Array(3) expression x, y, z of generated notes or null
noteOff(rv)
kill()
render(O, N)                                         // O = proc.sxOut {L, R, dL, dR, b1L, b1R, b2L, b2R}: OS-rate Float64Arrays; voices ADD
render(bufL, bufR, N, dirL, dirR)                    // Phase-1 signature (tests, tools): main → bufL/bufR, direct → dirL/dirR or main, buses → main
```

### 11.11 `fx-mods2.js` → `FXMods2` and `fx-dsp.js` → `FXDSP` v2 (F1)
`FXMods2`: `Bode`, `Convolve` (adopts shaped-IR entries; head FIR + middle/tail partitioned engine, §8.4), `Utility`, `RevVintage`, `RevNitrous`, `RevBasin`, `XoverLH`, `XoverLMH`, `MS`; Overdrive's staging (per-stage LP) around `SxBase.shape(16, …)`. The IR shaping chain is **not** in the worklet: it is `smp.irShape` (§11.12), run in `smpwork` and on the main thread for the IR display. `FXDSP`: `VER` 2, `FXRack` (§8.1), `distCurve(mode 0–17, drive, xsA, xsB, n, stages)`, `eqResponse(fxp, instIndex, effN, freqs, fs)`, `delayFilterResponse` (Phase 1), `splitResponse(type, f1, f2, freqs, fs)`, `MODFX_HZ`, `filterImpulse` (re-export).

### 11.12 Asset modules (S1)
`smp.js` (pure, node- and worker-importable): `sniff(bytes)`, `readAiff(buf)`, `resample(L, R, from, to)`, `rootFromName(name)`, `rootFromWav(wav)`, `parseSfz(text)`, `zipEntries(buf, inflate)`, `applyOps(rec, ops)`, `sampleLevels(L, R, len, maxLevels)`, `odf(L, R, rate)`, `slicesFromOdf(odf, thr, rate, len)`, `specAnalyse(L, R, rate)`, `irPrepare(rec, rate)`, `irShape(rec, {size, tone, minph, att, dec, damp}, convMax) → {L, R}` (§8.4 chain), `irPartition(L, R, rate) → shaped-IR entry fields` (§4.5), `wtAsSample(frames) → record` (§9.4.8), `loopSearch(L, R, rate, from, to, f0)`, `encodeAsset(rec)`, `decodeAsset(obj)`, `assetHash(L, R)`, `LIMITS`. `smpgen.js`: `GEN_VER`, `FACTORY_MULTI`, `FACTORY_SAMPLES`, `FACTORY_IRS`, `generate(id) → record` (procedural), `renderPlan(id) → {model, preset, notes, mix}` (render-farm items), `multiFromRenders(plan, renders)`, `sampleFromRenders(plan, renders)`, generator primitives (`glottal`, `karplus`, `imageSource`, `velvetTail`, `cabIR`, `spring`). `smpstore.js` §9.2. `smpwork.js`, `smprender.js` protocols §9.4.1, §10.1. **`assets.js`** (browser, split out of main.js; B19): `createAssetManager({ctxOf, post, worker, stores})` → `{ensure(needs, ctx) → Promise, entries(ids, rate) → Promise<entry[]>` (for `processorOptions.smps`), `release(trackId)`, `meter() → {bytes, budget}`, `host.smp` (§11.15)}` — the only module that talks to `smpwork`/`smprender`/`smpstore`.

### 11.13 `wt.js` deltas (W; inv B11)
`SUB_TABLES` + `sub:Triangle`; `FACTORY_TABLES` + §10.5; `noiseLevels(…, maxLevels)`; draw tool `mirror` (draws the stroke and its mirror image about the frame centre, `y[2047 − i] = −y[i]`); ops `add.reduceTo` (keep N evenly spaced frames, `ctx.n` 2–256), `all.spectraFromOther`/`all.phasesFromOther` and `all.subtractOther` take `ctx.other` = the chosen oscillator's table (menu "from OSC A/B/C"); FFT ops `repeatGroup` (bins 1…bin copied periodically up to bin 1023) and `nudgePhase(bin)` (Shift-Cmd/Ctrl-click: phase of that bin + 1/64); `estimatePeriod(wav) → samples` (YIN on the first channel, for FREQUENCY ESTIMATION); stereo import option `channel` chosen by the target oscillator (A/C ← L, B ← R); `.256` export and the rest Phase 1.

### 11.14 Browser modules (U1–U5)
| Module | New exports |
|---|---|
| `wk.js` (U5) | `fader(o)` (vertical dB fader + meter, `data-dest`), `meter(o)`, `xyPad(o)` (dot with X/Y bindings, drop target), `rangeBar(o)` (two-handle range for key/velocity maps; drag edges or body; double-tap to type), `pianoKeys(o)` (multi-touch on-screen keyboard: note on/off callbacks, velocity from the vertical touch position, highlight sets, scale shading), `stepGrid(o)` (Voice Control sequencer cells), `iconSeg(o)`, `modifierBar(o)` (touch modifier latches Fine/Alt/Shift, §13.1), `dragSource(el, payload)` (long-press drag on touch), `longPress` (exported), `zoomPan(el, o)` (pinch/two-finger pan for canvases) |
| `graph.js` (U5) | `PathEditor` (2-D LFO path: points anywhere, closed/open toggle, curvature handles), `CurveEditor` kinds `mcurve` (matrix editable curve with RISE/FALL fields, a **link** toggle for SmoothLink (`slink`: moving one field moves both) and Init), `spec` (log-frequency axis, GRID field, presets menu, snap/marquee, Alt-drag moves all curves), `PZEditor` (4 corner mini-editors + X/Y pad), `EnvEditor` with Start/End handles (ENV 2–4), BPM grid mode, `lfoEditorLarge(container, o)` (the LFO Editor overlay: same editor at full size with drawing tools Point/Flat/Ramp Up/Ramp Down) |
| `sx-ui-osc.js` (U2) | `OscPanel(root, kit, o)` (and `FilterPanel(root, kit, k)`, `SubPanel`, `NoisePanel` — extracted from serum-ui in M1a) with type sub-panels `WTView`, `MultiView`, `SampleView`, `GranView`, `SpecView`; pure helpers `markerLayout`, `zoneMapLayout`, `sliceAt`, `xyToN` |
| `sx-ui-mix.js` (U3) | `MixerPage(root, host)`; pure helpers `channelStrips(s, fx)`, `routeMenuItems(s, ch)` |
| `fx-ui.js` (U3) | `FXRackUI` v2 options `{embedded, dsp, racks: 1 | 3, mod: true}`; pure helpers `listRows(fx)`, `addMenuItems(fx, rack, branch)`, `moveTarget(fx, dragId, dropRow)` |
| `sx-ui-seq.js` (U4) | `ArpPage(root, host)`, `ClipPage(root, host)`, `KeyboardPanel(root, host)`, `OscMapEditor(root, host)`, `PianoRoll(root, o)` (clip notes, lanes, fold, scale shading, grid, zoom, record overlay); pure helpers `rollHit`, `laneValueAt`, `foldRows(notes, key, scale, mode)` |
| `serum-ui.js` (U1) | `SerumUI` v2 (pages §13.2), `PresetBrowser` v2 (§13.11), pure helpers of Phase 1 extended (`haloArcs` with dynamic ids, `knobMenuItems` with Aux Source/Edit Custom Curve/Unlock (module-locked controls, §13.3), `dropRow` 64, `lfoTileCount` 10, `captureDue` 3 osc), `undoStack(limit)`, **`makeKit(ui)`** (below). The Phase-1 local `initModule`/`clipModule`/`pasteModule` helpers delegate to serum-core's `initModule`/`copyModule` (§11.1) |

**Binding kit (normative, B19 — the U1/U2–U4 boundary).** Sub-panels never receive the main.js host directly; serum-ui passes them a kit built by `makeKit(ui)`:
`kit = { host, s(), fx(), snap(), knob(id, o) → wk knob bound to destination id (halo, drag-to-modulate drop target, knob menu, MIDI learn, locks, typed entry, tooltips), menu(id, items) / toggle(id) / seg(id) (the same bindings for enum/bool destinations), dropTarget(el, id) (any element as a modulation drop target), edit(fn, label) / editFx(fn, label) (state edits through the panel's undo and `host.edit`), notify(text), prefs() }`. `host` is the main.js host (for `host.smp`, `host.tracks`, …); everything that touches modulation, undo or locks goes through the kit. U1 owns `makeKit`; the stubs in `tests/serum-ui-stubs/` mirror it.

### 11.15 Host contract additions (`SerumUI` host; implemented by main.js)
```js
host.smp = {
  list: (kind /* 'sample' | 'multi' | 'ir' */) => [{ id, name, cat, folder, user, seconds, ch }],   // 'sample' also lists
                                                   // the wavetables (cat 'Wavetables/<category>', §9.4.8) and user folder 'Renders'
  import: (files /* FileList | File[] */, { kind, osc }) => Promise<id | null>,   // §9.3; notices on errors
  overview: (id, n) => Promise<Float32Array(2n)>,                                  // min/max peaks for displays
  spectrogram: (id, w, h) => Promise<{ w, h, data: Uint8ClampedArray }>,          // dB image (worker)
  odf: (id) => Promise<Float32Array>,                                              // onset function for slicing UI
  record: (id) => Promise<record | null>,                                          // root, loop, len, rate (no PCM transfer)
  pcm: (id) => Promise<{ rate, ch, L, R } | null>,                                // canonical PCM (Switch to Wavetable, Send to Noise)
  rename: (id, name) => Promise, del: (id) => Promise<bool>, users: (id) => [trackName],
  saveFrom: (oscIndex, kind) => Promise<id>,                                       // "Switch to Single Sample": the recent voice's zone sample
};
host.tracks = () => [{ id, name, model }];        // MIDI OUT target menus
host.seqCmd = (cmd, arg) => void;                 // {t: 'seqcmd'} to the track's node (§12.4)
host.onInput = (fn | null) => void;               // live input notes of this track (clip recording)
host.preview = (presetKey | null, clipNotes?) => void;   // preset browser preview (§12.6); assets by id through the asset manager
host.renderLast = ({ shift }) => Promise<void>;   // header wave icon (§13.2.5): render + download the last note group; shift: preset JSON
host.clipToTrack = (trackId, notes) => void;      // CLIP slot "Copy to Track ▸" (§7.5.6), one project-history step
host.download = (name, blob) => void;              // shared download helper (clip SMF, renders, presets)
host.tuningFile = () => Promise<{ name, kind, scl, kbm, tab } | null>;   // file picker + tuning.js
host.renderWarp = (o) => Promise<void>;           // Render OSC Warp v2 (§13.3.1), main-thread
host.resample = (target /* 'A' | 'B' | 'C' | 'AB' | 'sel' */) => Promise<void>;
```
`FXRackUI` host gains `racks: () => 1 | 3`, `irs: host.smp` (Convolve), `note` (none). All Phase-1 host members stay.

---

## 12. Engine and main-thread integration (owner I)

### 12.1 Loading
`addModules(ctx)` requests scripts 1–11 of §0.3 concurrently (`Promise.allSettled`, failures logged) and then `engine.js` (12 sequential awaits cost every `OfflineAudioContext` — bounce, resample — a noticeable delay). `wk.loadDSP` injects the list in table order on the main thread. The render farm `importScripts` the same list.

### 12.2 Processor fields (additions)
`sxOut = {L, R, dL, dR, b1L, b1R, b2L, b2R}` (Float64Array(128·OS) each; reallocated by `setOS`), one decimator chain per array (built lazily when `CP.outMask` first needs the pair), base-rate scratch for the six extra channels, tail buffers ×8 for `fadeSerum`, `mpe = Float64Array(17·4)`, `sxSeq` (SxSeq.Seq or null; the Phase-1 303 field `sq` is unrelated), `ext` (MIDI OUT ring: Float64Array(256·4) + read/write indices, §7.9), `trackId` (from `processorOptions`, registered in `PROCS`), `seqMacro = Float64Array(8).fill(NaN)`, `vcN`, `rr = Uint16Array(512)`, `lastNote` (for FX key tracking of other models).

### 12.3 Notes and allocation
- `noteOn(n, v, ch)` / `noteOff(n, rv, ch)`: Serum with `seq.active` → `sxSeq.input`; else `sxNoteOn`/`sxNoteOff`. `proc.sxNote(on, n, vel, ch, x, y, z, gen)` = the allocator entry used by the sequencer.
- Poly allocation over `voices[0…nUse−1]` (Phase 1) with: same-note reuse when `oneNote` and the same `(n, ch)`; candidates in order (1) inactive (lowest stamp), (2) not gated (lowest stamp), (3) sustain-held (lowest stamp), (4) gated by `steal`: Newest → lowest stamp · Oldest → highest stamp · Highest → lowest note · Lowest → highest note · Velocity → lowest velocity (ties: lowest stamp). Mono: Phase 1 (`voices[0]`).
- Poly legato (`legato` with MONO off): the allocator passes `legato = false` as in Phase 1; the voice detects poly legato itself and copies its state from the most recent gated voice (§6.2).
- Sustain (CC64) while the eff `arp.on`: forwarded to `sxSeq.cmd('latch', down ? 1 : 0)` instead of holding voices (§7.4.5).
- `rack.trigger()` on every voice note-on (generated notes included).

### 12.4 Messages (additions to Phase-1 §11.6)
| Direction | Message | Handling |
|---|---|---|
| main → worklet | `{t: 'on', n, v, ch?}` / `{t: 'off', n, rv?, ch?}` | `ch` = MIDI channel when the track receives MPE |
| main → worklet | `{t: 'mpe', ch, k: 'b' | 'y' | 'z' | 'x', v}` | `proc.mpe[ch·4 + {0, 1, 2, 3}]` (bend −1…1, y CC74/127, z pressure/127, x CC10 mapped to −1…1, §6.17) |
| main → worklet | `{t: 'seqcmd', cmd, arg}` | `sxSeq.cmd` (arp/clip launch/stop from the UI) |
| main → worklet | `{t: 'smp', …}`, `{t: 'smpdel', id}` | §9.5 |
| main → worklet | `{t: 'bye'}` | unregister from `PROCS` before the node is disconnected (§7.9) |
| main → worklet | `{t: 'ct', part}`, `{t: 'sqt', part}` | CT / seqT parts (§4.1, §4.4) when only tables changed |
| worklet → main | `{t: 'smon', s, gr, fxph}` | snapshot v2 (§6.20), `gr` Float32Array(4·48), `fxph` Float64Array(48) |
| processorOptions | `smps`, `trackId`, `seq`/`seqT` inside `patch` | §9.6, §7.9 |

### 12.5 Serum span v2 (`renderSerum`)
1. Sequencer events are handled by the span splitting in `process()` (`sxSeq.next` together with `songEvents` and the MIDI OUT ring `ext`; `sxSeq.fire` at the span start).
2. Clear the arrays of `sxOut` in `CP.outMask`; mix the de-click tails.
3. `SerumDSP.begin(this, N)`; active voices `render(this.sxOut, N)`; warm-up tick (Phase 1).
4. `rack.setMods(SerumDSP.fxMods(this), CP.fxKey)`; `rack.setNote(SerumDSP.recentPitch(this))`.
5. Decimate each used pair; OS fade on every used channel.
6. `rack.process(Lb, Rb, n, dLb?, dRb?, b1Lb?, b1Rb?, b2Lb?, b2Rb?)` (without FXDSP: Phase-1 fallback adds direct and applies master/bypass, reading them at **FX-ctx [0]/[1]** of the v2 FXP base — the Phase-1 code's `fb[86]` bypass index is migrated; buses are dropped).
7. Output stage (Phase 1).
Other models: Phase-1 path unchanged except `rack.process` signature (extra args omitted) and `rack.setNote(lastNote)`.

### 12.6 main.js
- **Patches**: `buildPatch` = `patchFor(model, vals, fx, {sr, bpm, drift, hiss, qr, prefs})` (models.js, §10.1) + live-session fields; for Serum `G` carries `fx`, `qr`, `smooth`, `ultra`, `mpeBendX`, `mpeYBi` (the app preferences, §13.10) to `compileSerum`; `patch.fxr = compileFx(fx, {…, rate, master, bypass, direct, bus1v, bus2v, modded: fxModded2(s, fx)})`; `patch.needs` = Serum needs ∪ FX needs. `sendPatch` sends only the CT/seqT parts and FXT whose keys changed for that node (§4.1, §4.4). `prefsChanged()` recompiles and re-sends every Serum track; bounces compile with the current preferences.
- **Mandatory `>= NS` migrations** (§3.11.1): `sxLoad`'s locked-FX filter and `midiMapCC`'s FX branch use `isFxId`; the `sxUni` safeguard (which indexed `patch.serum.base` by `DEST_INDEX` and missed `c.uni`) is retired — the unison cap travels as `CP.qr[0]` and the DSP applies it to A, B and C.
- **Module split (B19)**: the asset manager and `host.smp` live in `assets.js` (S1), Web MIDI/MPE routing and MIDI learn dispatch in `midi.js` (I2); main.js wires them.
- **Assets**: the generalised manager (§9.5) replaces `tables`; `ensureAssets(patch.needs)` before posting a patch; factory generation progress notices.
- **Project load**: `normalizeFx(r.fx, refs = the track's Serum state)`; Serum `vals.s` through `normalizeSerum` (migration); embedded `wts` and `smps` restored then dropped.
- **MIDI** (`midi.js`): a track whose Serum state has `global.mpe.on` receives every channel of its input (MPE zone: channel 1 master, 2–16 members): notes carry `ch`; pitch bend on a member channel → `{t: 'mpe', k: 'b'}`; CC74 → `k: 'y'`; channel pressure on a member → `k: 'z'`; **CC10 on a member channel → `k: 'x'`** with `v = cc ≤ 64 ? (cc − 64)/64 : (cc − 64)/63` (channel 1: Phase-1 bend/AT; CC10 on channel 1 is not an MPE message). MPE off: Phase-1 routing. Program change, MIDI learn: Phase 1 (dynamic FX ids allowed as learn targets; the FX/synth branch uses `isFxId`).
- **MIDI OUT**: nothing on the main thread except passing `trackId` in `processorOptions` and sending `{t: 'bye'}` before disconnecting a node (§7.9).
- **Storage (B21)**: project autosave, user presets (`synth.user.serum`), arp/clip banks and patterns, VC and FX module presets move from localStorage to the IndexedDB database **`synth-app`** (`appstore.js`, owner I: stores `kv` (key → JSON) and `tracks` (project autosave as one record per track, the serialisation History already uses per track, plus a header record), so a 400 ms autosave rewrites only changed tracks). On first run the existing localStorage keys are copied over (then left in place as a fallback). If IndexedDB is unavailable the Phase-1 localStorage paths are used; every quota error (IDB or localStorage) shows the notice "Storage full — the project was not saved" (no silent failure as in Phase-1 `setRaw`). `navigator.storage.persist()` is requested at the first user-asset import (§9.3).
- **Last-note render (§13.2.5)**: per Serum track main.js keeps the last **input** note group (on-screen keys, MIDI, auditions): a group starts at a note-on while no input note of the track is held and ends at the last note-off; ≤ 16 notes `{n, vel, on, off}` (seconds, relative to the first note-on).
- **Bounce**: Serum tracks render at `min(CP.os, osCap)` or 4 when the preference "Use Ultra quality when rendering" is on (B24.1).
- **Resample to** (B25): `target` A/B/C/AB/sel — renders 1 bar at the song tempo (Phase-1 recipe) and imports into the chosen oscillator(s) (AB: L → A, R → B).
- **Preset preview** (B25): `host.preview(key, notes)` creates a dedicated `AudioWorkletNode` (`processorOptions` with the preset's patch, **no** assets: its `needs` are ensured through the asset manager in the live context, §9.6) connected to the master, plays the preset's preview clip (or fallback clip i of 3: a chord C–E–G–B 2 beats, a bass line, a lead phrase — `seq-core FALLBACK_CLIPS`) for ≤ 4 s, then disconnects; one preview node at a time.
- **CPU safeguard** (§14.3): new levers after `sxCap`: `qr` levels (the unison cap is `qr.uniMax`).
- **Undo**: knob tweaks stay out of the project history (Phase 1); the panel's own undo (§13.2.4) covers them; `applyRestore(keepSound)` keeps `fx` (Phase 1).

### 12.7 models.js
Serum model: tag `Poly · 3 oscillators (wavetable, multisample, sample, granular, spectral) · 2 filters · mixer & buses · 13 FX + splitters · 10 LFOs · 64-slot matrix · arp & clips`; `about` text updated (original wording, no Xfer branding beyond the model's historical name); `defaults(prefs)` applies `defOs`, `author`, `view3d`, `mpeDefault`; `map(P, v, G)` = `compileSerum(v.s, {bpm: G.bpm, smooth: G.smooth, fx: G.fx, qr: G.qr, ultra: G.ultra, mpeBendX: G.mpeBendX, mpeYBi: G.mpeYBi})`. New pure export **`patchFor(model, vals, fx, {sr, bpm, drift = 0, hiss = 0, qr, prefs})`** → the complete engine patch of a track (model `map` + `compileFx` + `bpm`/`osCap`-free fields) — used by main.js, `assets.js` (render farm), `smpgen` plans and the tests (B15).

---

## 13. UI specification (U1–U5; iPad/touch first-class)

### 13.1 Look and input (delta on Phase-1 §9.1)
- Phase-1 tokens plus `--sx-osc-a #4fb3ff`, `--sx-osc-b #6fe3a1`, `--sx-osc-c #f7c948`, `--sx-sub #a78bfa`, `--sx-noise #94a3b8`, `--sx-f1 #ff8a5b`, `--sx-f2 #ff5b8a`, `--sx-bus1 #c084fc`, `--sx-bus2 #f472b6`, `--sx-arp #b07cff`, `--sx-clip #3ddc84` (channel colours used consistently on panels, mixer strips, routing buttons and halos of routing destinations). Original artwork only.
- **Touch (`@media (pointer: coarse)` or any touch event seen)**: targets ≥ 44 px (knobs `md` minimum), `touch-action: none` on controls and `manipulation` on containers (no double-tap zoom), pointer capture on drags, no hover-only information (tooltips appear in a status line while touching a control — VOICE's, in the parts view; the bundled panel has none, since a line that appears above the oscillators on every touch pushes the panel down), long-press 500 ms = context menu (Phase 1), double-tap = typed entry with `inputmode="decimal"`, tile drag-to-modulate starts after a 250 ms hold (so a swipe scrolls), pinch-zoom and two-finger pan on sample displays, spectrograms, the piano roll, the arp graph and the LFO editor.
- **Modifier bar** (`wk.modifierBar`, shown in the header on touch devices): latching **Fine** (= Shift drag), **Alt** (Alt-drag/-click gestures: copy module, modulation depth, snap), **Shift** (multi-select, step draw) — each latch applies to the next gesture and releases (double-tap = lock). Every modifier gesture of S2 has a menu equivalent as well.
- Canvases: device-pixel ratio capped at 2; animations ≤ 30 fps (10 fps with `prefers-reduced-motion`); hidden pages are not painted; displays redraw only on change or snapshot.
- Keyboard (hardware): Phase-1 parity rules; new shortcuts: Cmd/Ctrl-Z / Shift-Cmd/Ctrl-Z (panel undo), Alt/Option-F (expand FX/matrix), piano-roll shortcuts (§13.8), preset browser keys 7/8/9 (§13.11).

### 13.2 Page structure
| Area | Content |
|---|---|
| Header | **wave icon** (render-last-note, §13.2.5) · logo · tabs **OSC · MIX · FX · MATRIX · ARP · CLIP · GLOBAL** · preset area (name → browser, ◀ ▶, save, ARTIST/DESC button) · **Undo / Redo** · Menu · modifier bar (touch) · voice counter `x / y` (grains included) · MASTER knob + output meter |
| OSC page | row A: [SUB over NOISE] [OSC A] [OSC B] [OSC C] [FILTER 1 over FILTER 2] · row B: tile strip · row C: [MACROS 1–8] [ENV 1–4 editor] [LFO / VELO / NOTE editor] [VOICING] |
| MIX page | mixer strips (§13.4) · row B/C as OSC |
| FX page | rack tabs + list + rack (§13.5) · row B/C |
| MATRIX page | 64-row matrix (§13.6) · row B |
| ARP / CLIP pages | §13.7 / §13.8 (own lower layout; macros pane toggle) |
| GLOBAL page | §13.10 |
| Bottom (all pages) | **Keyboard panel** (§13.9), collapsible to a 40 px strip |
| Overlays | WT editor, LFO Editor, preset browser, OSC MAPPING editor, pattern editor, curve editors (`role=dialog`, Escape/✕ closes, full screen below 1100 px) |

Breakpoints: **≥ 1366 px** full layout; **1100–1365** (iPad Pro 11″ landscape 1194 px): oscillator panels with `sm` knobs, FILTER 1/2 as two tabs; **768–1099** (iPad portrait): OSC row shows one oscillator at a time (tabs A · B · C with colour dots and power LEDs) next to SUB/NOISE and a filter tab pair; tile strip scrolls horizontally; row C becomes 2 × 2; **< 768**: single column (Phase-1 phone rules).

#### 13.2.4 Panel undo/redo (deviation 16)
`undoStack(100)`: a snapshot `{s, fx}` (JSON) is pushed at the end of every non-UI edit gesture (gestures with the same label within 600 ms coalesce), at preset loads, conversions and module operations; ≤ 16 MB (oldest dropped). Undo/redo restore through `host.edit`/`host.editFx` (whole-state replacement, label "Undo"). Model changes or track switches clear the stack.

#### 13.2.5 Render last note / drag-export (inv A2.1/B25 header wave icon; deviation 21)
The wave icon left of the logo (hover on desktop, always visible on touch) renders the track's last played note or chord (main.js keeps the last input note group, §12.6) — "Serum silently plays the note or chord using the corresponding pitch, velocity, and duration":
- `host.renderLast()` renders the group in an `OfflineAudioContext` (stereo, the live context rate) through the track's **current** patch, FX rack and assets (`patchFor` + `assets.entries`), with the sequencer active (an arp/clip regenerates its output from the recorded input exactly as live when it started idle); duration = last note-off + a tail that ends when the output stays below −80 dBFS for 50 ms, at most 10 s after the last note-off.
- Output: a 24-bit WAV (song.js `encodeWAV(…, 24)`) named `<preset name> <lowest note name>.wav` (app naming, C4 = 60). It is **downloaded** (click/tap; desktop drag uses the `DownloadURL` drag type where supported) and **stored** as a user sample (`smp:` record with `folder: 'Renders'`), which the Sample/Granular/Spectral menus list under **Renders**.
- Alt-click (touch: modifier Alt) opens the Sample menu of the selected oscillator at the Renders folder ("opens Renders"); Shift-click (or Shift-drag) downloads the current preset as JSON (`{name, v: {s}, fx}` + embedded user assets, §9.8).
- Nothing to render (no note played yet on this track) ⇒ notice "Play a note first".
- Integration test: the render of a recorded group equals a bounce of the same notes (same patch, same frames) within 1e-6.

### 13.3 OSC page
Every control bound to a destination carries `data-dest` (drop target, knob menu, MIDI learn) — Phase-1 rule, including new menus (`k.w1`, `s.shp`, `f2.type` …).

| Panel | Controls (inventory) |
|---|---|
| OSC A/B/C header (B1, B2) | power (`k.on`); name = drag handle (plain drag onto another oscillator header = **swap** incl. mods; Alt = copy; Shift-Alt = copy with mods; touch: long-press menu "Copy · Copy (w/mods) · Paste · Swap with ▸"); **type menu** (Wavetable · Multisample · Sample · Granular · Spectral); **routing button** → popover: seg Filter/Main/Direct/None, Filter: balance knob `k.fbal` (F1 ← → F2), Main/Direct: ENV toggle, BUS 1 / BUS 2 knobs (`k.bus1/2`); module menu: Lock Module, Init Module (`initModule`, also removes the module's modulations), Copy, Copy (w/mods), Paste (allowed pairs §11.1), ✓ Enable Pitch Tracking (`k.track`), ✓ Pitch Bend Tracking (shown when tracking is off on Sample/Granular/Spectral), Edit Note Ranges…, type-specific switches (§ below). Inside a locked module every control's knob menu offers **Unlock Control** / **Lock Control** (adds/removes its id in `locks.unp`; unlocked controls show no lock tint) |
| Pitch row (B2) | OCT, SEM (right-click/long-press: Semitones · Harmonics · Ratio · Step (disabled without `.scl`); in Harmonics mode SEM shows `×h` / `÷k`, format `semiH`); Ratio mode replaces OCT/SEM by SRC menu (Note/A/B/C ≠ self) + RATIO knob (`k.ratio`, typed `3:2`); FIN, CRS; HZ knob (`k.hz`, small, in the pitch row's overflow ⋯) |
| Wavetable view (B3) | Phase-1 WT panel; **WARP 1** menu ◀ ▶ + knob, **WARP 2** menu ◀ ▶ + knob (menus grouped by category, unavailable entries greyed, FM category items Thru-Zero/Exp/Linear as radio items for that slot, item Swap Warps); Var fader under a slot set to Sync (`k.wvar1/2`); WT POS menu ✓ Smooth Interpolation; PHASE menu ✓ Phase Legato, New MemTrig phase set; phase-memory menu (All Voices · Contiguous · Per Voice); UNISON field + gear popover (MODE, STACK, WIDTH, RANGE, WT POS, WARP 1, WARP 2) with DETUNE/BLEND on the panel; 2D view shows both slots' effect for Sync/Alt/Distortion (via `renderWarp2`). The oscillator **display** is a drop target for an LFO tile with Alt/modifier (A, B **and C**): `lfoToWavetable(l, o)` (Phase 1 extended to o = 0–2) — on a non-wavetable oscillator the drop also switches its type to `wt` (one undo step) |
| Multisample view (B5) | instrument menu (Factory folders, User, Load SFZ…, Import ZIP…, Rename/Delete user; ◀ ▶ and mouse wheel step through the current folder — the Phase-1 table-menu rule, inv B2), **zone display** (x = key 0–127, y = velocity; zone rectangles; layers of the recent voice highlighted from `snapshot.zone`; tap = waveform of the last-played zone), envelope button → pane: OVERRIDE toggle, DELAY (menu BPM Sync), A, H, D, S, R knobs + draggable envelope graph; VEL TRACK, RAND (numBoxes), TIMBRE knob; UNISON/DETUNE/BLEND/gear; WARP 1/2; PAN; LEVEL; menu: Switch to Single Sample, Switch to Wavetable ▸ (import modes) |
| Sample view (B6) | sample menu (Factory folders, **Wavetables ▸** (tables as samples, §9.4.8), User, **Renders**, Load Sample…, Reload Sample (§9.4.2), Rename/Delete, Embed badge; ◀ ▶ and wheel step within the current folder); **waveform display** (stereo min/max overview; S/E markers white, LS/LE blue (drag, drag between = move both), X-fade handle (drag up), slices yellow (Auto: threshold line; Manual: Alt-click/long-press adds/removes), playheads and animated markers from `snapshot.smpPos` when "Show Marker Animation"; zoom: drag up/down (or pinch), pan: drag left/right); context menu: Show Marker Animation, Zoom to Start and End, Snap Off/to Zero/to Beats/to Loop, Fade Edges ▸ (None, 1–128 ms), Normalize, Reverse, Trim, Slicing Off/Auto/Manual (Auto → Manual keeps the auto marks as the manual starting point), and with slicing: Play Slice to End, Play Single Slice, Root Note ▸ (C−1…C8), Send to Selected Clip (n), ✓ Auto-Sync to Clip (`slice.sync`, §7.5.6) — the slicing items are disabled while Manual X\|Y is on; loop menu (One-shot, Fwd, Rev, Fwd/Rwd, Tailed, ✓ Relative Loop, ✓ Link Loop Length, ✓ Exit Loop on Release); LS/LE numBoxes; SCAN knob (menu: Range ±200/400/800 %, Reverse (`k.scanrev`), Lock Scan Rate, Sample Length to BPM → the knob is relabelled **RATE** and bound to `k.lenrate` (`LEN_LADDER` labels, modulatable, a drop target); the sign comes from Reverse); UNISON + gear (adds START, SPAN); WARP 1/2; PAN; LEVEL; Switch to Wavetable ▸ |
| Granular view (B7) | Sample view display and menus + loop menu adds Loop Grains, Manual (X\|Y dot replaces the playhead; the SCAN knob is relabelled **X** and bound to `k.pos`; slicing acts as Off; drop a source on the dot → "Modulate X / Modulate Y"; menu Y Axis ▸ (`XY_Y_GRAN`) and Y Modulation ▸ (`XY_MODES`)); **window button** (popover: AMOUNT + rand, SKEW + rand numBoxes, SHAPE list of 10 with icons; Alt-drag/modifier-drag on the button: x = amount, y = skew); SCAN (menu adds Key Track); DENS (menu: Free · BPM Sync ✓Triplet ✓Dotted · Grains · ✓ Jump Start · Max Grains…); LENGTH (menu: Free · BPM Sync · Percent (disabled with Grains)); OFFSET, DIR (menu ✓ Reverse Grains), PITCH, RAND LEN, RAND PAN, RAND LEVEL; UNISON + gear (START, SPAN, SPAWN PATTERN); WARP 1/2 (popover shows Rand Warp/Rand Warp 2); PAN; LEVEL |
| Spectral view (B8) | sample menu as Sample view (◀ ▶, wheel, Wavetables ▸, Renders); **spectrogram** (log-frequency, drag up/down zoom; S/E markers; Hi/Lo markers on the right edge (`k.flo`/`k.fhi`, drop targets); menu: Show Waveform Display, ✓ Smooth, ✓ Post Warp, sample ops, slicing); loop menu (+ Manual, X\|Y with `XY_Y_SPEC`; SCAN relabelled **X** → `k.pos`, slicing off); SCAN (menu adds Phase Lock, Transients); CUT; **FILTER display** (`CurveEditor` kind `spec`, GRID field; presets menu: Factory ▸, User ▸, Save…, Default; ▸ Use Wavetable as Filter (table menu) / Curve Filter); MIX; UNISON + gear (START, SPAN); WARP 1/2 (spectral and time-domain lists); PAN; LEVEL |
| SUB (B9) | power; shape menu (S2: 6 icons; S1 mode: 5); OCT; CRS; PHASE (menu ✓ Contiguous); PAN; LEVEL; routing button; module menu (Lock, Init, Copy, Paste, ✓ Enable Pitch Tracking, Edit Note Ranges…) |
| NOISE (B10) | power; noise menu (Color ▸ White/Pink/Brown/Geiger, factory categories, User, Load Sample…, Embed badge; ◀ ▶ and wheel step within the current folder); One-shot/Loop toggle; START (draggable field `n.phase`), RAND; PITCH; FINE; PAN; LEVEL; STEREO and FILTER (shown for colour noises); routing button; **module menu** (B2: Lock Module, Init Module, Copy, Copy (w/mods), Paste, ✓ Pitch Track (`noise.keytrack`), Edit Note Ranges…) |
| FILTER 1 / 2 (B12) | power; **label** = drag handle (plain drag onto the other filter's label = swap incl. mods; Alt = copy; Shift-Alt = copy with mods; touch: long-press menu) and **module menu** (B2: Lock Module, Init Module, Copy, Copy (w/mods), Paste — filter ↔ filter2 per §11.1); type menu (Normal · Multi · Flanges · Misc · New; ◀ ▶; wheel); display (drag x = cutoff, y = res; menu: Frequency Response · Frequency Response & FFT · Phase Response & FFT; Alt-click cycles; FFT from `snapshot.filtTap`); routing switches **S A B C N** (lit = that source reaches this filter: route Filter, share > 0 and switch on; tap toggles the switch and, when off-route, sets the source's route to Filter with the balance at this filter); key-track switch; CUTOFF (menu ✓ Key Track); RES; DRIVE (menu ✓ Clean Mode); VAR (per-type label; disabled when none); PAN; MIX (disabled for Combs); LEVEL slider; X/Y (PZ SVF: `PZEditor` popover); routing button (other filter / Main / Direct / None, ENV, BUS 1/2) |

#### 13.3.1 Render OSC Warp / Resample (main menu)
Render OSC Warp acts on the selected oscillator (`ui.osc`, set by tapping a panel header): 256 frames spanning "0 to 100 % of the WARP knob" (manual p.324): frame f = the oscillator's frame at its base WT POS rendered with **WARP 1 at the absolute amount f/255**; WARP 1's mode, Var, FM type and modulator (its base frame) and the whole WARP 2 slot stay at their base values (`renderWarp2`). If WARP 1 is Off, WARP 2 is swept instead (slot 1 Off). S1 mode, OSC A/B: `renderWarp` with the v1 slot-1 mode and w = f/255, slot 2 at base. Both slots Off ⇒ the item is disabled. The result is band-limited, saved as `<name> warp`, loaded into the oscillator, and the swept slot is set Off (the other slot keeps its setting). Resample to: selected oscillator, or A and B (stereo), per §12.6.

#### 13.3.2 Marker snapping and other sample-display actions (normative definitions)
- **Snap Off / to Zero / to Beats / to Loop** (`smpo.snap`): dragged S/E/LS/LE markers and manual slice marks snap — Zero: to the nearest upward zero crossing of the mono sum within ±5 ms; **Beats**: to a 1/16-note grid at the project tempo starting at S (`snapGrid`), or, with Sample Length to BPM on, to sixteenths of the region at its RATE length (`|E − S|/(4·beats)`); Loop: LS/LE snap to positions where the loop length is a whole number of root periods (`T₀`, §6.6.10) and the seam error (`loopSearch` criterion) is minimal within ±5 ms.
- **Reload Sample**: §9.4.2. **Show Marker Animation**, **Zoom to Start and End**: display only (`smpo.anim`, `smpo.zoomSE`).

VOICING panel: MONO, LEGATO, POLY (menu: Limit Same Note Poly to 1, Voice Steal Priority ▸ 5 options, lock), PORTA, CURVE, ALWAYS, SCALED, and BEND UP/DOWN bound to `g.bu2`/`g.bd2` (0…48 / −48…0) — in S1 mode to `g.bendup`/`g.benddn` (±24), kept consistent by `syncS1` (§2.6.4).

Opened from the hover/tap edit button at the top right of the oscillator display. Toolbar menu **PROCESS** is labelled **ALL** and gains Remove Low Spectra/Phases, Set Spectra/Phases from Other Osc ▸ (A/B/C), Shift to Zero-Crossing (all); ADD/REMOVE becomes a **Remove** button with "Reduce to ▸ N frames" (`add.reduceTo`); SORT gains Randomize (Phase-1 `sort.random`); draw palette gains **Mirror**; frame **COPY / PASTE** buttons (contiguous ranges; paste inserts after the current frame, ≤ 256); FFT menu gains Repeat Bin Group (Start to Cursor Pos); Shift-Cmd/Ctrl-click (touch: modifier bar Shift + Alt) on a phase bar nudges it right; IMPORT gains FREQUENCY ESTIMATION (writes `estimatePeriod` into the split-size field) and Switch OSC Type ▸ (import the file as a Sample/Granular/Spectral oscillator via `host.smp.import`); stereo files import L → A or C and R → B; EXPORT lists All as 16-bit / 32-bit WAV, Single-Cycle Waves (ZIP), 8-bit `.256`, Selection; the table name field's menu has Save Table and Rename; the formula field's preset menu has Save Formula… (Phase-1 user formulas).

### 13.14 Browser QA checklist (recorded in the PR; iPad Pro 11″/12.9″ Safari landscape and portrait, plus desktop Chrome/Safari/Firefox)
Every control of §13.3–§13.13 renders, edits (audible), shows correct text and accepts drag-to-modulate (touch hold-drag and mouse); halos on new widget types (fader, xyPad, rangeBar, menus); every new osc type loads factory content and a user file (WAV, AIFF, FLAC, MP3, SFZ+WAVs, ZIP); slicing auto/manual and Send to Clip; granular windows and X|Y; spectral filter editor and wavetable-as-filter; dual warps incl. FM types and cycle handling; routing popovers, S/A/B/C/N switches, mixer strips and meters; FX racks with buses, splitters, every new module, S1 instance conversion; arp: all shapes audibly, patterns, latch via CC64, trigger octave; clip: record overdub/extend, piano-roll shortcuts and touch gestures, macro lanes, KB SPAN modes, MIDI OUT to another track live and in a bounce; keyboard panel transpose/scale/swing, OSC MAPPING fold/warp; MPE controller (or simulator) bend/Y/Z per note; tuning file load; S1 preset load sounds identical (A/B with a Phase-1 build), S1 checkbox off/on round trip; header wave icon render/download (and drag into a DAW in Chromium), clip Export MIDI File / Copy to Track; MPE Expr X from CC10; storage persistence prompt and IndexedDB autosave after a reload; undo/redo; preset browser preview/ratings/tags/hybridize; panel performance (no long tasks > 50 ms while knob-dragging on iPad); no console errors.

---

## 14. CPU and memory budgets; quality reduction

### 14.1 CPU budgets
**Normative loads** (fraction of real time for 1 s of audio) on the reference machine recorded in `tests/bench.json` (`refMachine`, an M-class laptop; base 48 kHz, OS 2 unless stated). **Node gate**: each row's measured time × `k` must be ≤ **1.5 ×** its target, where `k = t_ref(calib)/t(calib)` normalises for the machine running the tests (`calib` = row b through `tests/ref-p1`, measured in the same run; `t_ref(calib)` is stored in `bench.json`). Each timing is the **median of 5 runs interleaved** with the comparison runs. **iPad gate**: `tests/bench.html` (owner I) runs the same scenario harness in Safari (desktop or iPad) and prints the loads; at the end of M2 the iPad Pro loads of rows d–m are recorded in the PR, and **every row must measure ≤ 0.6** on an iPad Pro (11″, M-class or A12Z) — the iPad column is the target the implementation aims for, 0.6 is the hard ceiling (M4 re-measures).

| # | Scenario (1 s of audio) | node target | iPad target |
|---|---|---|---|
| a, b, c | Phase-1 §8.4.3 budgets with v1 states through the v2 code | ≤ 1.15 × the `tests/ref-p1` time (medians of 5 interleaved runs; the test fails above 1.3×) | — |
| d | 8 voices × 3 WT osc × unison 7, W1 Bend + 50 %, W2 Tube 30 %, F1 MG Low 24 → F2 Low 12 (series), 2 LFOs, 4 matrix rows (SxWarp block kernels, §6.6.1) | ≤ 0.8 | ≤ 0.45 |
| e | 16 voices Multisample `DX Tine Piano`, unison 1 | ≤ 0.12 | ≤ 0.08 |
| f | 16 voices Sample stereo, unison 4, Fwd loop with X-fade | ≤ 0.35 | ≤ 0.2 |
| f′ | 8 voices Sample, unison 4, W1 Sync 50 % + W2 Bend+ (intra-period warps, §6.6.10) | ≤ 0.35 | ≤ 0.25 |
| g | 8 voices Granular: Free 40 Hz, length 200 ms (≈ 64 grains), Hann | ≤ 0.3 | ≤ 0.2 |
| g′ | one voice at 256 grains | ≤ 0.35 | ≤ 0.25 |
| g″ | 8 voices × 3 Granular oscillators at max density (processor cap `grainsProc` 512) | ≤ 2 × g′ | ≤ 0.5 |
| h | 4 voices Spectral, N 2048, hop 512 (High), one Smear warp | ≤ 0.25 | ≤ 0.18 |
| h′ | 8 voices Spectral, as h; worst single render quantum ≤ 3 × the mean (staggered FFTs, §6.10.1) | ≤ 0.5 | ≤ 0.35 |
| i | 64 active rows (20 with curves, 10 with slew), 8 voices — matrix + begin overhead only | ≤ 0.1 | ≤ 0.06 |
| j | FX: Convolve 4 s stereo IR; worst render quantum ≤ 3 × the mean (tail work spread, §8.4) | ≤ 0.08 | ≤ 0.05 |
| k | FX: each S2 reverb type | ≤ 0.05 | ≤ 0.03 |
| l | FX: Bode with BLUR and feedback | ≤ 0.03 | ≤ 0.02 |
| m | FX: Splitter L/M/H with 3 EQs | ≤ 0.04 | ≤ 0.03 |
| n | FX: a v1-migrated rack with all 10 S1 modules on | ≤ 1.05 × ref-p1 (medians, fails above 1.3×) | — |
| o | Sequencer: arp 1/32 at 180 BPM + 4 POLY clip playheads | ≤ 0.003 | ≤ 0.002 |
| p | idle Serum v2 track (no notes, default patch) | ≤ 0.002 (skip path) | — |
| q | warm-up: constructor `warmUp` ≤ 1.5 × Phase 1; every lazy `warmTick` ≤ 1 ms (§11.10) | — | — |

Cheap bypass (normative): unused OSC C, Filter 2, warp slot 2, buses, sequencer, MPE, tuning, VC and S2 LFO/ENV extras cost O(1) per chunk (`CP.use` bits, §0.7); a v2 patch with only S1-equivalent features costs within 15 % of Phase 1 (row a).

### 14.2 Memory budgets (worklet heap, base 48 kHz unless stated; × 4 at 192 kHz for rate-dependent buffers)
Desktop / **touch devices** (`pointer: coarse` or touch seen; main.js passes the class to every budget holder):

| Item | Budget |
|---|---|
| Serum voice without delay-family filters, WT only | ≤ 384 KB (Phase 1: 256 KB) |
| Filters (both, any type 0–107) | ≤ 2 MB per voice at base **192 kHz**; Diffusor/Comb 2 ≤ 1 MB per filter (Float32, 20 Hz floor, §6.13.1); a type change on the render path never allocates |
| + per Sample/Multisample osc | ≤ 64 KB |
| + per Granular osc | ≤ 96 KB (256 grains × 16 doubles + streams); processor grains ≤ `qr.grainsProc` 512 / **256** |
| Spectral stream pool | ≤ 64 KB per stream (N 2048 frames, OLA, FFT scratch, warp delay line Float32) × `qr.specStreams` 32 / **16** per processor, allocated in `apply` (§6.10.1) |
| Processor extras (sxOut ×8, decimators, tails, MIDI OUT ring) | ≤ 512 KB at OS 4 |
| FX rack (per track) | instances per §8.5 (S2 Delay ≤ 4 MB at 192 kHz, Float32); rack total ≤ 32 MB (over ⇒ the new instance stays bypassed + notice); Convolve partition spectra count as assets: ≈ 8 bytes × IR length × channels, cap 10 s |
| Assets in the worklet (all tracks) | ≤ 192 MB / **128 MB** (main-thread manager frees unreferenced entries beyond `KEEP_BYTES` 64 MB / **32 MB**; superseded variants immediately, §9.5) |
| Playback entries | ≤ 48 kHz (§9.4.3); multisample zones with levels only up to their transposition (§9.4.3); `FACTORY_MULTI` ≤ 150 MB raw in total, ≤ 16 MB per instrument (§10.1) |
| Spectral analysis per sample | ≤ 16 MB (input resampled to ≤ 48 kHz, 20 s cap, §9.4.6) |
| Main thread asset LRU | ≤ 256 MB / **128 MB**; IndexedDB cache ≤ 512 MB / **256 MB** (`cacheTrim`) |
| Project asset meter (I + S1) | the sum of the project's referenced user + factory entries is shown on the GLOBAL page; an import or a factory selection that would exceed the device's worklet budget is refused with "Not enough memory for this sample on this device" |

### 14.3 Quality-reduction hooks (main.js CPU safeguard, session only, never saved)
The Phase-1 safeguard (load > 0.85 for 1–2 s ⇒ one step) gets these steps for Serum tracks, applied in order after the existing OS cap (the Phase-1 unison cap `sxUni` is replaced by `qr.uniMax`, which the DSP applies to all three oscillators): **Q1** `qr.grainsMax` 128, `qr.grainsProc` 256 and `qr.specHopMul` 2 · **Q2** `qr.uniMax` 4, `qr.grainsMax` 64, `qr.grainsProc` 128, `qr.specStreams` 8 · **Q3** `convMax` 2 s (Convolve tails truncated; the shaped-IR entry is rebuilt at the shorter length), `qr.specStreams` 4 · **Q4** POLY capped at 8 (voices above are released). Each step posts a notice naming it; choosing a Quality on the GLOBAL page or reloading resets the steps. Steps travel in the patch (`CP.qr`, `FXP.convMax`) and change no state.

---

## 15. Tests (node ≥ 20, `node --test 'tests/*.test.mjs'`)

Phase-1 rules (§10.1 Phase 1) apply: one file per owner, deterministic, < 60 s per file, allocation-free checks by replacing typed-array constructors in the vm context, spectral measurement method unchanged. **All Phase-1 tests keep passing**; where a Phase-1 test exercises an API whose v2 contract changed (e.g. `DESTS.length = 248`), the owner updates the assertion to the v2 value and adds the equivalent v1-prefix check. **Shared helpers** (owners in §0.2): every DSP test loads the classic scripts through `tests/dsp-scope.mjs` (D5: the §0.3 list in table order into one vm context, with per-script `null`/replacement overrides for the failure-isolation tests); browser-module tests use `tests/wk-dom.mjs` (U5) and the serum-ui stubs (U1, with an export-parity test against the real modules).

### 15.1 Golden back-compat (I; `tests/golden-harness.mjs` + four shards, with `tests/ref-p1/`; a **merge gate** from M1a on)
The harness loads (A) ref-p1 core + DSP + engine and (B) the v2 modules + the §0.3 list, each in its own vm context whose `Math.random` is replaced by `mulberry32(fnv1a32(<render id>))`, reseeded before every render (engine.js Solina `Divide`, §0.4). The **event script** (per render, 4 s): notes 48, 60, 67 chords, legato pairs, a held note with mod-wheel and bend moves, **and a song-transport segment** — song playback from tick 0 with a seek (play-with-seek to bar 2), a loop wrap, a stop and a restart — so that transport-anchored code (BPM and Hz LFOs, HOST, chaos re-seed, FX re-seed) is exercised. Shards (`node --test` runs each < 60 s by default; `GOLDEN_FULL=1` runs the full sweep, ≈ 10 min, before each milestone exit and in the release checklist):
1. **`golden-presets.test.mjs`** — Serum v1 factory presets: all `FACTORY_PRESETS_V1.length` (48) presets at OS 2 (full: OS 1, 2, 4): (A) vs (B) with the migrated state, `max |A − B| ≤ 1e-6` per sample per channel. Also: for every Phase-1 wavetable, noise and sub table id, `wt.makeEntry`/`wt.makeNoise` (and `SUB_TABLES` entries) of (A) and (B) are **bit-identical** (W changes `noiseLevels` and `SUB_TABLES`); `filterImpulse(t, …)` for every type 0–95 at 3 cutoff/res settings is bit-identical between (A) and (B) (FX Filter S1 exactness depends on D1's SFilter).
2. **`golden-random.test.mjs`** — 60 seeded random v1 states per run (random destinations, rows, LFO graphs, FX racks, voicing; `normalizeV1` of random JSON), rotating through OS 1/2/4 (seed = run index, so different runs cover different states; full: 200 states × 3 OS): `≤ 1e-6`.
3. **`golden-classic.test.mjs`** — every preset of the 12 classic models, same script, ref-p1 engine vs v2 engine (with default FX): **bit-identical**.
4. **`golden-fx.test.mjs`** — 100 seeded random v1 FX states on a classic model and on noise input: v1 rack (ref-p1) vs v2 rack with the migrated state: **bit-identical**.
5. (in shard 1) Migration is lossless: `normalizeSerum(migrate1(v1))` re-normalised is stable; every v1 row survives with its id (RENAME1 applied); `setS1(s, 0)` then `setS1(·, 1)` returns a state deep-equal to `s` and renders bit-identically; `setS1(s, 0)` of states without v1 warp entry 2 (Sync 1/2 Window) renders within −60 dB of the S1 render (approximation check, informative).
6. (in shard 1) Projects: a Phase-1 project file (fixture) loads, plays, saves as v2 and reloads identically.
7. Timing rows a–c and n of §14.1 live in the shards (medians of 5 interleaved runs, fail above 1.3×).

Tuning: 12-TET `.scl` → identity table (1e-12); a 7-limit just scale's degrees (cents within 1e-9); `.kbm` with unmapped keys (`x`) → `NaN` entries (§6.18); `.tun` exact tuning; malformed files → `{error}`.

Sample: pitch (root 60 at note 72 → ×2 rate ±0.05 cents), **intra-period warps** (§6.6.10: a sine sample recorded at its root through Sync, Bend +, PWM, Mirror, Remap, Quantize, Flip and Odd/Even matches the same warp on a 1-frame sine wavetable within −60 dB after alignment; FM/PD sidebands as on WT), `sRev`/`sNorm` equal a reversed/normalised entry, `k.lenrate` drives the region length (1 bar at 120 BPM = 2 s ± 1 frame, modulating RATE steps the ladder), wavetable-as-sample entry (root 18.232, whole-table loop, one cycle per frame at the root), loop modes timing (Fwd/Rev/Fwd-Rwd/Tailed positions after N periods), X-fade continuity (no step > 2× steady slope at the seam), Relative/Link loop, Exit on Release, start/end reversal, slices (key → slice, single slice destination), unison START/SPAN offsets, level selection alias ≤ −50 dBc for white noise played 2 octaves up at 48 kHz, missing entry → silence. Multisample: zone selection by key/velocity/TIMBRE, round robin order, sustain loop, override envelope, vel track law. Granular: spawn rate (count over 1 s ± 1 grain), jump start count, max grains cap, processor cap `grainsProc` (8 voices × 3 oscillators never exceed it; at the cap the most recent voice still spawns), per-stream START/SPAN playheads (stream u starts at `S + Δ_u` and advances by σ; Manual X|Y `pos·L + Δ_u`), slicing ignored while X|Y is on, per-grain randomisation ranges and determinism, window shapes (grain envelope = table), skew moves the peak, reverse grains, Loop Grains wrap, manual X|Y, density Grains mode overlap, spawn patterns offsets, allocation-free. Spectral: identity resynthesis of a 440 Hz sine (ρ = 1, σ = 1, no warps) within −40 dB error after the first hop (pre-roll fade-in over one hop); stream pool (streams taken/returned, `specCap` when empty, allocation only in `apply`); staggered IFFTs (8 streams started in the same quantum spread their frame syntheses over ≥ 4 different chunks of the hop); Vocode modulator at base rate; F/P-domain warps through the warp delay line (PD on a sine stream gives the WT PD sidebands within 1 dB; base delay D₀ = min(25 ms, 2·T₀); selecting the mode crossfades without a click); pitch shift +12 → 880 Hz peak; time stretch σ = 0.5 doubles duration with pitch unchanged (peak ±2 cents); filter curve and CUT shift; Lo/Hi limits (−60 dB outside, Smooth slope 24 dB/oct); each spectral warp's defining property (Comb notches, Gate silence below threshold, Shift moves the peak by Δ Hz ±1 bin, Mirror moves a peak to 2q − p, Pitch Shift ratio); streams cap; Phase Lock reduces magnitude modulation of a sustained harmonic tone (measured envelope ripple lower than without).

## Appendix C — Review log (round 2)

Every critic point, its resolution and where the spec changed. **A** = coverage critic, **B** = contract/back-compat/performance critic. "Accepted" = fixed as proposed; "Accepted (modified)" = fixed, with the stated difference from the proposal; "Partially rejected" = the rationale is given. Decisions needing the lead's approval are collected in §0.8.

### A — coverage

| # | Point | Resolution | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | MPE Expr X has no default source | Accepted (modified): `proc.mpe[ch·4+3]` = CC10, but stored already mapped to −1…1 with the centre at CC 64 (`cc ≤ 64 ? (cc−64)/64 : (cc−64)/63`), so 0/64/127 give exactly −1/0/+1 (the proposed `2·cc10 − 1` over CC/127 gives +0.008 at 64); `mpex = mpeBendX ? bend : x`; CC10 on channel 1 ignored; test added | §6.17, §12.4, §12.6, §15.9 |
| A2 | MPE preferences stored twice | Accepted: `ybi`/`bendX` removed from state (normalisation deletes them); app prefs `mpeBendX`/`mpeYBi` → `ctx` → `CP.mpeBendX`/`CP.srcBi`; `prefsChanged` recompiles; bounces use current prefs | §2.1, §2.2.2, §2.2.5, §3.10, §4.1, §12.6, §12.7, §13.10 |
| A3 | Harmonics formula wrong | Accepted: `h = semi ≥ 0 ? semi + 1 : 1/(1 − semi)`; `semiH` display; test values | §3.2, §6.3.3, §15.2, §15.9 |
| A4 | Swing range too narrow | Accepted: s ∈ [−1, 1], midpoint `2kd + d(1 + 0.75s)`, display `50 + 37.5s %`, LFO cycles ×(1 ± 0.75s), seq test 0.875/0.125 | §2.4.3, §4.4, §6.15.2, §7.2, §13.9, §15.4 |
| A5 | Deviation 4 drops warps on sample types, conflicts with ARCH2 | Accepted (extended): deviation 4 withdrawn. X types use the intra-period phase of the root period as proposed — except **Flip**, which needs only φ (`φ < w ? x : −x`, Phase-1 formula), not a half-period read; `T₀` is in source frames and independent of ρ (the proposal's "ρ-scaled" would make unison voices disagree on the period). Spectral gets FM/PD as proposed **and** the P-domain warps through the same per-stream delay line (otherwise "every S2 warp" would still not hold); the delay line's base delay is note-constant (≤ 25 ms) and active whenever an F/P mode is selected, so amount changes never jump — the one exception to §0.7 (sign-off item 1). FM's integral is leaky (5 Hz) so Exp FM cannot drift without bound | §0.6, §0.8, §3.4, §6.6.1, §6.6.4, §6.6.10, §6.9.3, §6.10.4, §6.10.7, §11.7, §14.1 (f′), §15.7, §15.8 |
| A6 | Header drag-export missing | Accepted (modified): `host.renderLast()`, 24-bit WAV `<preset> <note>.wav`, download + `smp:` record in a "Renders" folder listed by the sample menus, Alt-click opens Renders, Shift-click downloads the preset JSON, tail to −80 dBFS ≤ 10 s, test vs bounce. Difference: main.js records the last **input** group and renders it **through** the sequencer chain instead of capturing post-chain notes — the worklet would otherwise have to post every generated note (allocation on the audio thread); the offline render reproduces the arp/clip output exactly when the sequencer started idle (§7.2). Deviation 21 documents the browser drag | §0.6 (21), §12.6, §13.2, §13.2.5, §11.15, §15.13 |
| A7 | Clip export missing | Accepted: `clipToSmf(clip, bpm)` (SMF 0, 960 PPQ, tempo meta, velocities, muted skipped), "Copy to Track ▸" via `host.clipToTrack` (one history step), SMF byte test | §7.5.6, §11.3, §11.15, §13.8, §15.4 |
| A8 | Load MIDI Map from Presets missing | Accepted: pref `midiFromPreset` (default 1), `applyPreset(cur, preset, {midiFromPreset})`, GLOBAL row, core test | §11.1, §13.10, §15.2 |
| A9 | Lock behaviour | Accepted: `initPreset` (all locks incl. FX bus locks cleared, not via `applyPreset`), `locks.unp` + knob-menu Unlock/Lock Control + `applyPreset` re-applying preset values for `unp`, tuning lock keeps `a4` and `tun`. Per-parameter unlocks apply to synth modules only — S2 removed per-parameter FX locks (inv B14.1, 2.0.21) | §2.1, §2.2.2, §2.2.5, §2.5, §11.1, §13.3, §13.10, §13.12, §15.2 |
| A10 | Init Module must remove modulations | Accepted: `initModule(s, m)` in serum-core; rows *targeting* the module are deleted (rows *from* an LFO are not — that is "Init LFOs + LFO Mods"); test | §11.1, §11.14, §13.3, §15.2 |
| A11 | Filter/Noise module menus; copy pairs | Accepted: menus and filter label drag; allowed pairs stated (osc×3 with type and assets, filter↔filter2 with `f.type2`↔`f2.type`, env, lfo, self) plus the route-loop repair and S1-mode `f.type` rule | §11.1, §13.3, §15.2, §15.12 |
| A12 | Wavetables cannot be loaded as samples | Accepted: `osc[i].smp` takes `factory:`/`user:` ids; "Wavetables ▸" folder; entry `wtsmp:<table id>` at 48 kHz, root = 69 + 12·log2(48000/(2048·440)) ≈ 18.232 (the proposal's 18.22 was a rounding slip), whole-table loop, plus `spec:` analysis | §2.2.1, §2.2.5, §4.1, §9.1, §9.4.1, §9.4.8, §11.12, §13.3, §15.8 |
| A13 | Render OSC Warp scales the wrong value | Accepted: frame f = WARP 1 at absolute f/255, everything else at base; WARP 2 swept when WARP 1 is Off; S1: `renderWarp` with w = f/255; both Off ⇒ item disabled | §13.3.1, §15.7 |
| A14 | Length-to-BPM RATE controls nothing | Accepted (modified): `a/b/c.lenrate` appended at 524–526 (enum over `LEN_LADDER`, default 5/14), `lenIdx` migrated into `p`, SCAN binds to `k.lenrate` while Length to BPM is on, sign from `scanrev`, `scan` format plain %. Scope is `V` (continuous, like `k.scan`, which it replaces) rather than `V*`; with A23 the counts are ND 529 / NSV 429 / NS2 281 | §2.2.3, §3.2, §3.11.1, §3.11.2, §4.1 (`sLenOn`, `lenLad`), §6.7.2, §13.3, §15.2, §15.8 |
| A15 | Granular unison START/SPAN undefined | Accepted: per-stream playheads from `S + Δ_u`, shared σ; Manual `pos·L + Δ_u`; same for Spectral streams | §6.7.6, §6.9.1, §6.10.1, §15.8 |
| A16 | Manual X\|Y under-specified | Accepted: SCAN relabelled X and bound to `k.pos`; `sSlMode` compiled 0 while `xyOn`, slicing state kept, menu items disabled; Manual exists only for Granular/Spectral (the Sample-type sentence was removed) | §2.2.1, §4.1, §6.7.5, §6.7.7, §6.9.1, §6.10.1, §13.3 |
| A17 | Slicing details missing | Accepted: `slice.sync` with 200 ms rate limit and one undo step per update; Auto → Manual keeps the auto marks. Added: `marks` always holds the effective marks (the panel computes Auto marks) so `compileSerum` stays pure | §2.2.3, §6.7.5, §7.5.6, §13.3 |
| A18 | Knob-end phase memory still applies in S2 | Accepted (modified): knob-end rule and "Mem" text only in S1 mode; migration fills `phMem` (contig/voice) from the knob ends. The phase n is **not** reset — S1 mode still reads it and the checkbox must stay reversible (A19); in S2 mode the residual PHASE offset of a migrated Mem sound is ≤ 0.2° | §2.6.2, §2.6.4, §6.4, §6.21 (item 6) |
| A19 | S1 mode one-way; chaos unmapped | Accepted, option 2 (checkbox): `setS1` changes only the flag; paired fields pre-filled by migration and kept in sync by `syncS1`; matrix rows aliased at compile time (never rewritten); "Convert to Serum 2" removed. Chaos: **partially rejected** as an automatic step — re-targeting rows would make the checkbox irreversible, and chaos sources keep working exactly in both modes; `chaosToLfo` is offered as an explicit action, with chaos 1 → Rössler and chaos 2 → Lorenz (the Phase-1 attractor assignment; the proposal mapped both to Lorenz) (deviation 22) | §0.6 (22), §0.8, §2.2.2, §2.6.2, §2.6.4, §4.1 (`mAlias`), §11.1, §13.6, §13.10, §15.1, §15.2 |
| A20 | Poly legato has no mechanism | Partially rejected: the mechanism exists in Phase 1 (voice-side detection with a reference voice R, Phase-1 spec §6.1 and serum-dsp `noteOn`); having the allocator pass `legato = 1` would collide with the mono-legato meaning of that argument. Accepted parts: the spec now states the mechanism and extends the copied state to ENV 1–4 and LFO 1–10, with `envLegInv` restarting (sign-off item 11) | §6.2, §12.3, §15.6 |
| A21 | VC SCALING "rate" missing | Accepted: `vc.lmode`, `VC_RATES` snapping (log-nearest, ties to the smaller), `vcScale` display 25–400 % | §2.2.6, §3.2, §3.11.2, §4.1, §6.15.2, §6.16, §13.10 |

| A23 | Deviation 9 reason does not hold | Accepted: `g.bu2` (0…48, def 2) and `g.bd2` (−48…0, def −2) appended at 527/528, used outside S1 mode; migration copies the clamped v1 values; they are an S1 pair (§2.6.4); deviation 9 withdrawn | §0.6, §2.6.2, §2.6.4, §3.11.2, §6.3.2, §6.21 (item 7), §15.9 |
