### B — contract, back-compatibility, performance

| # | Point | Resolution | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| B1 | `randd` draws shift the Phase-1 voice PRNG | Accepted (modified): second stream `r2` for every Phase-2 per-note draw (incl. OSC C's unison/phase draws, which would otherwise follow A/B's on `r`); `randd` drawn only when a row uses it. Correction to the diagnosis: the Phase-1 voice stream is re-seeded at every non-legato note-on from the processor stream, so the damage is within a note-on (and on legato notes), not across notes. The salt is 0x85EBCA6B instead of 0x9E3779B9: the latter is Phase-1's per-voice multiplier and would make voice 0's `r2` replay the processor stream | §0.1, §0.4, §5.3, §6.2, §6.7.6, §6.8, §6.9.3, §6.10.5, §6.11.2, §6.15.3, §11.5, §15.6, §15.9 |
| B2 | LFO HOST rule and start phase change v1 sounds | Accepted: start = `pts[start].x` when PHASE is 0 (any mode; neutral term), Hz HOST only outside S1 mode (§6.21 item 5); song-transport segment (play, seek, loop, stop) added to the golden script | §4.1 (CT), §6.15.1, §6.15.2, §6.21, §15.1, §15.6 |
| B3 | "idx ≥ NS ⇒ FX" breaks | Accepted: `NV1`, `NF1`, `isFxId`, `vi`; the test is forbidden and the call sites are listed as mandatory M1a migrations; `CP.base` indexed only by `vi()`; the `sxUni` safeguard is retired for `qr.uniMax` | §0.5, §3.11.1, §11.1, §12.6, §14.3, §15.2, §15.13 |
| B4 | Neutral multiplicative terms not exactly neutral | Accepted: normative §0.7 with the complete list, gates (`use` bits or exact equality) and a bit-identity test per term | §0.1, §0.7, §6.14, §6.21, §15.6, §15.9 |
| B5 | Classic goldens and render farm not deterministic | Accepted: seeded `Math.random` in the golden vm contexts and the render-farm worker, reseeded per render; live unchanged | §0.4, §10.1, §15.1, §15.11 |
| B6 | M1 leaves the app broken for M2; cross-owner refactors | Accepted: M1a (serial hand-offs, the single D1+D2 joint extraction, F1 S1-only FXP v2, U1 panel/kit extraction; exit = golden + Phase-1 tests green; golden is a merge gate from then on) and M1b (skeletons) | §0.2, §0.5, §11.6, §11.14 |
| B7 | serum-core/fx-core split: move, cycle, two sources of truth | Accepted: leaf `laws.js` (C1); C1 moves the FX constants/functions once in M1a, then F1 owns fx-core; `FX_ROWS_V1` generates DESTS 148–247; `fxModded2(s, fx)` (v2) vs `fxModded(s)` (v1); `applyPresetFx(curFx, presetFx, s)` v2 specified | §0.2, §11.1, §11.1b, §11.2, §12.6, §15.3 |
| B8 | Convolve infeasible as written | Accepted: head FIR [0,128), 128-sample uniform partitions to 8192, 4096-partitions from 8192 with the work spread over 4096 frames; shaping/min-phase/partition spectra in `smpwork` (op `irshape`) delivered as `ir:<id>@<rate>~<shapeKey>`; shaping params `nomod` (deviation 20); 20 ms crossfade kept. Also: IR GAIN is applied live (not baked) so it stays modulatable | §0.6 (20), §3.16.2, §4.3, §4.5, §8.4, §9.1, §9.4.1, §11.11, §11.12, §13.5, §15.10 |
| B9 | Spectral memory/CPU unbounded | Accepted: processor-level stream pool allocated in `apply`; frame k synthesised during hop k−1 with per-stream chunk offsets; pre-roll spread over the first hop with fade-in; Vocode modulator decimated to base rate; analysis input ≤ 48 kHz (≤ 16 MB); 16 streams on touch devices | §4.1 (`qr`), §6.10.1, §6.10.5, §9.4.6, §11.8, §14.1 (h′), §14.2, §15.8 |
| B10 | No processor-wide grain cap | Accepted: `qr.grainsProc` 512 / 256 on touch; at the cap only the most recent granular voice keeps spawning (oldest thin out first); test and CPU row g″ | §4.1, §6.9.2, §14.1, §14.2, §14.3, §15.8 |
| B11 | Asset memory plan does not fit an iPad | Accepted: per-instrument 16 MB / total 150 MB with a recomputed table (mono when L ≡ R, sustain gates 1.5 s, roots step 12 outside 36–84, fewer velocity layers where the model ignores velocity); entries ≤ 48 kHz; zone levels by transposition; `norm`/`rev` as CP flags; superseded variants dropped at once; "slices" removed from the entry-changing list; preview by id; touch budgets and the asset meter | §2.2.3, §4.1, §4.5, §6.7.7, §9.1, §9.4.2, §9.4.3, §9.5, §9.6, §10.1, §14.2, §15.11 |
| B12 | FX and new-filter memory budgets cannot hold | Accepted: per-instance budgets (S2 Delay Float32 ≤ 4 MB at 192 kHz), 32 MB per track with bypass + notice; Diffusor/Comb 2 Float32 with a 20 Hz floor ≤ 1 MB per filter; ≤ 2 MB per voice for types 0–107 at 192 kHz; `setType` never allocates on the render path. S1-variant delays keep their Phase-1 lines (bit-exactness) | §6.13.1, §8.5, §14.2, §15.7, §15.10 |
| B13 | CT/seqT re-sent whole on every drag | Accepted: CT parts (`lfo0…9`, `path0…9`, `remap0…2`, `crv0…63`, `spec0…2`, `velo`, `note`) and seqT parts (`arp0…11`, `clip0…11`); host sends changed parts only; DSP merges; ≤ 12 KB per drag frame test (typical sizes: ≤ 64-point LFO, one clip) | §4.1, §4.4, §7.7, §11.10, §12.4, §12.6, §15.13 |
| B14 | MIDI OUT via main thread cannot meet timing | Accepted: `PROCS` registry in the shared worklet scope, `trackId` in `processorOptions`, `extNote(F + 128, …)` into a 256-event ring, identical offline; `simulate`, `seqout` and the bounce merge dropped; `bye` unregisters; no forwarding chains | §0.3, §0.4, §0.6 (2), §4.4, §7.9, §11.9, §12.2, §12.4, §12.6, §15.13 |
| B15 | Render-farm contract holes | Accepted: request with `wts`, `smps`, an event list and `frames`, one processor per request; pure `patchFor` exported by models.js; Serum sources are migrated presets | §10.1, §12.6, §12.7, §15.11 |
| B16 | Golden tests slow / blind spots | Accepted: four shards with a default subset and `GOLDEN_FULL=1`; `FACTORY_PRESETS_V1.length` (48); wavetable/noise/sub entry equality and `filterImpulse` 0–95 equality; timing as medians of 5 interleaved runs, fail above 1.3× | §0.2, §15.1 |
| B17 | CPU budgets gate nothing | Accepted (modified): one normative load per row, node fails above 1.5× **after machine calibration** against a ref-p1 scenario measured in the same run (an absolute 1.5× would be flaky across CI machines); `tests/bench.html` required at M2 end for rows d–m; iPad: every row ≤ 0.6 (hard ceiling), the per-row iPad column stays as the target | §0.5, §14.1 |
| B18 | Per-sample SxWarp calls | Accepted: `incBlock`/`phaseBlock`/`outBlock` per unison voice per chunk; callers read precomputed buffers; budget d verified with this structure | §6.6.1, §11.7, §11.8, §14.1, §15.7 |
| B19 | Interfaces between parallel owners undefined | Accepted: `gc`/`fr`, `sx.g` layout, taps, `sx.mod` fields (§11.6); the binding kit `makeKit` passed to sub-panels (§11.14); `assets.js` (S1) and `midi.js` (new role I2) split out of main.js | §0.2, §11.6, §11.12, §11.14, §12.6 |
| B20 | SxFilt2 cannot drive PZ SVF | Accepted: `setXY`, `setPZ` per chunk; FX Filter uses `PZ_DEFAULT` with X = Y = 0 | §6.13.1, §11.7, §15.7 |
| B21 | localStorage overflow; IndexedDB eviction | Accepted: `synth-app` IndexedDB via `appstore.js` (per-track autosave records, presets, banks), localStorage fallback with visible quota notices, one-time migration; `navigator.storage.persist()` at the first user-asset import | §0.2, §9.3, §12.6, §15.13 |
| B22 | Shared test helpers unowned | Accepted: `wk-dom.mjs` → U5; stubs + hooks → U1 with an export-parity test; `dsp-scope.mjs` → D5; `fxp*`/`fxt*` fixtures → F1 | §0.2, §15 (intro), §15.5, §15.12 |
| B23 | Small engine/sequencer contracts | Accepted: `cmd('latch', 0 or 1)` with pedal semantics (§7.4.5 no longer "toggles"); processor field `sxSeq`; `phases()[i]` in `fxLayout` order, NaN without LFO; fallback reads FX-ctx [0]/[1] | §7.4.5, §7.7, §8.1, §12.2, §12.3, §12.5 |
| B24 | Load order too strict and slow | Accepted: scripts 1–11 via `Promise.allSettled`, engine last, table order kept for `wk.loadDSP`; fx-dsp S1 variants independent of SxBase; `SxBase.shape` (D5) shared by F1 and D3 with a bit-exactness test against the Phase-1 shapers | §0.3, §11.5, §11.11, §12.1, §15.5, §15.10 |
| B25 | M-scope rows get voice indices | Accepted: `vi()` = −1 for scope M; FX-ctx 2/3/4 only; `CP.base` slots stay 0 | §3.11.1, §4.1, §15.2 |
| B26 | Static vs dynamic FX ids ambiguous | Accepted: with an FX state every `fx.*` id resolves through `fxDest`; static rows only for v1 normalisation/migration/tests; `compileSerum` never uses `DEST_INDEX` for FX rows | §3.11.1 |
| B27 | Warm-up has no time budget | Accepted: heavy engines warm up lazily through `warmTick` (≤ 1 ms per quantum); constructor warm-up ≤ 1.5 × Phase 1; tests | §11.5, §11.10, §14.1 (q), §15.9 |
| B28 | Factory preset keys can collide | Accepted: C1 test — v2 names disjoint from Phase-1 names, Phase-1 names resolve to the migrated presets | §15.2 |
| B29 | Import suffixes must stay uniform | Accepted: shared `?v=N` on every relative import incl. the new modules, bumped by I; lint test | §0.4, §15.13 |
